FILE: README.md

# SBDD HPC Cluster Orchestrator (Nextflow DSL2 + Slurm)

Масштабируемый, модульный вычислительный пайплайн для молекулярного моделирования и дизайна лекарственных соединений (Structure-Based Drug Design, SBDD) на базе HPC-кластера под управлением Slurm и оркестрации Nextflow DSL2.

---

## 1. Архитектура и Структура Проекта

```
sbdd_pipeline_new/
├── assets/                     # Статические шаблоны, параметры и базы данных
│   ├── mdp/                    # Шаблоны параметров GROMACS (min, nvt, npt, md)
│   └── ppm/                    # Данные топологии мембран для PPM 3.0
├── bin/                        # Атомарные утилиты и научные скрипты (CLI)
│   ├── amber_fix_pdb.py        # Коррекция PDB под требования силовых полей Amber
│   ├── declash_system.py       # Устранение ван-дер-ваальсовых клэшей в мембране/растворе
│   ├── extract_md_files.py     # Подготовка 3D-лиганда (RDKit) и запуск antechamber
│   ├── md_metrics.py           # Расчет RMSD, RMSF и контактов через MDAnalysis
│   ├── merge_complex.py        # Сборка финального комплекса и топологии
│   ├── parse_immers.py         # Парсинг результатов PPM 3.0 в структурированный JSON
│   ├── split_sdf.py            # Чанкование библиотек лигандов с сохранением $$$$
│   └── summarize_results.py    # Агрегация оценок (GNINA CNN, Glide, MM-GBSA) в ranking.csv
├── cli/                        # Пользовательский терминальный интерфейс sbdd
│   ├── commands/               # Команды: init, prepare, screen, dynamics, run, status
│   └── core/                   # Валидация YAML, построитель аргументов Nextflow
├── conf/                       # Конфигурационные файлы Nextflow
│   ├── base.config             # Ресурсные метки (process_single, process_gpu, etc.)
│   ├── slurm.config            # Параметры очередей, партиций и хуков Slurm
│   └── containers.config       # Привязка образов Apptainer (GNINA, GROMACS, Immers)
├── modules/                    # Атомарные DSL2-процессы Nextflow
│   ├── analysis/               # MM-GBSA (Prime/gmx_MMPBSA) и MD-метрики
│   ├── common/                 # Staging входных файлов и очистка scratch
│   ├── docking/                # GNINA (GPU) и Glide (CPU)
│   ├── md/                     # Чанковая динамика GROMACS (CHUNK_1..6, trjcat, trjconv)
│   ├── preparation/            # PrepWizard, PPM 3.0, LigPrep, Antechamber, TLEAP, Packmol
│   └── reporting/              # Построение ranking.csv и сводных отчетов
├── subworkflows/               # Композитные функциональные блоки
│   ├── prepare_receptor.nf     # Подготовка рецептора + мембрана
│   ├── prepare_ligands.nf      # Подготовка библиотеки лигандов
│   ├── dock_library.nf         # Распределенный параллельный докинг
│   ├── setup_md_system.nf      # Построение системы Amber/GROMACS
│   ├── run_chunked_md.nf       # Чанковая MD (N независимых реплик)
│   └── analyze_md_traj.nf      # Траекторный анализ и термодинамика
├── workflows/                  # Точки входа по подзадачам
│   ├── prepare.nf              # `sbdd prepare`
│   ├── screen.nf               # `sbdd screen`
│   ├── dynamics.nf             # `sbdd dynamics`
│   └── sbdd.nf                 # `sbdd run` (сквозной расчет)
├── tests/                      # 3-уровневая система автоматического тестирования
│   ├── unit/                   # Tier 1: Модульные тесты Python (pytest)
│   ├── test_workflows_stub.sh  # Tier 2: Валидация графов процессов Nextflow (-stub)
│   └── cluster_smoke/          # Tier 3: Сквозной smoke-тест на Slurm GPU
├── main.nf                     # Главный диспетчер запуска
├── nextflow.config             # Корневая конфигурация проекта
└── pyproject.toml              # Метаданные пакета CLI sbdd
```

---

## 2. Что сделано (ключевые отличия от старого пайплайна)

1. **Полная смена парадигмы на HPC Slurm**:
   - Никаких локальных монолитных bash-скриптов и ручного управления процессами.
   - Все расчеты оркестрируются через **Nextflow DSL2** и выполняются через Slurm-очереди (`main`, `urgent`).
   - Распараллеливание докинга и реплик молекулярной динамики по GPU-узлам кластера.
2. **Чистая модульная архитектура без костылей**:
   - Schrödinger, GNINA, PPM 3.0 и AmberTools/GROMACS полностью изолированы друг от друга.
   - Каждый инструмент выполняется в своем окружении/контейнере Apptainer. Системные пути не пробрасываются в чужие контейнеры.
3. **Надежное хранение и автоматическая очистка**:
   - Все тяжелые промежуточные файлы хранятся на быстром NFS-диске `/shared/work/<project_name>`.
   - Финальные артефакты (структуры, траектории, таблицы) публикуются в чистовые директории проекта (`docking/`, `md/`, `reports/`).
   - По завершении расчетной сессии scratch-директория очищается автоматически.
4. **Запуск из любой точки (`-resume`)**:
   - Благодаря расчету контрольных сумм в Nextflow, сбой любого узла или добавление новых соединений не требует перезапуска с нуля — расчет продолжается ровно с места остановки.
5. **Атомарные конфигурационные файлы**:
   - Настройки разделены на независимые файлы: `gnina.yaml`, `glide.yaml`, `gromacs.yaml`, `project.yaml`.
6. **Единый CLI-инструмент `sbdd`**:
   - Управление проектом через удобный интерфейс в терминале.

---

## 3. Сценарии работы и команды запуска

### Сценарий A: Инициализация нового проекта
Создает структуру каталогов проекта и генерирует чистые шаблоны параметров:
```bash
sbdd init --name kinase_target
cd kinase_target
```
В каталоге появятся:
- `project.yaml` — базовые пути (рецептор, лиганд, мембрана: true/false).
- `configs/gnina.yaml` — параметры GNINA (exhaustiveness, cnn_scoring, batch_size).
- `configs/glide.yaml` — параметры Glide (precision, ligand_charges).
- `configs/gromacs.yaml` — параметры MD (temperature, n_replicates, chunk_ns).

### Сценарий B: Подготовка белка и лигандов (`prepare`)
Выполняет подготовку структуры (PrepWizard или PDBFixer), расчет положения в мембране (PPM 3.0) и подготовку лигандов:
```bash
sbdd prepare
```

### Сценарий C: Виртуальный скрининг (`screen`)
Разбивает библиотеку молекул на чанки и распределяет задачи докинга по GPU/CPU воркерам Slurm:
```bash
# Скрининг с помощью GNINA (GPU)
sbdd screen --engine gnina

# Либо с помощью Glide (CPU)
sbdd screen --engine glide
```
Результаты автоматически склеиваются в `docking/<project>_docked_all.sdf`, а скоры агрегируются в `reports/ranking.csv`.

### Сценарий D: Чанковая молекулярная динамика (`dynamics`)
Проводит сборку системы в явном растворителе/мембране, параметризацию в GAFF2, деклэш, равновесие и $N$ независимых реплик чанковой MD (например, 6 чанков по 10 нс = 60 нс на реплику):
```bash
sbdd dynamics --replicates 3
```
Каждый чанк генерирует чекпоинт `.cpt`, позволяя безопасно переживать лимиты времени Slurm и вытеснение задач.

### Сценарий E: Сквозной конвейер (`run`)
Выполняет полный цикл от исходных файлов до пост-анализа и итогового рейтинга:
```bash
sbdd run
```

---

## 4. Автоматизированное тестирование

Для быстрой проверки пайплайна без тяжелых вычислений реализована 3-уровневая система тестов:

### Уровень 1: Модульные тесты Python (Tier 1)
Проверяет математику, парсеры, форматирование GRO/TOP и RDKit:
```bash
/home/user/miniforge3/envs/sbdd_cluster_env/bin/pytest tests/unit
```
*Время выполнения: ~5 секунд. Результат: 9/9 тестов passed.*

### Уровень 2: Валидация DAG Nextflow (Tier 2)
Проверяет целостность графа потоков данных всех 4 подпайплайнов через `-stub`:
```bash
bash tests/test_workflows_stub.sh
```
*Время выполнения: ~1.5 минуты. Результат: 4/4 DAGs passed (все 50 процессов).*

### Уровень 3: Интеграционный Smoke-тест на Slurm (Tier 3)
Реальный запуск микро-расчета через Slurm на GPU-воркере с использованием Apptainer:
```bash
bash tests/cluster_smoke/run_smoke_test.sh
```
*Время выполнения: ~35 секунд. Результат: реальный расчет на GPU-ноде, публикация `ranking.csv` и автоочистка scratch.*

---

## 5. Точки роста и перспективы развития

1. **Интерактивный HTML-отчет (MultiQC / Streamlit)**:
   - Встроить автоматическую генерацию HTML-отчета с интерактивными графиками RMSD/RMSF, графиками кластеризации и 3D-просмотром связывания лиганда (NGL Viewer / Mol*).
2. **Подключение новых генеративных и AI-моделей**:
   - Благодаря Nextflow DSL2 добавление нового докинг-движка (DiffDock, Uni-Mol, AutoDock Vina-GPU) требует написания ровно одного модуля в `modules/docking/` без изменения остального пайплайна.
3. **Автоматический переход от скрининга к MD (Active Learning / Auto-Top)**:
   - Добавить в `main.nf` автоматический отбор лучших топ-$K$ лигандов из `ranking.csv` и прямую передачу их в подпайплайн `dynamics.nf` без ручного вмешательства человека.
4. **Оптимизация упаковки мембраны**:
   - Интеграция MemProtMD / insane.py в качестве быстрой альтернативы Packmol-Memgen для стандартных липидных бислоев.


======================================================================
FILE: assets/configs/glide.yaml
======================================================================
glide:
  enabled: false
  precision: "SP"                    # HTVS, SP, XP
  poses_per_lig: 1
  sample_rings: true
  epik_penalties: true
  forcefield: "OPLS4"
  postdock: true
  write_csv: true
  grid:
    inner_box: [10, 10, 10]
    outer_box: [26, 26, 26]
    forcefield: "OPLS4"
  ligprep:
    target_ph: 7.4
    ph_tolerance: 2.0
    force_field: 16                  # 16 = OPLS4, 14 = OPLS_2005
    max_states: 32


======================================================================
FILE: assets/configs/gnina.yaml
======================================================================
gnina:
  enabled: true
  exhaustiveness: 16
  cnn_scoring: "rescore"             # none, rescore, refinement, all
  num_modes: 3                       # Number of binding poses to output
  autobox_add: 4.0                   # Padding around autobox reference ligand (Å)
  cnn_model: "crossdock_default2018" # Default GNINA CNN model
  batch_size: 15                     # Molecules per Slurm GPU task


======================================================================
FILE: assets/configs/md.yaml
======================================================================
md_simulation:
  enabled: true
  system_build:
    water_model: "opc"               # opc, tip3p
    box_padding: 15.0
    neutralize: true
    salt_conc: 0.15
  equilibration:
    nvt_short_ps: 50
    nvt_long_ps: 500
    npt_berendsen_ps: 1000
    npt_pr_ps: 1000
  production:
    temperature: 310.15              # Kelvin
    total_time_ns: 60                # Total simulation duration per replicate
    chunk_size_ns: 10                # Duration of each Slurm chunk
    replicates: 3                    # Independent replicates with different initial seeds
  selection:
    mode: "top_n"                    # "top_n" or "manual"
    top_n: 3                         # Automatically pick top N candidates from ranking
    selected_hits: []                # Or explicit titles: ["native", "PPZ_1H_Ph_BnMe"]


======================================================================
FILE: assets/configs/membrane.yaml
======================================================================
membrane:
  enabled: true                      # Set false for soluble proteins in water
  type: "MOM"                        # PPM 3.0 membrane type (e.g. MOM, plasma)
  topology: "in"                     # N-terminus topology (in/out)
  chains: ["A"]                      # Chains participating in transmembrane bundle
  lipids:
    upper_leaflet:
      composition: "POPC:POPE:CHL1"
      ratio: "5:4:1"
    lower_leaflet:
      composition: "POPC:POPE:POPS:CHL1"
      ratio: "4:4:1:1"
  padding:
    lipid_padding: 10.0              # Distance from protein to membrane edge (Å)
    box_padding: 15.0                # Water layer above/below membrane (Å)
  ions:
    salt_conc: 0.15                  # Molar salt concentration (KCl)


======================================================================
FILE: assets/configs/mmpbsa.yaml
======================================================================
mmpbsa:
  enabled: true
  cores: 16                          # CPU cores for MPI / OpenMP
  start_ns: 20.0                     # Sample after RMSD equilibration plateau (ns)
  step_frames: 40                    # Stride interval for trajectory frame sampling
  per_residue_decomp: true           # Enable per-residue binding energy decomposition
  temperature: 310.15
  salt_conc: 0.15


======================================================================
FILE: assets/configs/project.yaml
======================================================================
project:
  name: "SBDD_Project"
  mode: "screening"                  # Options: screening, redocking, single
  cluster:
    queue: "main"                    # Options: main (default, 7 days), urgent (2 hours)
    urgent_quick_tasks: true         # Automatically route fast single tasks to urgent


======================================================================
FILE: assets/configs/receptor.yaml
======================================================================
receptor:
  input_file: "2MGY.cif"             # Structure file in project folder (.cif or .pdb)
  model_index: 0                     # 0 = all models/ensemble, >0 = specific model index
  native_ligand_resname: "PKA"       # 3-letter residue name of native reference ligand
  keep_cofactors: []                 # List of cofactor residue names to retain (e.g. ["HEM", "MG"])
  prep_wizard:
    cap_termini: true                # Add ACE and NME caps to termini
    target_ph: 7.4                   # pH for Epik and PROPKA
    ph_tolerance: 1.0
    minimize_rmsd: 0.3               # Max RMSD for heavy atom minimization


======================================================================
FILE: assets/configs/screening.yaml
======================================================================
screening:
  library_file: "pyrrolo_pyrazine_library.sdf" # Library file in project root
  funnel:
    top_frames_per_ligand: 3         # Retain top K ensemble frames per ligand
    library_percentile_cutoff: 0.50  # Top 50% advance to MM-GBSA
    benchmark_ligands: ["PPZ_1H_Ph_BnMe", "PPZ_1H_Ph_BuMe", "PPZ_1Br_Ph_BnMe"]

FILE: bin/align_ligand.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
bin/align_ligand.py
Separated Schrödinger worker:
Superimposes CA atoms of raw protein to PPM-oriented protein,
applies the transformation matrix to native ligand, and saves aligned ligand (MAE & SDF).
"""
import argparse
import sys
import logging
from pathlib import Path
from schrodinger import structure
from schrodinger.structutils import analyze, rmsd, transform

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s: %(message)s", stream=sys.stdout)

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Align native ligand to PPM-oriented receptor frame")
    parser.add_argument("--raw_protein", required=True, help="Input un-oriented protein PDB")
    parser.add_argument("--oriented_protein", required=True, help="Input PPM-oriented protein PDB")
    parser.add_argument("--raw_ligand", required=True, help="Input raw native ligand MAE")
    parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="Output aligned native ligand MAE")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    raw_lig_path = Path(args.raw_ligand)
    sdf_out = str(Path(args.output).with_suffix(".sdf"))

    if not raw_lig_path.exists() or raw_lig_path.stat().st_size == 0:
        logging.warning("No valid raw ligand file found. Writing empty placeholders.")
        Path(args.output).touch()
        Path(sdf_out).touch()
        sys.exit(0)

    try:
        raw_prot = structure.StructureReader.read(args.raw_protein, index=1)
        orient_prot = structure.StructureReader.read(args.oriented_protein, index=1)
        lig_st = structure.StructureReader.read(str(raw_lig_path), index=1)
    except Exception as e:
        logging.error(f"Failed to read input structures for alignment: {e}")
        sys.exit(1)

    calpha_asl = "atom.ptype ' CA '"
    at_list_orig = analyze.evaluate_asl(raw_prot, calpha_asl)
    at_list_orient = analyze.evaluate_asl(orient_prot, calpha_asl)

    if len(at_list_orig) == len(at_list_orient) and len(at_list_orig) >= 3:
        matrix = rmsd.get_super_transformation_matrix(orient_prot, at_list_orient, raw_prot, at_list_orig)
        transform.transform_structure(lig_st, matrix)
        lig_st.write(args.output)
        lig_st.write(sdf_out)
        logging.info(f"Successfully aligned native ligand -> {args.output} and {sdf_out}")
    else:
        logging.warning("CA atom mismatch during alignment; writing untransformed ligand.")
        lig_st.write(args.output)
        lig_st.write(sdf_out)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

======================================================================
FILE: bin/amber_fix_pdb.py
======================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
File: src/utils/amber_prep_parmed.py
Description: Robust preparation of Schrodinger PDBs for AMBER using ParmEd.
Handles NMA->NME, ACE hydrogens, HIS protonation, and CYS->CYX mapping.
"""
import sys
import numpy as np
import parmed as pmd
from loguru import logger

def fix_for_amber(in_pdb: str, out_pdb: str):
    logger.info(f"Loading {in_pdb} via ParmEd...")
    struct = pmd.load_file(in_pdb)

    # 1. Identify Disulfide Bridges (SG-SG distance < 2.5 A)
    cyx_res = set()
    cys_residues = [r for r in struct.residues if r.name in ["CYS", "CYX"]]
    for i, res1 in enumerate(cys_residues):
        sg1 = next((a for a in res1.atoms if a.name == "SG"), None)
        if not sg1: continue
        for res2 in cys_residues[i+1:]:
            sg2 = next((a for a in res2.atoms if a.name == "SG"), None)
            if not sg2: continue
            dist = np.linalg.norm(np.array([sg1.xx, sg1.xy, sg1.xz]) - np.array([sg2.xx, sg2.xy, sg2.xz]))
            if dist < 2.5:
                cyx_res.add(res1)
                cyx_res.add(res2)

    # 2. Rename residues and atoms
    for res in struct.residues:
        # Standard amino acid backbone amide hydrogen fix (H1 / HN / 1H -> H)
        if res.name not in ["ACE", "NME", "NMA"]:
            for a in res.atoms:
                if a.name in ["H1", "HN", "1H"]:
                    a.name = "H"

        # C-Terminal Cap (NMA / NME -> NME: atoms N, H, CH3, H1, H2, H3)
        if res.name in ["NMA", "NME"]:
            res.name = "NME"
            for a in res.atoms:
                if a.name in ["CA", "CH3"]: a.name = "C"
                elif a.name in ["1HA", "1H", "H1", "HH31"]: a.name = "H1"
                elif a.name in ["2HA", "2H", "H2", "HH32"]: a.name = "H2"
                elif a.name in ["3HA", "3H", "H3", "HH33"]: a.name = "H3"
        
        # N-Terminal Cap (ACE: atoms C, O, CH3, H1, H2, H3)
        elif res.name == "ACE":
            for a in res.atoms:
                if a.name in ["1H", "H1", "HH31"]: a.name = "H1"
                elif a.name in ["2H", "H2", "HH32"]: a.name = "H2"
                elif a.name in ["3H", "H3", "HH33"]: a.name = "H3"
        
        # Protonated Aspartate (ASP with HD2 -> ASH)
        elif res.name == "ASP":
            if any(a.name in ["HD2", "2HD"] for a in res.atoms):
                res.name = "ASH"

        # Protonated Glutamate (GLU with HE2 -> GLH)
        elif res.name == "GLU":
            if any(a.name in ["HE2", "2HE"] for a in res.atoms):
                res.name = "GLH"

        # Neutral Lysine (LYS without HZ3 -> LYN)
        elif res.name == "LYS":
            if not any(a.name in ["HZ3", "3HZ", "HZ"] for a in res.atoms):
                res.name = "LYN"

        # Histidine Protonation States (HIP, HID, HIE)
        elif res.name in ["HIS", "HSD", "HSE", "HSP"]:
            names = [a.name for a in res.atoms]
            if "HD1" in names and "HE2" in names: res.name = "HIP"
            elif "HD1" in names: res.name = "HID"
            elif "HE2" in names: res.name = "HIE"
            else: res.name = "HIE"
        
        # Cysteine Disulfides
        elif res in cyx_res:
            res.name = "CYX"
            hg_atom = next((a for a in res.atoms if a.name == "HG"), None)
            if hg_atom:
                struct.atoms.remove(hg_atom)
                res.atoms.remove(hg_atom)

    logger.info(f"Saving AMBER-compliant PDB to {out_pdb}...")
    struct.save(out_pdb, overwrite=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    fix_for_amber(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2])

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
File: bin/apply_membrane.py
Description: Injects implicit membrane slab properties for Schrödinger Prime MM-GBSA into MAE structure.
"""
import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from schrodinger import structure

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Inject Implicit Membrane Properties")
    parser.add_argument("--input", required=True, help="Input prepared receptor MAE")
    parser.add_argument("--info", required=True, help="Input membrane thickness text or json file")
    parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="Output receptor MAE with implicit membrane")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    try:
        st = structure.StructureReader.read(args.input)
        thickness = 28.0
        
        info_path = Path(args.info)
        if info_path.exists():
            content = info_path.read_text().strip()
            if content.startswith("{"):
                import json
                thickness = float(json.loads(content).get("thickness", 28.0))
            else:
                thickness = float(content)
            print(f"Applying membrane thickness: {thickness:.2f} Å", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"Warning: info file not found, defaulting thickness to {thickness:.2f} Å", flush=True)

        half_t = thickness / 2.0
        st.property['r_psp_Memb1_x'] = 0.0
        st.property['r_psp_Memb1_y'] = 0.0
        st.property['r_psp_Memb1_z'] = -half_t

        st.property['r_psp_Memb2_x'] = 0.0
        st.property['r_psp_Memb2_y'] = 0.0
        st.property['r_psp_Memb2_z'] = half_t

        st.property['r_psp_Prime_Membrane_Thickness'] = thickness
        st.property['r_psp_Prime_Membrane_Buffer'] = 2.5

        st.write(args.output)
        print(f"Saved receptor with implicit membrane to {Path(args.output).name}", flush=True)
        
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error applying implicit membrane: {e}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        sys.exit(1)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


======================================================================
FILE: bin/build_funnel_whitelist.py
======================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
bin/build_funnel_whitelist.py
Aggregates Glide and GNINA Rescore data to triage candidates using consensus (Physics + AI),
matching the exact logic from the original SBDD pipeline.
Outputs funnel_whitelist.csv with [Title, Frame].
"""
import argparse
import csv
import sys
from pathlib import Path
import pandas as pd


def parse_glide_csv(csv_path: Path) -> dict:
    data = {}
    if not csv_path.exists():
        return data
    with open(csv_path, "r", errors="ignore") as f:
        reader = csv.DictReader(f)
        for row in reader:
            title = row.get("title") or row.get("Title") or row.get("s_m_title")
            if not title or title in data:
                continue

            score = None
            for k in ["r_i_docking_score", "docking_score"]:
                if k in row and row[k]:
                    try:
                        score = float(row[k])
                        break
                    except ValueError:
                        pass
            if score is None:
                for k in ["r_i_glide_gscore", "gscore"]:
                    if k in row and row[k]:
                        try:
                            score = float(row[k])
                            break
                        except ValueError:
                            pass
            if score is not None:
                data[title] = {"GlideScore": score}
    return data


def parse_gnina_sdf(sdf_path: Path, prefix: str = "") -> dict:
    import re
    data = {}
    if not sdf_path or not sdf_path.exists():
        return data
    with open(sdf_path, "r", errors="ignore") as f:
        content = f.read()
    for entry in content.split("$$$$"):
        lines = entry.strip().splitlines()
        if not lines:
            continue

        raw_title = lines[0].strip()
        prop_title = None
        props = {}

        for i, line in enumerate(lines):
            line_str = line.strip()
            if line_str.startswith("> <") and line_str.endswith(">"):
                tag_clean = line_str[3:-1].strip().lower()
                if i + 1 < len(lines):
                    val_str = lines[i + 1].strip()
                    if tag_clean in ["_name", "s_m_title", "title", "compound_id"]:
                        prop_title = val_str
                    else:
                        try:
                            val = float(val_str)
                            if tag_clean == "cnnscore":
                                props[f"{prefix}CNNscore"] = val
                            elif tag_clean == "cnnaffinity":
                                props[f"{prefix}CNNaffinity"] = val
                            elif tag_clean in ["minimizedaffinity", "affinity"]:
                                props[f"{prefix}VinaAff"] = val
                        except ValueError:
                            pass

        title = prop_title or raw_title
        if not title:
            continue

        title_clean = re.sub(r"(_model_\d+|_dock_\d+|_\d+)$", "", title).strip()

        if props:
            if title not in data:
                data[title] = props
            if title_clean and title_clean not in data:
                data[title_clean] = props
    return data


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Build screening funnel whitelist")
    parser.add_argument("--glide_csvs", nargs="+", default=[], help="Glide docking CSV files")
    parser.add_argument("--gnina_sdfs", nargs="+", default=[], help="GNINA rescore SDF files")
    parser.add_argument("--top_k_frames", type=int, default=3, help="Max frames per ligand")
    parser.add_argument("--percentile_cutoff", type=float, default=0.50, help="Fraction of top ligands (e.g. 0.50)")
    parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="Output whitelist CSV path")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    # Map frame id to files
    import re
    glide_by_frame = {}
    for f in args.glide_csvs:
        p = Path(f)
        # expected format: ..._f<frame>... or ..._f<frame>.csv
        stem = p.stem
        parts = stem.split("_f")
        frame_id = parts[-1].split("_")[0] if len(parts) > 1 else "1"
        m = re.search(r"_f(\d+)", p.stem)
        frame_id = m.group(1) if m else "1"
        glide_by_frame[frame_id] = p

    gnina_by_frame = {}
    for f in args.gnina_sdfs:
        p = Path(f)
        parts = p.stem.split("_f")
        frame_id = parts[-1].split("_")[0] if len(parts) > 1 else "1"
        m = re.search(r"_f(\d+)", p.stem)
        frame_id = m.group(1) if m else "1"
        gnina_by_frame[frame_id] = p

    all_data = []
    for frame_id, g_csv in glide_by_frame.items():
        glide_data = parse_glide_csv(g_csv)
        resc_sdf = gnina_by_frame.get(frame_id)
        resc_data = parse_gnina_sdf(resc_sdf, prefix="Resc_") if resc_sdf else {}

        for title, scores in glide_data.items():
            entry = {
                "Title": title,
                "Frame": frame_id,
                "GlideScore": scores["GlideScore"],
                "Resc_CNNaffinity": resc_data.get(title, {}).get("Resc_CNNaffinity", None)
            }
            all_data.append(entry)

    df = pd.DataFrame(all_data)
    out_csv = Path(args.output)
    if df.empty:
        print("Warning: No docking data available to build funnel whitelist. Outputting empty whitelist.")
        pd.DataFrame(columns=["Title", "Frame"]).to_csv(out_csv, index=False)
        sys.exit(0)

    # 1. Percentile Ranks (Glide lower is better, CNN higher is better)
    df["PR_Glide"] = df["GlideScore"].rank(ascending=False, pct=True)
    if df["Resc_CNNaffinity"].notna().any():
        df["PR_CNN"] = df["Resc_CNNaffinity"].rank(ascending=True, pct=True).fillna(df["PR_Glide"])
        df["Triage_Score"] = (df["PR_Glide"] + df["PR_CNN"]) / 2.0
    else:
        df["Triage_Score"] = df["PR_Glide"]

    # 2. Cutoff top percentile (e.g. top 50%)
    best_triage = df.groupby("Title")["Triage_Score"].max()
    threshold = best_triage.quantile(1.0 - args.percentile_cutoff)
    kept_ligands = set(best_triage[best_triage >= threshold].index)

    df_filtered = df[df["Title"].isin(kept_ligands)]

    # 3. Top K frames per ligand
    whitelist = df_filtered.sort_values("Triage_Score", ascending=False).groupby("Title").head(args.top_k_frames)

    whitelist[["Title", "Frame"]].to_csv(out_csv, index=False)
    print(f"Screening Funnel: Retained {len(kept_ligands)}/{len(best_triage)} ligands across {len(whitelist)} tasks -> {out_csv}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

======================================================================
FILE: bin/calc_rmsd.py
======================================================================
"""
Worker script to calculate symmetry-aware heavy-atom RMSD between reference and docked poses.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import logging
from pathlib import Path
from schrodinger import structure
from schrodinger.structutils import rmsd

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s: %(message)s")

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Calculate Conformer RMSD")
    parser.add_argument("--reference", required=True, help="Path to reference ligand MAE")
    parser.add_argument("--poses", required=True, help="Path to Glide PV or Ligand MAE")
    parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="Path to output CSV")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    ref_st = structure.StructureReader.read(args.reference)
    
    results = []
    
    with structure.StructureReader(args.poses) as reader:
        for i, st in enumerate(reader):
            # Skip the receptor
            if i == 0 and st.atom_total > 500:
                continue
                
            try:
                conf_eval = rmsd.ConformerRmsd(ref_st, st, asl_expr='NOT atom.element H', in_place=True)
                conf_eval.use_symmetry = True
                rmsd_val = conf_eval.calculate()
                
                gscore = st.property.get('r_i_glide_gscore', '')
                dscore = st.property.get('r_i_glide_docking_score', '')
                title = st.title
                
                # Extract GNINA energy and CNN properties if present in SDF
                gnina_affinity, cnn_score, cnn_affinity = "", "", ""
                for k, v in st.property.items():
                    k_low = k.lower()
                    if "minimizedaffinity" in k_low or (k_low.endswith("affinity") and "cnn" not in k_low):
                        gnina_affinity = v
                    elif "cnnscore" in k_low:
                        cnn_score = v
                    elif "cnnaffinity" in k_low:
                        cnn_affinity = v
                
                # Universal primary score (GlideScore or GNINA affinity)
                primary_score = gscore or gnina_affinity or dscore or ""

                results.append({
                    "Title": title,
                    "Pose_Index": i,
                    "Score": primary_score,
                    "GlideScore": gscore,
                    "DockingScore": dscore,
                    "CNN_Score": cnn_score,
                    "CNN_Affinity": cnn_affinity,
                    "RMSD_Heavy": round(rmsd_val, 3)
                })
            except Exception as e:
                logging.error(f"Error processing pose {i}: {e}")

    keys = ["Title", "Pose_Index", "Score", "GlideScore", "DockingScore", "CNN_Score", "CNN_Affinity", "RMSD_Heavy"]

    with open(args.output, 'w', newline='') as csvfile:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(csvfile, fieldnames=keys)
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(results)
        
    logging.info(f"Successfully calculated RMSD for {len(results)} poses. Saved to {args.output}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

======================================================================
FILE: bin/core/config_parser.py
======================================================================
"""
Parses and validates the YAML configuration file.
"""
import yaml
from pathlib import Path
from loguru import logger

def load_config(config_path: Path) -> dict:
    """
    Loads YAML configuration and returns a dictionary.
    """
    if not config_path.exists():
        logger.critical(f"Configuration file not found: {config_path}")
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Missing {config_path}")

    with open(config_path, "r") as f:
        try:
            config = yaml.safe_load(f)
            logger.debug(f"Successfully loaded configuration from {config_path.name}")
            return config
        except yaml.YAMLError as e:
            logger.critical(f"Invalid YAML format in {config_path.name}: {e}")
            raise

======================================================================
FILE: bin/core/logger.py
======================================================================
"""
Configures the Loguru logger for both console output (colorized) and file output.
"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from loguru import logger
from rich.console import Console

console = Console()

def setup_logger(log_file: Path = None, verbose: bool = False):
    """
    Initializes logger settings.
    """
    logger.remove()  # Remove default handler
    
    # Console handler
    log_level = "DEBUG" if verbose else "INFO"
    logger.add(
        lambda msg: console.print(msg, end=""),
        level=log_level,
        format="<green>{time:YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss}</green> | <level>{level: <8}</level> | <level>{message}</level>"
    )
    
    # File handler (if working directory is initialized)
    if log_file:
        logger.add(
            str(log_file),
            level="DEBUG",
            format="{time:YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss} | {level: <8} | {name}:{function}:{line} - {message}",
            rotation="10 MB"
        )

======================================================================
FILE: bin/core/parsers.py
======================================================================
"""
File: src/core/parsers.py
Description: Modular strategy-based parsers for external CLI tool logs.
Standardizes progress updates for Rich UI and isolates tool-specific regex.
"""
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Optional, Any

@dataclass
class ProgressUpdate:
    completed: Optional[int] = None
    total: Optional[int] = None
    description: Optional[str] = None

class BaseLogParser:
    def parse(self, text: str) -> Optional[ProgressUpdate]:
        raise NotImplementedError

class PackmolParser(BaseLogParser):
    def __init__(self):
        self.progress_re = re.compile(r"(\d+)/(\d+)\s*\[")
        self.pct_re = re.compile(r"(\d+)%")

    def parse(self, text: str) -> Optional[ProgressUpdate]:
        lines = [line.strip() for line in text.replace("\r", "\n").split("\n") if line.strip()]
        for line in reversed(lines[-30:]):
            m = self.progress_re.search(line)
            if m:
                comp, tot = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2))
                pct_m = self.pct_re.search(line)
                pct = pct_m.group(1) if pct_m else str(int(comp / tot * 100))
                stage = line.split(":")[0].strip() if ":" in line else "Packing"
                return ProgressUpdate(
                    completed=comp,
                    total=tot,
                    description=f"[bold magenta]Packmol: {stage} ({pct}%)[/bold magenta]"
                )
        return None

class GninaParser(BaseLogParser):
    def __init__(self):
        self.header_re = re.compile(r"mode\s+\|\s+affinity", re.IGNORECASE)

    def parse(self, text: str) -> Optional[ProgressUpdate]:
        matches = len(self.header_re.findall(text))
        if matches > 0:
            return ProgressUpdate(completed=matches)
        return None

class DeclashParser(BaseLogParser):
    def __init__(self):
        # Matches iteration and remaining clashes: "iter  12: clashes=   45 applied= ..."
        self.iter_re = re.compile(r"iter\s+(\d+):\s+clashes=\s*(\d+)", re.IGNORECASE)

    def parse(self, text: str) -> Optional[ProgressUpdate]:
        lines = [l.strip() for l in text.splitlines() if l.strip()]
        for line in reversed(lines[-20:]):
            m = self.iter_re.search(line)
            if m:
                it = int(m.group(1)) + 1
                clashes = int(m.group(2))
                return ProgressUpdate(
                    completed=it,
                    total=300,
                    description=f"[bold cyan]Declash: Step {it}/300 | Clashes left: {clashes}[/bold cyan]"
                )
        return None

class GlideParser(BaseLogParser):
    def __init__(self):
        self.lig_re = re.compile(r"LIG\s+(\d+)")

    def parse(self, text: str) -> Optional[ProgressUpdate]:
        lines = [l.strip() for l in text.splitlines() if l.strip()]
        for line in reversed(lines[-20:]):
            m = self.lig_re.search(line)
            if m:
                return ProgressUpdate(completed=int(m.group(1)))
        return None

class PrimeParser(BaseLogParser):
    def __init__(self):
        # Matches JobDJ table rows: "  8  8  0 | finished ..."
        self.jobdj_re = re.compile(r"^\s*(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+(\d+)\s+\|\s+(finished|launched)", re.MULTILINE)

    def parse(self, text: str) -> Optional[ProgressUpdate]:
        lines = [l for l in text.splitlines() if "|" in l]
        for line in reversed(lines[-20:]):
            m = self.jobdj_re.search(line)
            if m:
                c, a, w = int(m.group(1)), int(m.group(2)), int(m.group(3))
                tot = c + a + w
                if tot > 0:
                    pct = int((c / tot) * 100)
                    return ProgressUpdate(
                        completed=c,
                        total=tot,
                        description=f"[yellow]Prime MM-GBSA Subjobs: {c}/{tot} ({pct}%)[/yellow]"
                    )
        return None

class GromacsParser(BaseLogParser):
    def __init__(self):
        # Matches inline steps: "step 220000", "Step= 500", "Writing checkpoint, step 220000"
        self.inline_re = re.compile(r"(?:step\s*[:=]?\s*|writing checkpoint,\s+step\s+)(\d+)", re.IGNORECASE)
        # Matches GROMACS energy table header: "Step           Time"
        self.header_re = re.compile(r"^\s*Step\s+Time\s*$", re.IGNORECASE)
        # Matches step and time values: "220000      440.00000"
        self.step_val_re = re.compile(r"^\s*(\d+)\s+[\d\.]+\s*$")

    def parse(self, text: str) -> Optional[ProgressUpdate]:
        lines = [l.strip() for l in text.splitlines() if l.strip()]
        for idx in range(len(lines) - 1, -1, -1):
            line = lines[idx]

            # 1. Parse two-line step block from .log files
            m_val = self.step_val_re.match(line)
            if m_val and idx > 0 and self.header_re.match(lines[idx - 1]):
                return ProgressUpdate(completed=int(m_val.group(1)))

            # 2. Parse minimization live metrics: "Step= 420, Dmax= ..., Epot= -9.8e+05 Fmax= 8.4e+02"
            if "fmax=" in line.lower() and "step=" in line.lower():
                try:
                    parts = line.split(",")
                    step_val = int(parts[0].split("=")[1].strip())
                    epot_val = parts[2].split("=")[1].split()[0].strip()
                    fmax_val = float(parts[2].split("Fmax=")[1].split()[0].strip())
                    return ProgressUpdate(
                        completed=step_val,
                        description=f"[bold green]EM: Step {step_val} | Fmax: {fmax_val:.1f} -> 500 | Epot: {epot_val}[/bold green]"
                    )
                except Exception:
                    pass

            # 3. Parse inline MD progress
            m_inline = self.inline_re.search(line)
            if m_inline:
                return ProgressUpdate(completed=int(m_inline.group(1)))

        return None

class MmpbsaParser(BaseLogParser):
    def __init__(self):
        self.tqdm_re = re.compile(r"(\d+)%\|.*?\|\s*(\d+)/(\d+)")
        self.stage_re = re.compile(r"calculating\s+(complex|receptor|ligand)\s+contribution", re.IGNORECASE)
        self.model_re = re.compile(r"Beginning\s+(GB|PB)\s+calculations", re.IGNORECASE)
        self.current_model = "PB"
        self.substage_name = "Complex"
        self.substage_idx = 1

    def parse(self, text: str) -> Optional[ProgressUpdate]:
        # Scan entire buffer forward so we never lose the state
        for line in text.splitlines():
            m_mod = self.model_re.search(line)
            if m_mod:
                self.current_model = m_mod.group(1).upper()
                
            m_stg = self.stage_re.search(line)
            if m_stg:
                name = m_stg.group(1).capitalize()
                self.substage_name = name
                self.substage_idx = 1 if name == "Complex" else (2 if name == "Receptor" else 3)

        # Parse latest frame progress from the tail
        lines = [l.strip() for l in text.splitlines() if l.strip()]
        for line in reversed(lines[-20:]):
            m = self.tqdm_re.search(line)
            if m:
                pct = int(m.group(1))
                c = int(m.group(2))
                tot = int(m.group(3))
                return ProgressUpdate(
                    completed=c,
                    total=tot,
                    description=f"[bold green]MMPBSA-{self.current_model} [{self.substage_name} {self.substage_idx}/3]: {c}/{tot} ({pct}%)[/bold green]"
                )
        return None

def get_parser(cmd: list) -> Optional[BaseLogParser]:
    """Factory function to auto-detect the parser based on command context."""
    cmd_str = " ".join(str(x) for x in cmd)
    cmd_name = cmd[0].split("/")[-1]

    if "gmx_MMPBSA" in cmd_name or "gmx_MMPBSA" in cmd_str:
        return MmpbsaParser()
    elif cmd_name == "packmol-memgen":
        return PackmolParser()
    elif cmd_name == "gnina":
        return GninaParser()
    elif "declash" in cmd_str:
        return DeclashParser()
    elif cmd_name.startswith("gmx"):
        return GromacsParser()
    elif cmd_name in ["glide", "ligprep"] or any(str(x).endswith(".in") for x in cmd):
        return GlideParser()
    elif "prime" in cmd_name or "prime_mmgbsa" in cmd_str:
        return PrimeParser()
    return None

======================================================================
FILE: bin/core/state_manager.py
======================================================================
"""
Validates dependencies and checkpoint files to ensure modules run in correct order.
"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from loguru import logger

class StateManager:
    @staticmethod
    def require_file(filepath: Path, module_name: str) -> None:
        """
        Validates existence of a required file. Exits gracefully if missing.
        """
        if not filepath.exists():
            logger.critical(f"Missing required dependency: {filepath}")
            logger.error(f"Cannot execute '{module_name}'. Please run prerequisites first.")
            sys.exit(1)

    @staticmethod
    def check_output_exists(filepath: Path, force: bool = False) -> bool:
        """
        Checks if output already exists to handle checkpoints / overwrites.
        """
        if filepath.exists():
            if force:
                logger.warning(f"Forcing overwrite of existing output: {filepath}")
                return False
            else:
                logger.info(f"Checkpoint found: {filepath.name} exists. Skipping step. Use --force to override.")
                return True
        return False

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
bin/count_models.py
Counts structures/NMR models in CIF, PDB, MAE, or SDF.
Outputs integer count to stdout and models_count.txt.
"""
import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def count_cif_models(file_path: Path) -> int:
    models = 0
    with open(file_path, "r", errors="ignore") as f:
        for line in f:
            if line.startswith("_pdbx_nmr_ensemble.conformers_submitted_total_number"):
                parts = line.split()
                if len(parts) >= 2:
                    try:
                        return int(parts[1])
                    except ValueError:
                        pass
            if line.startswith("_pdbx_nmr_ensemble.conformers_calculated_total_number"):
                parts = line.split()
                if len(parts) >= 2:
                    try:
                        return int(parts[1])
                    except ValueError:
                        pass
            if line.strip().startswith("_atom_site.pdbx_PDB_model_num"):
                pass
            if line.strip().startswith("data_") and not line.strip().startswith("data_general"):
                # some CIF files repeat data blocks
                pass
    # If not found via tags, scan model numbers or structure reader
    # Schrodinger fallback if available
    try:
        from schrodinger.structure import count_structures
        return count_structures(str(file_path))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return 1

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Count structures or NMR models in macromolecular file")
    parser.add_argument("--input", required=True, help="Input macromolecule file")
    parser.add_argument("--output", default="models_count.txt", help="Output text file")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    p = Path(args.input)
    count = 1

    try:
        from schrodinger.structure import count_structures
        count = count_structures(str(p.resolve()))
    except Exception:
        if p.suffix.lower() in [".cif", ".mmcif"]:
            count = count_cif_models(p)
        elif p.suffix.lower() == ".pdb":
            # count MODEL records in PDB
            models = 0
            with open(p, "r", errors="ignore") as f:
                for line in f:
                    if line.startswith("MODEL"):
                        models += 1
            count = max(1, models)

    print(count)
    with open(args.output, "w") as out:
        out.write(f"{count}\n")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

======================================================================
FILE: bin/declash_gmx.py
======================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
declash_gmx_molecules_v2.py

Rigid-body de-clashing for GROMACS systems that crash with "infinite force" or
LINCS warnings at step 0 due to catastrophic INTER-molecular overlaps.

Why v2
------
Compared to a residue-based de-clasher, this script moves WHOLE MOLECULES as
defined by the topology [ molecules ] section. This is critical for Amber/Lipid
multi-residue lipids (PC+PA+OL etc.), where constrained/bonded atoms can span
multiple residues. Moving residues independently can stretch those bonds and
cause LINCS failures with enormous energies.

Compared to v1, v2 uses a *sequential* (Gauss–Seidel-like) resolution of the
worst overlaps each iteration. This reduces the "vector cancellation" effect
where many small clashes overwhelm a few catastrophic ones.

Recommended workflow
--------------------
1) De-clash coordinates:
   python declash_gmx_molecules_v2.py -f step3_input.gro -p topol.top -o step3_declashed.gro \
       --fix-moltype system1 --fix-moltype UNK --cutoff 0.10 --niter 120 --max-move 0.10 \
       --report declash_report.txt -v
       
       python3 declash_gmx_molecules_v2.py -f step3_input.gro -p topol.top -o step3_declashed.gro \
       --fix-moltype system1 --fix-moltype UNK --no-heavy-only --cutoff 0.10 --niter 300 --max-move 0.05 \
       --report declash_report.txt -v

2) First EM "unbrick" with *constraints disabled*:
   constraints = none

3) Then re-enable constraints / position restraints and proceed normally.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import math
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

import numpy as np


def read_text_with_includes(path: Path, max_depth: int = 20) -> str:
    """Recursively expand #include lines if the include file exists locally."""
    seen: set[Path] = set()

    def _read(p: Path, depth: int) -> str:
        if depth > max_depth:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Exceeded include depth {max_depth} while reading {p}")
        p = p.resolve()
        if p in seen:
            return ""
        seen.add(p)
        base = p.parent
        out_lines: List[str] = []
        for line in p.read_text(errors="ignore").splitlines():
            m = re.match(r'^\s*#include\s+["<]([^">]+)[">]\s*$', line)
            if not m:
                out_lines.append(line)
                continue
            inc = m.group(1)
            cand = base / inc
            if cand.exists():
                out_lines.append(_read(cand, depth + 1))
            else:
                out_lines.append(line)
        return "\n".join(out_lines)

    return _read(path, 0)


def read_gro(path: Path) -> Tuple[str, np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Return (title, coords[n,3], resid[n], resname[n], atomname[n], box[3])."""
    lines = path.read_text(errors="ignore").splitlines()
    if len(lines) < 3:
        raise ValueError(f"{path} does not look like a .gro file.")
    title = lines[0].rstrip("\n")
    nat = int(lines[1].strip())
    if len(lines) < 3 + nat:
        raise ValueError(f"{path}: declared {nat} atoms, but file is truncated.")
    coords = np.zeros((nat, 3), dtype=np.float64)
    resid = np.zeros(nat, dtype=np.int32)
    resname = np.empty(nat, dtype=object)
    atomname = np.empty(nat, dtype=object)

    for i in range(nat):
        l = lines[2 + i]
        resid[i] = int(l[0:5])
        resname[i] = l[5:10].strip()
        atomname[i] = l[10:15].strip()
        coords[i, 0] = float(l[20:28])
        coords[i, 1] = float(l[28:36])
        coords[i, 2] = float(l[36:44])

    box = np.array(list(map(float, lines[2 + nat].split()[:3])), dtype=np.float64)
    return title, coords, resid, resname, atomname, box


def write_gro(path: Path, title: str, coords: np.ndarray, resid: np.ndarray, resname: np.ndarray, atomname: np.ndarray, box: np.ndarray):
    nat = coords.shape[0]
    with path.open("w") as f:
        f.write(f"{title}\n")
        f.write(f"{nat:5d}\n")
        for i in range(nat):
            f.write(f"{resid[i]:5d}{str(resname[i])[:5]:<5}{str(atomname[i])[:5]:>5}{(i+1)%100000:5d}"
                    f"{coords[i,0]:8.3f}{coords[i,1]:8.3f}{coords[i,2]:8.3f}\n")
        f.write(f"{box[0]:10.5f}{box[1]:10.5f}{box[2]:10.5f}\n")


def parse_moleculetype_atomcounts(top_text: str) -> Dict[str, int]:
    lines = top_text.splitlines()
    mt_idx = [i for i, l in enumerate(lines) if re.match(r"^\s*\[\s*moleculetype\s*\]\s*$", l, re.I)]
    out: Dict[str, int] = {}
    for idx in mt_idx:
        j = idx + 1
        while j < len(lines) and (lines[j].strip() == "" or lines[j].lstrip().startswith(";")):
            j += 1
        if j >= len(lines):
            continue
        name = lines[j].split()[0]

        k = j + 1
        atoms_start = None
        while k < len(lines):
            if re.match(r"^\s*\[\s*moleculetype\s*\]\s*$", lines[k], re.I):
                break
            if re.match(r"^\s*\[\s*system\s*\]\s*$", lines[k], re.I):
                break
            if re.match(r"^\s*\[\s*atoms\s*\]\s*$", lines[k], re.I):
                atoms_start = k + 1
                break
            k += 1
        if atoms_start is None:
            continue

        n = 0
        k = atoms_start
        while k < len(lines):
            if re.match(r"^\s*\[", lines[k]):
                break
            s = lines[k].strip()
            if s and (not s.startswith(";")) and (not s.startswith("#")):
                n += 1
            k += 1
        out[name] = n
    return out


def parse_molecules(top_text: str) -> List[Tuple[str, int]]:
    lines = top_text.splitlines()
    idx = None
    for i, l in enumerate(lines):
        if re.match(r"^\s*\[\s*molecules\s*\]\s*$", l, re.I):
            idx = i
            break
    if idx is None:
        raise ValueError("Topology does not contain a [ molecules ] section.")
    out: List[Tuple[str, int]] = []
    i = idx + 1
    while i < len(lines):
        l = lines[i]
        if re.match(r"^\s*\[", l):
            break
        s = l.strip()
        if s and (not s.startswith(";")) and (not s.startswith("#")):
            parts = s.split()
            if len(parts) >= 2:
                out.append((parts[0], int(float(parts[1]))))
        i += 1
    if not out:
        raise ValueError("[ molecules ] section is empty.")
    return out


@dataclass
class MolRange:
    moltype: str
    start: int
    end: int


def build_molecule_ranges(natoms: int, atomcounts: Dict[str, int], mol_list: List[Tuple[str, int]]) -> List[MolRange]:
    ranges: List[MolRange] = []
    idx = 0
    for mt, count in mol_list:
        if mt not in atomcounts:
            raise ValueError(f"Cannot find [ moleculetype ] {mt} in topology.")
        n = atomcounts[mt]
        for _ in range(count):
            ranges.append(MolRange(mt, idx, idx + n))
            idx += n
    if idx != natoms:
        raise ValueError(f"Topology expands to {idx} atoms, but .gro has {natoms}.")
    return ranges


def wrap(coords: np.ndarray, box: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    return coords - np.floor(coords / box) * box


def min_image(d: np.ndarray, box: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    return d - box * np.round(d / box)


def is_heavy(atomname: str) -> bool:
    a = atomname.strip().upper()
    if a == "EPW":
        return False
    return not (a.startswith("H") or a.startswith("D"))


def find_clashes(coords: np.ndarray, box: np.ndarray, atom_molid: np.ndarray, atomname: np.ndarray,
                 cutoff: float, max_pairs: int, heavy_only: bool) -> List[Tuple[int, int, np.ndarray, float]]:
    L = box.astype(np.float64)
    coords = coords.astype(np.float64)

    if heavy_only:
        mask = np.array([is_heavy(a) for a in atomname], dtype=bool)
    else:
        mask = np.array([a.strip().upper() != "EPW" for a in atomname], dtype=bool)

    idxs = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
    pos = wrap(coords[idxs], L)
    mol = atom_molid[idxs]

    nx = max(int(L[0] / cutoff), 1)
    ny = max(int(L[1] / cutoff), 1)
    nz = max(int(L[2] / cutoff), 1)
    sx, sy, sz = L[0] / nx, L[1] / ny, L[2] / nz

    cx = (pos[:, 0] / sx).astype(np.int32) % nx
    cy = (pos[:, 1] / sy).astype(np.int32) % ny
    cz = (pos[:, 2] / sz).astype(np.int32) % nz
    cell_id = (cx * ny + cy) * nz + cz

    order = np.argsort(cell_id)
    cell_id = cell_id[order]
    pos = pos[order]
    mol = mol[order]
    idxs = idxs[order]

    unique, start = np.unique(cell_id, return_index=True)
    end = np.r_[start[1:], len(cell_id)]
    slices = {int(u): (int(s), int(e)) for u, s, e in zip(unique, start, end)}
    neigh = [(dx, dy, dz) for dx in (-1, 0, 1) for dy in (-1, 0, 1) for dz in (-1, 0, 1)]
    c2 = cutoff * cutoff

    out: List[Tuple[int, int, np.ndarray, float]] = []
    for u, st, en in zip(unique, start, end):
        u = int(u); st = int(st); en = int(en)
        cz_u = u % nz
        tmp = u // nz
        cy_u = tmp % ny
        cx_u = tmp // ny
        for dx, dy, dz in neigh:
            u_n = (((cx_u + dx) % nx) * ny + ((cy_u + dy) % ny)) * nz + ((cz_u + dz) % nz)
            if u_n not in slices:
                continue
            st2, en2 = slices[u_n]
            if u_n < u:
                continue
            for ii in range(st, en):
                jj0 = st2 if u_n != u else ii + 1
                mi = int(mol[ii])
                for jj in range(jj0, en2):
                    mj = int(mol[jj])
                    if mi == mj:
                        continue
                    d = pos[jj] - pos[ii]
                    d = min_image(d, L)
                    r2 = float(d.dot(d))
                    if r2 < c2:
                        r = math.sqrt(r2) if r2 > 1e-16 else 0.0
                        uvec = d / r if r > 1e-6 else np.array([1.0, 0.0, 0.0], dtype=np.float64)
                        out.append((int(idxs[ii]), int(idxs[jj]), uvec, float(r)))
                        if len(out) >= max_pairs:
                            return out
    return out


def declash_sequential(coords: np.ndarray, box: np.ndarray, mol_ranges: List[MolRange],
                       atom_molid: np.ndarray, atomname: np.ndarray,
                       mobile_mol: np.ndarray, restrict_xy_mol: np.ndarray,
                       cutoff: float, niter: int, damping: float, max_move: float,
                       max_pairs: int, heavy_only: bool, seed: int, verbose: bool):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed if seed >= 0 else None)
    L = box.astype(np.float64)
    coords = wrap(coords.astype(np.float64), L)
    nmol = len(mol_ranges)
    mol_slices = [slice(m.start, m.end) for m in mol_ranges]

    for it in range(niter):
        clashes = find_clashes(coords, L, atom_molid, atomname, cutoff, max_pairs=max_pairs, heavy_only=heavy_only)
        if not clashes:
            if verbose:
                print(f"iter {it:3d}: clashes=0")
            return coords, {"cutoff_nm": float(cutoff), "iterations": float(it), "final_clashes": 0.0}

        clashes.sort(key=lambda x: x[3])
        worst_r = clashes[0][3]
        moved = np.zeros((nmol, 3), dtype=np.float64)
        applied = 0

        def cap(mid: int, d: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
            if not mobile_mol[mid]:
                return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float64)
            trial = moved[mid] + d
            if np.linalg.norm(trial) <= max_move:
                return d
            rem = max_move - float(np.linalg.norm(moved[mid]))
            if rem <= 0:
                return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float64)
            dn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
            if dn < 1e-12:
                return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float64)
            return d * (rem / dn)

        for ai, aj, uvec, r in clashes:
            mi = int(atom_molid[ai]); mj = int(atom_molid[aj])
            if (not mobile_mol[mi]) and (not mobile_mol[mj]):
                continue
            if r < 1e-6:
                uvec = rng.normal(size=3); uvec /= (np.linalg.norm(uvec) + 1e-12)

            push = (cutoff - r) * damping
            if push <= 0:
                continue

            if mobile_mol[mi] and mobile_mol[mj]:
                di = -0.5 * push * uvec
                dj = +0.5 * push * uvec
            elif mobile_mol[mi]:
                di = -push * uvec
                dj = np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                di = np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float64)
                dj = +push * uvec

            di = cap(mi, di); dj = cap(mj, dj)
            if restrict_xy_mol[mi]:
                di[2] = 0.0
            if restrict_xy_mol[mj]:
                dj[2] = 0.0

            if np.any(di):
                coords[mol_slices[mi]] += di; moved[mi] += di; applied += 1
            if np.any(dj):
                coords[mol_slices[mj]] += dj; moved[mj] += dj; applied += 1

        coords = wrap(coords, L)

        if verbose:
            print(f"iter {it:3d}: clashes={len(clashes):6d} applied={applied:6d} worst={worst_r:8.4f} nm")

        if applied == 0:
            # escape local trap
            jitter = rng.normal(size=(nmol, 3)) * 0.02
            jitter[~mobile_mol] = 0.0
            jitter[restrict_xy_mol, 2] = 0.0
            for mid in range(nmol):
                if mobile_mol[mid]:
                    coords[mol_slices[mid]] += jitter[mid]
            coords = wrap(coords, L)

    final = find_clashes(coords, L, atom_molid, atomname, cutoff, max_pairs=500000, heavy_only=heavy_only)
    return coords, {"cutoff_nm": float(cutoff), "iterations": float(niter), "final_clashes": float(len(final))}


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("-f", "--gro", required=True, type=Path)
    ap.add_argument("-p", "--top", required=True, type=Path)
    ap.add_argument("-o", "--out", required=True, type=Path)
    ap.add_argument("--cutoff", type=float, default=0.10)
    ap.add_argument("--niter", type=int, default=120)
    ap.add_argument("--damping", type=float, default=0.8)
    ap.add_argument("--max-move", type=float, default=0.10)
    ap.add_argument("--max-pairs", type=int, default=5000)
    ap.add_argument("--scale", type=float, default=1.0)
    ap.add_argument("--no-heavy-only", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=1)
    ap.add_argument("--fix-moltype", action="append", default=[])
    ap.add_argument("--fix-first-n", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--restrict-xy-moltype", action="append", default=[])
    ap.add_argument("--report", type=Path, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("-v", "--verbose", action="store_true")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    title, coords, resid, resname, atomname, box = read_gro(args.gro)

    if args.scale and abs(args.scale - 1.0) > 1e-8:
        sc = float(args.scale)
        center = box / 2.0
        coords = wrap(coords, box)
        coords = (coords - center) * sc + center
        box = box * sc
        coords = wrap(coords, box)

    top_text = read_text_with_includes(args.top)
    atomcounts = parse_moleculetype_atomcounts(top_text)
    mol_list = parse_molecules(top_text)
    mol_ranges = build_molecule_ranges(coords.shape[0], atomcounts, mol_list)

    atom_molid = np.empty(coords.shape[0], dtype=np.int32)
    moltype_of_molid: List[str] = []
    for mid, m in enumerate(mol_ranges):
        atom_molid[m.start:m.end] = mid
        moltype_of_molid.append(m.moltype)
    moltype_of_molid = np.array(moltype_of_molid, dtype=object)

    mobile = np.ones(len(mol_ranges), dtype=bool)
    if args.fix_first_n > 0:
        mobile[:args.fix_first_n] = False
    for mt in args.fix_moltype:
        mobile[moltype_of_molid == mt] = False

    restrict_xy = np.zeros(len(mol_ranges), dtype=bool)
    for mt in args.restrict_xy_moltype:
        restrict_xy[moltype_of_molid == mt] = True

    heavy_only = not args.no_heavy_only

    new_coords, stats = declash_sequential(coords, box, mol_ranges, atom_molid, atomname,
                                          mobile, restrict_xy, args.cutoff, args.niter,
                                          args.damping, args.max_move, args.max_pairs,
                                          heavy_only, args.seed, args.verbose)

    write_gro(args.out, title + " (declashed)", new_coords, resid, resname, atomname, box)

    if args.report is not None:
        pairs = find_clashes(new_coords, box, atom_molid, atomname, args.cutoff, max_pairs=500000, heavy_only=True)
        pairs = [(ai, aj, r) for ai, aj, _, r in pairs]
        pairs.sort(key=lambda x: x[2])
        with args.report.open("w") as f:
            f.write(f"declash stats: {stats}\n")
            f.write(f"worst inter-molecular heavy-atom contacts < {args.cutoff} nm:\n")
            for ai, aj, r in pairs[:50]:
                f.write(f"{r:10.5f} nm  atom {ai+1:8d}  atom {aj+1:8d}\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

======================================================================
FILE: bin/extract_complex.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
bin/extract_complex.py
Separated Schrödinger worker:
Reads a macromolecular complex (.cif or .pdb), extracts protein PDB and native ligand MAE & SDF.
"""
import argparse
import sys
import logging
from pathlib import Path
from schrodinger import structure
from schrodinger.structutils import analyze

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s: %(message)s", stream=sys.stdout)

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Extract protein PDB and native ligand from complex")
    parser.add_argument("--input", required=True, help="Input complex file (.cif or .pdb)")
    parser.add_argument("--frame_index", type=int, default=1, help="NMR model index (1-based)")
    parser.add_argument("--ligand", required=True, help="Native ligand residue name (e.g. PKA)")
    parser.add_argument("--cofactors", nargs="*", default=[], help="Cofactors to retain with protein")
    parser.add_argument("--out_protein", required=True, help="Output protein PDB path")
    parser.add_argument("--out_ligand", required=True, help="Output native ligand MAE path")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    input_file = Path(args.input)
    idx = args.frame_index
    logging.info(f"Reading model {idx} from {input_file}...")

    try:
        st = structure.StructureReader.read(str(input_file), index=idx)
    except Exception as e:
        logging.error(f"Failed to read model index {idx} from {input_file}: {e}")
        sys.exit(1)

    # 1. Extract protein & cofactors
    cofactors_asl = " OR ".join([f"res.ptype '{c}'" for c in args.cofactors])
    keep_asl = f"(protein) OR ({cofactors_asl})" if cofactors_asl else "(protein)"
    rec_atoms = analyze.evaluate_asl(st, keep_asl)
    if not rec_atoms:
        logging.error(f"No protein atoms found for model {idx} in {input_file}!")
        sys.exit(1)

    rec_st = st.extract(rec_atoms)
    rec_st.write(args.out_protein)
    logging.info(f"Wrote raw protein to {args.out_protein} ({len(rec_atoms)} atoms)")

    # 2. Extract native ligand (MAE and SDF)
    lig_asl = f"res.ptype '{args.ligand}'"
    lig_atoms = analyze.evaluate_asl(st, lig_asl)
    sdf_out = str(Path(args.out_ligand).with_suffix(".sdf"))
    if not lig_atoms:
        logging.warning(f"Native ligand '{args.ligand}' not found in model {idx}.")
        Path(args.out_ligand).touch()
        Path(sdf_out).touch()
    else:
        lig_st = st.extract(lig_atoms)
        lig_st.write(args.out_ligand)
        lig_st.write(sdf_out)
        logging.info(f"Wrote native ligand to {args.out_ligand} and {sdf_out} ({len(lig_atoms)} atoms)")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: bin/extract_md_files.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
File: bin/extract_md_files.py
Description: Extracts receptor PDB and ligand MOL2 for MD setup.
Computes formal charge for Antechamber. Supports both Schrödinger API and RDKit fallback.
"""
import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def extract_via_schrodinger(lig_file, lig_title, rec_file, out_dir):
    from schrodinger import structure
    out_dir = Path(out_dir)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    # 1. Receptor PDB
    rec_st = structure.StructureReader.read(rec_file)
    rec_out = out_dir / "receptor.pdb"
    structure.StructureWriter.write(rec_st, str(rec_out))

    # 2. Ligand
    charge = 0
    lig_found = False
    all_ligs = []
    
    with structure.StructureReader(lig_file) as reader:
        for i, st in enumerate(reader):
            if i == 0 and (st.property.get("b_glide_receptor", 0) == 1 or st.atom_total > 500):
                continue
            all_ligs.append(st)
            title = (st.property.get("s_m_title") or st.title or "").strip()
            if not lig_title or title == lig_title.strip() or title.lower() == lig_title.strip().lower():
                lig_mol2 = out_dir / "ligand.mol2"
                structure.StructureWriter.write(st, str(lig_mol2))
                charge = st.formal_charge
                lig_found = True
                break

    if not lig_found and len(all_ligs) == 1:
        st = all_ligs[0]
        lig_mol2 = out_dir / "ligand.mol2"
        structure.StructureWriter.write(st, str(lig_mol2))
        charge = st.formal_charge
        lig_found = True

    if not lig_found:
        raise ValueError(f"Ligand '{lig_title}' not found in {lig_file}")

    with open(out_dir / "lig_charge.txt", "w") as f:
        f.write(str(charge))
    print(f"Extracted files successfully. Ligand formal charge: {charge}")

def extract_via_rdkit(lig_file, lig_title, rec_file, out_dir):
    from rdkit import Chem
    out_dir = Path(out_dir)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    # Receptor copy / conversion
    rec_path = Path(rec_file)
    if rec_path.suffix.lower() == ".pdb":
        import shutil
        shutil.copy(rec_path, out_dir / "receptor.pdb")
    
    suppl = Chem.SDMolSupplier(str(lig_file), removeHs=False)
    target_mol = None
    
    for mol in suppl:
        if mol is None: continue
        name = mol.GetProp("_Name") if mol.HasProp("_Name") else ""
        if not lig_title or name == lig_title.strip() or name.lower() == lig_title.strip().lower():
            target_mol = mol
            break
            
    if target_mol is None and len(suppl) == 1 and suppl[0] is not None:
        target_mol = suppl[0]
        
    if target_mol is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Ligand '{lig_title}' not found in {lig_file}")
        
    charge = Chem.GetFormalCharge(target_mol)
    
    from rdkit.Chem import AllChem
    target_mol = Chem.AddHs(target_mol, addCoords=True)
    if not target_mol.GetNumConformers() or all(target_mol.GetConformer().GetAtomPosition(i).z == 0 for i in range(target_mol.GetNumAtoms())):
        AllChem.EmbedMolecule(target_mol)

    # Save mol2 via Chem.MolToMol2Block or openbabel / antechamber prep
    sdf_out = out_dir / "ligand.sdf"
    writer = Chem.SDWriter(str(sdf_out))
    writer.write(target_mol)
    writer.close()
    
    # Use antechamber to convert sdf to mol2 inside out_dir only
    import subprocess
    import shutil
    antechamber_bin = shutil.which("antechamber") or str(Path(sys.prefix) / "bin" / "antechamber")
    cmd = [antechamber_bin, "-i", "ligand.sdf", "-fi", "sdf", "-o", "ligand.mol2", "-fo", "mol2"]
    subprocess.run(cmd, check=True, cwd=str(out_dir))
    # Clean up antechamber intermediate files in out_dir
    for tmp_ac in out_dir.glob("ANTECHAMBER*"):
        tmp_ac.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    for tmp_info in out_dir.glob("ATOMTYPE*"):
        tmp_info.unlink(missing_ok=True)

    with open(out_dir / "lig_charge.txt", "w") as f:
        f.write(str(charge))
    print(f"Extracted files via RDKit. Formal charge: {charge}")

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Extracts receptor and ligand files for MD")
    parser.add_argument("--lig_file", required=True, help="Input docked pose file (.maegz or .sdf)")
    parser.add_argument("--lig_title", default="", help="Title or ID of target ligand")
    parser.add_argument("--rec_file", required=True, help="Input receptor file (.mae or .pdb)")
    parser.add_argument("--out_dir", required=True, help="Output directory")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    try:
        extract_via_schrodinger(args.lig_file, args.lig_title, args.rec_file, args.out_dir)
    except ImportError:
        extract_via_rdkit(args.lig_file, args.lig_title, args.rec_file, args.out_dir)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error in extract_md_files: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


======================================================================
FILE: bin/filter_pv.py
======================================================================
"""
Worker script executed via $SCHRODINGER/run python3.
Filters a Pose Viewer file, keeping only ligands explicitly present in the whitelist for a given frame.
Supports splitting the filtered poses into chunks of N poses, each with the receptor prepended,
enabling massive distributed Prime MM-GBSA across cluster nodes.
"""
import argparse
import sys
import csv
from schrodinger import structure

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Filter Pose Viewer file by whitelist and optionally chunk")
    parser.add_argument("--input", required=True, help="Input _pv.maegz file")
    parser.add_argument("--output", required=True, help="Filtered output _pv.maegz file")
    parser.add_argument("--whitelist", required=True, help="Path to whitelist.csv")
    parser.add_argument("--frame", required=True, help="Current frame ID")
    parser.add_argument("--max_poses", type=int, default=1, help="Maximum poses per ligand for MM-GBSA")
    parser.add_argument("--out_prefix", default="", help="Prefix for chunked outputs, e.g. frame_filtered")
    parser.add_argument("--batch_size", "--chunk_size", dest="batch_size", type=int, default=25, help="Poses per chunk")
    parser.add_argument("--output", default="", help="Optional single output _pv.maegz file")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    allowed_titles = set()
    with open(args.whitelist, "r") as f:
        reader = csv.DictReader(f)
        for row in reader:
            if str(row["Frame"]) == str(args.frame):
                allowed_titles.add(row["Title"])

    kept = 0
    receptor = None
    whitelisted_poses = []
    pose_counts = {}
    with structure.StructureReader(args.input) as reader, structure.StructureWriter(args.output) as writer:

    with structure.StructureReader(args.input) as reader:
        for i, st in enumerate(reader):
            # Always keep the receptor (first structure)
            if i == 0 and (st.property.get("b_glide_receptor", 0) == 1 or st.atom_total > 500):
                writer.append(st)
            if i == 0:
                receptor = st.copy()
                continue
                
            title = (st.property.get("s_m_title") or st.title or "").strip()
            # Only keep top n poses for whitelisted ligands
            if title in allowed_titles and pose_counts.get(title, 0) < args.max_poses:
                writer.append(st)
                whitelisted_poses.append(st.copy())
                pose_counts[title] = pose_counts.get(title, 0) + 1
                kept += 1

    print(f"SUCCESS: Extracted receptor and {kept} whitelisted poses for Frame {args.frame}.")
    print(f"Frame {args.frame}: Identified receptor and {len(whitelisted_poses)} whitelisted poses.")

    # 1. Single file output if requested
    if args.output:
        with structure.StructureWriter(args.output) as writer:
            if receptor:
                writer.append(receptor)
            for p in whitelisted_poses:
                writer.append(p)
        print(f"Wrote single filtered PV to {args.output}")

    # 2. Chunked output if out_prefix specified
    if args.out_prefix:
        if not whitelisted_poses:
            # Output at least one chunk with receptor so downstream step has a valid PV file
            c0_file = f"{args.out_prefix}_c0.maegz"
            with structure.StructureWriter(c0_file) as writer:
                if receptor:
                    writer.append(receptor)
            print(f"No whitelisted poses for frame {args.frame}. Wrote receptor-only chunk {c0_file}.")
        else:
            chunk_idx = 0
            for i in range(0, len(whitelisted_poses), args.batch_size):
                chunk = whitelisted_poses[i : i + args.batch_size]
                chunk_file = f"{args.out_prefix}_c{chunk_idx}.maegz"
                with structure.StructureWriter(chunk_file) as writer:
                    if receptor:
                        writer.append(receptor)
                    for p in chunk:
                        writer.append(p)
                print(f"Wrote chunk {chunk_idx}: receptor + {len(chunk)} poses -> {chunk_file}")
                chunk_idx += 1

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: bin/generate_lids.py
