#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
File: src/schrodinger_workers/generate_lids.py
Description: Worker script executed via $SCHRODINGER/run python3.
Generates publication-quality 2D Ligand Interaction Diagrams (LID) using
the Schrödinger LiveDesign API (schrodinger.application.livedesign.lid).
Enforces headless offscreen Qt initialization to prevent segmentation faults.
"""
import argparse
import json
import os
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path

# 1. Enforce headless offscreen platform before any Qt or Schrodinger module imports
os.environ["QT_QPA_PLATFORM"] = "offscreen"

# 2. Initialize QApplication instance to satisfy C++ Qt paint engine in _canvas2d
try:
    from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QApplication
except ImportError:
    from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QApplication

app = QApplication.instance()
if app is None:
    app = QApplication(["--platform", "offscreen"])

from schrodinger import structure
from schrodinger.application.livedesign import lid


def _clean_title(title: str) -> str:
    """Strips conformer and docking mode suffixes for title matching."""
    return re.sub(r"(_model_\d+|_dock_\d+|_\d+)$", "", title).strip()


def _render_and_save(lig_st, task: dict, receptor_cache: dict) -> bool:
    """Renders 2D interaction diagram for a ligand-receptor pair and saves PNG."""
    rec_file = task.get("receptor_file")
    out_png = Path(task["out_png"])
    title = task["title"]

    rec_st = None
    if rec_file and Path(rec_file).exists():
        if rec_file not in receptor_cache:
            try:
                receptor_cache[rec_file] = structure.StructureReader.read(rec_file)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[ERROR] Failed reading receptor file {rec_file}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
                receptor_cache[rec_file] = None
        rec_st = receptor_cache[rec_file]

    if rec_st is None:
        print(f"[ERROR] No receptor structure available for {title} (path: {rec_file})", file=sys.stderr)
        return False

    try:
        qimage = lid.generate_lid(lig_st=lig_st, rec_st=rec_st)
        if qimage is not None and not qimage.isNull():
            out_png.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            qimage.save(str(out_png), "PNG")
            print(f"[SUCCESS] Saved LID: {out_png.name}")
            return True
        else:
            print(f"[WARN] Empty LID generated for compound: {title}", file=sys.stderr)
            return False
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[ERROR] Rendering failed for {title}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        return False


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Schrodinger 2D LID Generator Worker")
    parser.add_argument("--manifest", required=True, help="Path to JSON manifest file")
    parser.add_argument("--out_dir", required=True, help="Directory to save generated PNGs")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    manifest_path = Path(args.manifest)
    if not manifest_path.exists():
        print(f"[CRITICAL] Manifest file not found: {manifest_path}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

    with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        tasks = json.load(f)

    print(f"Loaded {len(tasks)} LID task(s) from manifest.")

    receptor_cache = {}
    tasks_by_file = {}
    for t in tasks:
        tasks_by_file.setdefault(t["ligand_file"], []).append(t)

    success_count = 0

    for lig_file_str, file_tasks in tasks_by_file.items():
        lig_path = Path(lig_file_str)
        if not lig_path.exists():
            print(f"[ERROR] Ligand source file missing: {lig_path}", file=sys.stderr)
            continue

        # Build lookup table for fast matching
        task_lookup = {}
        for t in file_tasks:
            raw_t = t["title"].strip()
            task_lookup[raw_t] = t
            task_lookup[_clean_title(raw_t)] = t
            task_lookup[raw_t.lower()] = t

        rendered_outputs = set()
        collected_ligands = []

        try:
            with structure.StructureReader(str(lig_path)) as reader:
                for idx, st in enumerate(reader):
                    # Skip receptor entry in Pose Viewer files
                    if idx == 0 and (st.property.get("b_glide_receptor", 0) == 1 or st.atom_total > 500):
                        continue

                    collected_ligands.append(st)
                    st_title = (
                        st.property.get("s_m_title")
                        or st.title
                        or st.property.get("_Name")
                        or f"pose_{idx}"
                    ).strip()

                    match = (
                        task_lookup.get(st_title)
                        or task_lookup.get(_clean_title(st_title))
                        or task_lookup.get(st_title.lower())
                    )

                    if match:
                        out_key = match["out_png"]
                        if out_key not in rendered_outputs:
                            if _render_and_save(st, match, receptor_cache):
                                success_count += 1
                            rendered_outputs.add(out_key)

            # Fallback for dedicated single-ligand files (e.g. ref_native_f*.mae)
            if len(file_tasks) == 1 and len(rendered_outputs) == 0 and collected_ligands:
                single_task = file_tasks[0]
                if _render_and_save(collected_ligands[0], single_task, receptor_cache):
                    success_count += 1

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[ERROR] Failed reading ligand poses from {lig_path}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)

    print(f"LID generation finished. Successfully generated: {success_count}/{len(tasks)} diagrams.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

======================================================================
FILE: bin/gromacs_ndx_fixer.py
======================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
File: src/utils/gromacs_utils.py
Description: Safe topology and index manipulations for GROMACS.
Builds thermostat groups strictly by dictionary name lookup, NO index offsets.
"""
import os
import sys
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Set
from loguru import logger

def get_gmx_binary(config: dict = None) -> str:
    """
    Strictly validates and returns the system GROMACS binary 'gmx' from PATH.
    Terminates gracefully on failure without unhandled tracebacks.
    """
    if not shutil.which("gmx"):
        logger.critical("System GROMACS binary 'gmx' not found in PATH.")
        logger.error("Please ensure your newly compiled GROMACS is added to PATH (e.g., source GMXRC).")
        sys.exit(1)
    return "gmx"

def parse_ndx(ndx_path: Path) -> Dict[str, List[int]]:
    """Parses an NDX file into a dictionary of group_name -> list of atom IDs."""
    groups: Dict[str, List[int]] = {}
    current_group = None
    with open(ndx_path, "r") as f:
        for line in f:
            line = line.strip()
            if not line:
                continue
            if line.startswith("[") and line.endswith("]"):
                current_group = line[1:-1].strip()
                groups[current_group] = []
            elif current_group is not None:
                groups[current_group].extend([int(x) for x in line.split()])
    return groups

def write_ndx_group(f, name: str, atom_ids: List[int]):
    """Writes a single NDX group strictly formatted in columns of 15."""
    f.write(f"\n[ {name} ]\n")
    for i in range(0, len(atom_ids), 15):
        f.write(" ".join(f"{x:5d}" for x in atom_ids[i:i+15]) + "\n")

def add_thermostat_groups_by_name(ndx_path: Path):
    """
    Builds PROT_LIG and SOL_ION groups strictly by string matching names in index.ndx.
    NO numerical index offsets. Guaranteed partition of the entire System.
    """
    groups = parse_ndx(ndx_path)
    
    if "System" not in groups:
        raise KeyError("Group [ System ] not found in index.ndx")

    water_and_ion_atoms: Set[int] = set()
    ion_names = {"K+", "Cl-", "NA+", "CL-", "NA", "CL", "K", "POT", "CLA", "SOD", "MG", "ZN", "CA", "CAL"}
    
    # 1. Match water strictly by name
    if "SOL" in groups:
        water_and_ion_atoms.update(groups["SOL"])
    elif "Water" in groups:
        water_and_ion_atoms.update(groups["Water"])
    else:
        raise KeyError("Neither [ SOL ] nor [ Water ] found in index.ndx")

    # 2. Match ions strictly by name
    for ion_name in ion_names:
        if ion_name in groups:
            water_and_ion_atoms.update(groups[ion_name])

    # 3. Everything else in System is solute + membrane (Protein + UNK + Lipids)
    system_atoms = set(groups["System"])
    solute_membrane_atoms = system_atoms - water_and_ion_atoms

    # Strict partition validation
    if not solute_membrane_atoms:
        raise ValueError("PROT_LIG group is empty! Check index.ndx names.")
    if not water_and_ion_atoms:
        raise ValueError("SOL_ION group is empty! Check index.ndx names.")
    if len(solute_membrane_atoms) + len(water_and_ion_atoms) != len(system_atoms):
        raise ValueError("Partition mismatch: PROT_LIG + SOL_ION != System!")

    # Append new groups strictly formatted
    with open(ndx_path, "a") as f:
        write_ndx_group(f, "PROT_LIG", sorted(list(solute_membrane_atoms)))
        write_ndx_group(f, "SOL_ION", sorted(list(water_and_ion_atoms)))

def inject_ligand_posre(top_file: Path):
    """Injects position restraints with multiple force constants at the end of UNK moleculetype."""
    if not top_file.exists(): return
    lines = top_file.read_text().splitlines()
    
    out_lines = []
    in_unk = False
    injected = False
    
    posre_block = (
        "\n#ifdef POSRES_LIG\n"
        "#include \"posre_lig.itp\"\n"
        "#endif\n"
        "#ifdef POSRES_LIG_250\n"
        "#include \"posre_lig_250.itp\"\n"
        "#endif\n"
        "#ifdef POSRES_LIG_50\n"
        "#include \"posre_lig_50.itp\"\n"
        "#endif\n"
    )
    
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        
        if in_unk and not injected and (stripped.startswith("[ moleculetype ]") or stripped.startswith("[ system ]")):
            out_lines.append(posre_block)
            injected = True
            in_unk = False
            
        if stripped.startswith("[ moleculetype ]"):
            in_unk = False
            
        if not stripped.startswith(";") and stripped.startswith("UNK") and len(stripped.split()) >= 2:
            in_unk = True
            
        out_lines.append(line)

    if in_unk and not injected:
        out_lines.append(posre_block)
        
    # Inject protein multi-level posres at the end of system1 or Protein
    out_lines_prot = []
    in_prot = False
    injected_prot = False
    
    posre_block_prot = (
        "\n#ifdef POSRES\n"
        "#include \"posre.itp\"\n"
        "#endif\n"
        "#ifdef POSRES_250\n"
        "#include \"posre_250.itp\"\n"
        "#endif\n"
        "#ifdef POSRES_50\n"
        "#include \"posre_50.itp\"\n"
        "#endif\n"
    )
    
    for line in out_lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        
        if in_prot and not injected_prot and (stripped.startswith("[ moleculetype ]") or stripped.startswith("[ system ]")):
            out_lines_prot.append(posre_block_prot)
            injected_prot = True
            in_prot = False
            
        if stripped.startswith("[ moleculetype ]"):
            in_prot = False
            
        if not stripped.startswith(";") and (stripped.startswith("system1") or stripped.startswith("Protein")) and len(stripped.split()) >= 2:
            in_prot = True
            
        out_lines_prot.append(line)

    top_file.write_text("\n".join(out_lines_prot) + "\n")

def fix_posre_indices(itp_file: Path):
    """Converts global atom indices in posre_lig.itp to 1-based local molecule indices (1 to N)."""
    if not itp_file.exists(): return
    lines = itp_file.read_text().splitlines()
    out_lines = []
    atom_idx = 1
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if not stripped or stripped.startswith(";") or stripped.startswith("["):
            out_lines.append(line)
            continue
        parts = stripped.split()
        if len(parts) >= 5:
            out_lines.append(f"{atom_idx:5d}{int(parts[1]):5d}{float(parts[2]):10.1f}{float(parts[3]):10.1f}{float(parts[4]):10.1f}")
            atom_idx += 1
        else:
            out_lines.append(line)
    itp_file.write_text("\n".join(out_lines) + "\n")

======================================================================
FILE: bin/md_metrics.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
File: bin/md_metrics.py
Description: Standalone CLI to calculate MD trajectory metrics (RMSD, RMSF, Contacts).
Outputs clean CSV tables and JSON summary without graphical dependencies.
"""
import argparse
import sys
import json
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import MDAnalysis as mda
from MDAnalysis.analysis import align, rms
from MDAnalysis.lib.distances import capped_distance

def compute_rmsd_rmsf(pdb_path: str, xtc_path: str):
    u = mda.Universe(pdb_path, xtc_path)
    ref = mda.Universe(pdb_path)
    
    prot_bb = u.select_atoms("protein and backbone")
    ref_bb = ref.select_atoms("protein and backbone")
    lig_heavy = u.select_atoms("resname UNK and not name H*")
    ref_lig = ref.select_atoms("resname UNK and not name H*")
    
    rmsd_results = []
    for ts in u.trajectory:
        align.alignto(u.atoms, ref.atoms, select="protein and backbone")
        time_ns = ts.time / 1000.0
        
        prot_rmsd = float(rms.rmsd(prot_bb.positions, ref_bb.positions))
        lig_rmsd = float(rms.rmsd(lig_heavy.positions, ref_lig.positions)) if lig_heavy.n_atoms > 0 else np.nan
        
        rmsd_results.append({
            "Time_ns": round(time_ns, 3),
            "Prot_BB_RMSD_A": round(prot_rmsd, 3),
            "Lig_Heavy_RMSD_A": round(lig_rmsd, 3) if not np.isnan(lig_rmsd) else None
        })
        
    df_rmsd = pd.DataFrame(rmsd_results)
    
    calphas = u.select_atoms("protein and name CA")
    R = rms.RMSF(calphas).run()
    
    df_rmsf = pd.DataFrame({
        "ResID": calphas.resids,
        "ResName": calphas.resnames,
        "RMSF_A": [round(float(v), 3) for v in R.results.rmsf]
    })
    
    return df_rmsd, df_rmsf

def compute_contacts(pdb_path: str, xtc_path: str, c_heavy=3.5, c_hyd=4.0, c_pol=3.4):
    u = mda.Universe(pdb_path, xtc_path)
    lig = u.select_atoms("resname UNK")
    prot = u.select_atoms("protein")
    
    if lig.n_atoms == 0 or prot.n_atoms == 0:
        return pd.DataFrame()
        
    l_heavy = lig.select_atoms("not name H*")
    p_heavy = prot.select_atoms("not name H*")
    l_c = lig.select_atoms("name C*")
    p_c = prot.select_atoms("name C*")
    l_pol = lig.select_atoms("name N* or name O* or name S* or name P*")
    p_pol = prot.select_atoms("name N* or name O* or name S* or name P*")
    
    unique_resids = np.unique(p_heavy.resids)
    counts = {r: {"Total": 0, "Hydrophobic": 0, "Polar": 0} for r in unique_resids}
    n_frames = len(u.trajectory)
    
    for _ in u.trajectory:
        pairs_tot = capped_distance(l_heavy.positions, p_heavy.positions, max_cutoff=c_heavy, return_distances=False)
        if len(pairs_tot) > 0:
            for r in np.unique(p_heavy.resids[pairs_tot[:, 1]]):
                counts[r]["Total"] += 1
                
        if l_c.n_atoms > 0 and p_c.n_atoms > 0:
            pairs_hyd = capped_distance(l_c.positions, p_c.positions, max_cutoff=c_hyd, return_distances=False)
            if len(pairs_hyd) > 0:
                for r in np.unique(p_c.resids[pairs_hyd[:, 1]]):
                    counts[r]["Hydrophobic"] += 1
                    
        if l_pol.n_atoms > 0 and p_pol.n_atoms > 0:
            pairs_pol = capped_distance(l_pol.positions, p_pol.positions, max_cutoff=c_pol, return_distances=False)
            if len(pairs_pol) > 0:
                for r in np.unique(p_pol.resids[pairs_pol[:, 1]]):
                    counts[r]["Polar"] += 1

    results = []
    for r, c in counts.items():
        occ_tot = (c["Total"] / n_frames) * 100.0
        if occ_tot >= 1.0:
            res_atoms = prot.select_atoms(f"resid {r}")
            res_name = res_atoms.resnames[0] if len(res_atoms) > 0 else "UNK"
            results.append({
                "ResID": int(r),
                "ResName": res_name,
                "Total_Occ_Pct": round(occ_tot, 1),
                "Hydrophobic_Occ_Pct": round((c["Hydrophobic"] / n_frames) * 100.0, 1),
                "Polar_Occ_Pct": round((c["Polar"] / n_frames) * 100.0, 1)
            })
            
    df_contacts = pd.DataFrame(results)
    if not df_contacts.empty:
        df_contacts = df_contacts.sort_values("Total_Occ_Pct", ascending=False)
    return df_contacts

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Calculate MD metrics (RMSD, RMSF, Contacts)")
    parser.add_argument("--pdb", required=True, help="Topology or Reference PDB/GRO")
    parser.add_argument("--xtc", required=True, help="Clean Centered Trajectory XTC")
    parser.add_argument("--outdir", required=True, help="Output directory")
    parser.add_argument("--heavy_cutoff", type=float, default=3.5)
    parser.add_argument("--hydrophobic_cutoff", type=float, default=4.0)
    parser.add_argument("--polar_cutoff", type=float, default=3.4)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    out_dir = Path(args.outdir)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    try:
        df_rmsd, df_rmsf = compute_rmsd_rmsf(args.pdb, args.xtc)
        df_rmsd.to_csv(out_dir / "rmsd.csv", index=False)
        df_rmsf.to_csv(out_dir / "rmsf.csv", index=False)

        df_contacts = compute_contacts(
            args.pdb, args.xtc,
            c_heavy=args.heavy_cutoff,
            c_hyd=args.hydrophobic_cutoff,
            c_pol=args.polar_cutoff
        )
        df_contacts.to_csv(out_dir / "contacts.csv", index=False)

        summary = {
            "Prot_BB_RMSD_Mean_A": round(float(df_rmsd["Prot_BB_RMSD_A"].mean()), 3),
            "Prot_BB_RMSD_Std_A": round(float(df_rmsd["Prot_BB_RMSD_A"].std()), 3),
            "Prot_BB_RMSD_Last_A": round(float(df_rmsd["Prot_BB_RMSD_A"].iloc[-1]), 3),
            "Lig_Heavy_RMSD_Mean_A": round(float(df_rmsd["Lig_Heavy_RMSD_A"].dropna().mean()), 3) if not df_rmsd["Lig_Heavy_RMSD_A"].dropna().empty else None,
            "Lig_Heavy_RMSD_Last_A": round(float(df_rmsd["Lig_Heavy_RMSD_A"].dropna().iloc[-1]), 3) if not df_rmsd["Lig_Heavy_RMSD_A"].dropna().empty else None,
            "Top_Contacts": df_contacts.head(10).to_dict(orient="records") if not df_contacts.empty else []
        }

        with open(out_dir / "md_metrics_summary.json", "w") as f:
            json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)

        print(f"MD metrics calculated successfully. Saved to {out_dir}")

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error calculating MD metrics: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(1)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
File: src/utils/merge_complex_parmed.py
Description: Safely merges Protein PDB and Ligand MOL2 using ParmEd.
Converts ResidueTemplate to Structure if necessary.
"""
import sys
import parmed as pmd
from loguru import logger

def merge_complex(prot_pdb: str, lig_mol2: str, out_pdb: str):
    logger.info(f"Merging {prot_pdb} and {lig_mol2}...")
    prot = pmd.load_file(prot_pdb)
    lig = pmd.load_file(lig_mol2)

    # Convert ResidueTemplate to Structure to expose .residues
    if hasattr(lig, "to_structure"):
        lig = lig.to_structure()

    # Find a free chain ID for the ligand
    used_chains = {res.chain for res in prot.residues}
    lig_chain = next((c for c in "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ" if c not in used_chains), "Z")

    for res in lig.residues:
        res.chain = lig_chain
        res.name = "UNK"

    complex_struct = prot + lig
    complex_struct.save(out_pdb, overwrite=True)
    logger.info(f"Complex saved to {out_pdb} (Ligand assigned to chain {lig_chain})")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    merge_complex(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3])

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
bin/parse_immers.py
Parses PPM 3.0 datapar1 output file into JSON and text thickness deliverables.
"""
import argparse
import json
import logging
import sys
from pathlib import Path

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(levelname)s: %(message)s", stream=sys.stdout)

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Parse PPM 3.0 immersion datapar1 output")
    parser.add_argument("--datapar", default="datapar1", help="Path to datapar1 file")
    parser.add_argument("--out_json", required=True, help="Output JSON path")
    parser.add_argument("--out_txt", required=True, help="Output text path (thickness only)")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    data = {"thickness": 28.0, "tilt": 0.0, "dg": 0.0}
    datapar_path = Path(args.datapar)

    if datapar_path.exists():
        try:
            with open(datapar_path, "r") as f:
                parts = [p.strip() for p in f.readline().split(";")]
                data["thickness"] = float(parts[1])
                data["tilt"] = float(parts[3])
                data["dg"] = float(parts[5])
            logging.info(f"Parsed PPM 3.0: thickness={data['thickness']}, tilt={data['tilt']}, dG={data['dg']}")
        except Exception as e:
            logging.warning(f"Failed to parse datapar1 ({e}); using defaults: {data}")
    else:
        logging.warning(f"{datapar_path} not found; using defaults: {data}")

    with open(args.out_json, "w") as f_json:
        json.dump(data, f_json, indent=2)

    with open(args.out_txt, "w") as f_txt:
        f_txt.write(str(data["thickness"]))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: bin/pv_to_sdf.py

"""
Extracts docked ligand poses from Pose Viewer MAE to a multi-conformation SDF.
"""
import argparse
from schrodinger import structure

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--pv", required=True, help="Input _pv.maegz file")
    parser.add_argument("--sdf", required=True, help="Output .sdf file")
    parser.add_argument("--max_poses", type=int, default=1, help="Maximum poses per ligand to export")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    pose_counts = {}
    with structure.StructureReader(args.pv) as reader, structure.StructureWriter(args.sdf) as writer:
        for i, st in enumerate(reader):
            # Skip the receptor
            if i == 0 and (st.property.get("b_glide_receptor", 0) == 1 or st.atom_total > 500):
                continue
            title = (st.property.get("s_m_title") or st.title or "").strip()
            # Retain only up to max_poses for each unique ligand title
            if not title or pose_counts.get(title, 0) < args.max_poses:
                writer.append(st)
                if title:
                    pose_counts[title] = pose_counts.get(title, 0) + 1

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
bin/select_hits.py
Extracts top-ranked hits from ranking.csv using standard library csv.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Select top hits from ranking.csv")
    parser.add_argument("--ranking_csv", required=True, help="Input ranking.csv file")
    parser.add_argument("--top_n", type=int, default=1, help="Number of top hits to select")
    parser.add_argument("--include_native", default="true", help="Always include native reference ligand")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default="selected_hits.txt", help="Output text file with selected titles")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    selected = []
    csv_path = Path(args.ranking_csv)
    if csv_path.exists() and csv_path.stat().st_size > 0:
        try:
            with open(csv_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
                reader = csv.DictReader(f)
                for row in reader:
                    title = row.get("Title") or row.get("title") or row.get("s_m_title") or ""
                    title = title.strip()
                    frame = row.get("Frame") or row.get("frame") or "F1"
                    frame_id = str(frame).replace("F", "").replace("f", "").strip() or "1"
                    if title and not any(s[0] == title for s in selected):
                        selected.append((title, frame_id))
                        if len(selected) >= args.top_n:
                            break
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Warning: Could not parse {args.ranking_csv}: {e}", file=sys.stderr)

    include_nat = str(args.include_native).lower() in ["true", "1", "yes"]
    if include_nat and not any(s[0] in ["native", "PKA"] for s in selected):
        selected.append(("native", "1"))

    if not selected:
        selected = [("native", "1")]

    print(f"Selected {len(selected)} hit(s) for MD simulation: {selected}")
    with open(args.out, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for title, fid in selected:
            f.write(f"{title},{fid}\n")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
bin/split_sdf.py
Splits an SDF library into chunks of N molecules.
Splits an SDF library into chunks of N molecules with minimal memory footprint (streaming).
"""
import argparse
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Split SDF into batches")
    parser.add_argument("--input", required=True, help="Input SDF file")
    parser.add_argument("--batch_size", "--chunk_size", dest="batch_size", type=int, default=15, help="Molecules per chunk")
    parser.add_argument("--outdir", default=".", help="Output directory for chunks")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    out_dir = Path(args.outdir)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    total_mols = 0
    chunk_idx = 0
    current_chunk = []
    current_mol_lines = []

    with open(args.input, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
        content = f.read()
        for line in f:
            current_mol_lines.append(line)
            if line.startswith("$$$$"):
                mol_text = "".join(current_mol_lines).strip()
                if mol_text:
                    current_chunk.append(mol_text)
                    total_mols += 1
                current_mol_lines = []

    mols = [m.strip() for m in content.split("$$$$") if m.strip()]
    print(f"Detected {len(mols)} molecules in {args.input}")
                if len(current_chunk) >= args.batch_size:
                    out_file = out_dir / f"chunk_{chunk_idx:03d}.sdf"
                    with open(out_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
                        out.write("\n$$$$\n".join(current_chunk) + "\n$$$$\n")
                    chunk_idx += 1
                    current_chunk = []

    total_chunks = 0
    for idx, i in enumerate(range(0, len(mols), args.batch_size)):
        chunk = mols[i:i + args.batch_size]
        out_file = out_dir / f"chunk_{idx:03d}.sdf"
        with open(out_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
            out.write("\n$$$$\n".join(chunk) + "\n$$$$\n")
        total_chunks += 1
        # Handle any trailing molecule without $$$$
        if current_mol_lines:
            mol_text = "".join(current_mol_lines).strip()
            if mol_text:
                current_chunk.append(mol_text)
                total_mols += 1

    print(f"Successfully created {total_chunks} chunks of size <= {args.batch_size}.")
        if current_chunk:
            out_file = out_dir / f"chunk_{chunk_idx:03d}.sdf"
            with open(out_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
                out.write("\n$$$$\n".join(current_chunk) + "\n$$$$\n")
            chunk_idx += 1

    print(f"Processed {total_mols} molecules across {chunk_idx} chunks (size <= {args.batch_size}).")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

======================================================================
FILE: bin/summarize_results.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
bin/summarize_results.py
Master aggregator:
Parses Glide CSVs, GNINA Dock SDFs, GNINA Rescore SDFs, Prime MM-GBSA CSVs,
and outputs complete, structured analysis CSV tables:
1. ranking.csv (Consensus ranked table with Ultimate_Score, Percentile Ranks, Glide, MMGBSA, CNN Affinity, Pose RMSD)
2. cross_frame_matrix.csv (Pivot matrix of Ultimate_Score across all frames)
3. summary.json (JSON dump of ranking)
"""
import argparse
import sys
import json
import csv
import re
from pathlib import Path
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np


def parse_glide_csv(csv_path: Path) -> dict:
    data = {}
    if not csv_path.exists():
        return data
    with open(csv_path, "r", errors="ignore") as f:
        reader = csv.DictReader(f)
        for row in reader:
            title = row.get("title") or row.get("Title") or row.get("s_m_title")
            if not title or title in data:
                continue

            score = None
            for k in ["r_i_docking_score", "docking_score"]:
                if k in row and row[k]:
                    try:
                        score = float(row[k])
                        break
                    except ValueError:
                        pass
            if score is None:
                for k in ["r_i_glide_gscore", "gscore"]:
                    if k in row and row[k]:
                        try:
                            score = float(row[k])
                            break
                        except ValueError:
                            pass
            if score is not None:
                data[title] = {"GlideScore": score}
    return data


def parse_prime_csv(csv_path: Path) -> dict:
    data = {}
    if not csv_path.exists():
        return data
    with open(csv_path, "r", errors="ignore") as f:
        reader = csv.DictReader(f)
        for row in reader:
            title = row.get("title") or row.get("Title") or row.get("s_m_title")
            if not title or title in data:
                continue
            props = {}
            for k, v in row.items():
                if not k or not v:
                    continue
                k_clean = k.strip()
                if k_clean in ["r_psp_MMGBSA_dG_Bind", "MMGBSA dG Bind"]:
                    try:
                        props["MMGBSA"] = float(v)
                        break
                    except ValueError:
                        pass

            for k, v in row.items():
                if not k or not v:
                    continue
                if k.strip() in ["r_psp_Lig_Strain_Energy", "Lig Strain Energy"]:
                    try:
                        props["Lig_Strain"] = float(v)
                        break
                    except ValueError:
                        pass

            if props:
                data[title] = props
    return data


def parse_gnina_sdf(sdf_path: Path, prefix: str = "") -> dict:
    data = {}
    if not sdf_path or not sdf_path.exists():
        return data
    with open(sdf_path, "r", errors="ignore") as f:
        content = f.read()
    for entry in content.split("$$$$"):
        lines = entry.strip().splitlines()
        if not lines:
            continue

        raw_title = lines[0].strip()
        prop_title = None
        props = {}

        for i, line in enumerate(lines):
            line_str = line.strip()
            if line_str.startswith("> <") and line_str.endswith(">"):
                tag_clean = line_str[3:-1].strip().lower()
                if i + 1 < len(lines):
                    val_str = lines[i + 1].strip()
                    if tag_clean in ["_name", "s_m_title", "title", "compound_id"]:
                        prop_title = val_str
                    else:
                        try:
                            val = float(val_str)
                            if tag_clean == "cnnscore":
                                props[f"{prefix}CNNscore"] = val
                            elif tag_clean == "cnnaffinity":
                                props[f"{prefix}CNNaffinity"] = val
                            elif tag_clean in ["minimizedaffinity", "affinity"]:
                                props[f"{prefix}VinaAff"] = val
                        except ValueError:
                            pass

        title = prop_title or raw_title
        if not title:
            continue

        title_clean = re.sub(r"(_model_\d+|_dock_\d+|_\d+)$", "", title).strip()

        if props:
            if title not in data:
                data[title] = props
            if title_clean and title_clean not in data:
                data[title_clean] = props
    return data


def compute_pose_consensus_rmsd(df: pd.DataFrame, dock_dir: Path):
    """Calculates in-place heavy-atom RMSD between Glide and independent GNINA dock poses."""
    try:
        from rdkit import Chem
        from rdkit.Chem import rdMolAlign
    except ImportError:
        df["Pose_RMSD"] = np.nan
        return df

    def _extract_sdf_block(filepath: Path, target_title: str) -> str:
        if not filepath.exists():
            return ""
        with open(filepath, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
            content = f.read()
        for block in content.split("$$$$"):
            lines = block.strip().splitlines()
            if not lines:
                continue
            title = lines[0].strip()
            if title == target_title:
                return block.strip() + "\n$$$$\n"
        return ""

    rmsd_list = []
    for _, row in df.iterrows():
        frame_id = str(row["Frame"]).replace("F", "").replace("f", "")
        title = row["Title"]

        # Search for glide and gnina poses in dock_dir
        glide_sdfs = list(dock_dir.glob(f"**/*f{frame_id}*dock*.sdf"))
        gnina_sdfs = list(dock_dir.glob(f"**/*f{frame_id}*gnina*.sdf"))
        # Search for glide and gnina poses in dock_dir with exact frame boundary
        patt = rf"_f{frame_id}(?:[^0-9]|$)"
        glide_sdfs = [f for f in dock_dir.glob("**/*dock*.sdf") if re.search(patt, f.name)]
        gnina_sdfs = [f for f in dock_dir.glob("**/*gnina*.sdf") if re.search(patt, f.name)]

        glide_sdf = glide_sdfs[0] if glide_sdfs else None
        gnina_sdf = gnina_sdfs[0] if gnina_sdfs else None

        if glide_sdf and gnina_sdf:
            block_g = _extract_sdf_block(glide_sdf, title)
            block_n = _extract_sdf_block(gnina_sdf, title)
            if block_g and block_n:
                try:
                    mg = Chem.RemoveHs(Chem.MolFromMolBlock(block_g))
                    mn = Chem.RemoveHs(Chem.MolFromMolBlock(block_n))
                    if mg and mn and mg.GetNumAtoms() == mn.GetNumAtoms():
                        val = rdMolAlign.GetBestRMS(mg, mn)
                        rmsd_list.append(round(val, 2))
                        continue
                except Exception:
                    pass
        rmsd_list.append(np.nan)

    df["Pose_RMSD"] = rmsd_list
    return df


def calculate_consensus_score(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Computes Percentile Rank consensus score (0-100) exactly matching original pipeline."""
    if "GlideScore" in df.columns:
        df["PR_Glide"] = df["GlideScore"].rank(ascending=False, pct=True).fillna(0.0)
    if "MMGBSA" in df.columns:
        df["PR_MMGBSA"] = df["MMGBSA"].rank(ascending=False, pct=True).fillna(0.0)
    if "Resc_CNNaffinity" in df.columns:
        df["PR_CNN_Resc"] = df["Resc_CNNaffinity"].rank(ascending=True, pct=True).fillna(0.0)
    if "Dock_CNNaffinity" in df.columns:
        df["PR_GNINA_Dock"] = df["Dock_CNNaffinity"].rank(ascending=True, pct=True).fillna(0.0)

    pr_cols = [c for c in ["PR_Glide", "PR_MMGBSA", "PR_CNN_Resc"] if c in df.columns]
    if not pr_cols:
        pr_cols = [c for c in ["PR_GNINA_Dock"] if c in df.columns]

    if not pr_cols:
        df["Ultimate_Score"] = 0.0
    else:
        df["Ultimate_Score"] = (df[pr_cols].sum(axis=1) / len(pr_cols)) * 100.0
    return df


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Aggregate project results into master ranking")
    parser.add_argument("--docking_dir", help="Directory containing docking poses and tables")
    parser.add_argument("--analysis_dir", help="Directory containing MM-GBSA csv files")
    parser.add_argument("--outdir", required=True, help="Output reports directory")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    out_dir = Path(args.outdir)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    dock_dir = Path(args.docking_dir) if args.docking_dir else Path(".")
    analysis_dir = Path(args.analysis_dir) if args.analysis_dir else dock_dir

    # Find frames by searching CSVs and SDFs
    glide_csvs = list(dock_dir.glob("**/*glide*f*.csv")) or list(dock_dir.glob("**/*dock*.csv"))
    mmgbsa_csvs = list(analysis_dir.glob("**/*mmgbsa*.csv"))
    resc_sdfs = list(dock_dir.glob("**/*rescored*.sdf")) or list(dock_dir.glob("**/*gnina_score_only*.sdf"))
    dock_sdfs = list(dock_dir.glob("**/*gnina_dock*.sdf"))

    # Map by frame ID
    all_frames = set()
    for p in glide_csvs + mmgbsa_csvs + resc_sdfs + dock_sdfs:
        match = re.search(r"_f(\d+)", p.stem)
        if match:
            all_frames.add(match.group(1))

    rows = []
    if not all_frames:
        # Fallback to single frame 1
        all_frames = {"1"}

    for fid in sorted(all_frames, key=lambda x: int(x) if x.isdigit() else x):
        frame_tag = f"F{fid}"

        # Match files for frame
        g_file = next((f for f in glide_csvs if f"_f{fid}" in f.name), None)
        m_file = next((f for f in mmgbsa_csvs if f"_f{fid}" in f.name), None)
        r_file = next((f for f in resc_sdfs if f"_f{fid}" in f.name), None)
        d_file = next((f for f in dock_sdfs if f"_f{fid}" in f.name), None)
        # Match files for frame with exact regex boundary (avoid _f1 matching _f10)
        pattern = rf"_f{fid}(?:[^0-9]|$)"
        g_files = [f for f in glide_csvs if re.search(pattern, f.name)]
        m_files = [f for f in mmgbsa_csvs if re.search(pattern, f.name)]
        r_files = [f for f in resc_sdfs if re.search(pattern, f.name)]
        d_files = [f for f in dock_sdfs if re.search(pattern, f.name)]

        glide_data = parse_glide_csv(g_file) if g_file else {}
        prime_data = parse_prime_csv(m_file) if m_file else {}
        resc_data = parse_gnina_sdf(r_file, prefix="Resc_") if r_file else {}
        dock_data = parse_gnina_sdf(d_file, prefix="Dock_") if d_file else {}
        glide_data = {}
        for f in g_files:
            glide_data.update(parse_glide_csv(f))

        prime_data = {}
        for f in m_files:
            prime_data.update(parse_prime_csv(f))

        resc_data = {}
        for f in r_files:
            resc_data.update(parse_gnina_sdf(f, prefix="Resc_"))

        dock_data = {}
        for f in d_files:
            dock_data.update(parse_gnina_sdf(f, prefix="Dock_"))

        titles = set(list(glide_data.keys()) + list(prime_data.keys()) + list(resc_data.keys()) + list(dock_data.keys()))
        for t in titles:
            r = {"Title": t, "Frame": frame_tag}
            r.update(glide_data.get(t, {}))
            r.update(prime_data.get(t, {}))
            r.update(resc_data.get(t, {}))
            r.update(dock_data.get(t, {}))
            rows.append(r)

    df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    if df.empty:
        print("Warning: No records found. Generating empty templates.")
        pd.DataFrame(columns=["Rank", "Title", "Frame", "Ultimate_Score"]).to_csv(out_dir / "ranking.csv", index=False)
        pd.DataFrame(columns=["Title"]).to_csv(out_dir / "cross_frame_matrix.csv", index=False)
        with open(out_dir / "summary.json", "w") as fj:
            json.dump([], fj)
        sys.exit(0)

    # Calculate Consensus Score
    df = calculate_consensus_score(df)

    # Pivot matrix: Ultimate_Score across all frames
    if "Ultimate_Score" in df.columns and "Frame" in df.columns:
        matrix_df = df.pivot_table(index="Title", columns="Frame", values="Ultimate_Score", aggfunc="max")
        matrix_df.to_csv(out_dir / "cross_frame_matrix.csv")

    # Resolved top hit per ligand
    df_resolved = df.sort_values("Ultimate_Score", ascending=False).drop_duplicates(subset="Title").copy()
    df_resolved = compute_pose_consensus_rmsd(df_resolved, dock_dir)

    df_resolved.reset_index(drop=True, inplace=True)
    df_resolved.index += 1
    df_resolved.index.name = "Rank"

    # Save ranking.csv
    df_resolved.to_csv(out_dir / "ranking.csv")

    # Save summary.json
    with open(out_dir / "summary.json", "w") as fj:
        json.dump(df_resolved.reset_index().to_dict(orient="records"), fj, indent=2)

    print(f"Master consensus ranking generated with {len(df_resolved)} compounds in {out_dir / 'ranking.csv'}")
    print(f"Cross-frame matrix saved in {out_dir / 'cross_frame_matrix.csv'}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

======================================================================
FILE: bin/utils/amber_prep_parmed.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
File: src/utils/amber_prep_parmed.py
Description: Robust preparation of Schrodinger PDBs for AMBER using ParmEd.
Handles NMA->NME, ACE hydrogens, HIS protonation, and CYS->CYX mapping.
"""
import sys
import numpy as np
import parmed as pmd
from loguru import logger

def fix_for_amber(in_pdb: str, out_pdb: str):
    logger.info(f"Loading {in_pdb} via ParmEd...")
    struct = pmd.load_file(in_pdb)

    # 1. Identify Disulfide Bridges (SG-SG distance < 2.5 A)
    cyx_res = set()
    cys_residues = [r for r in struct.residues if r.name in ["CYS", "CYX"]]
    for i, res1 in enumerate(cys_residues):
        sg1 = next((a for a in res1.atoms if a.name == "SG"), None)
        if not sg1: continue
        for res2 in cys_residues[i+1:]:
            sg2 = next((a for a in res2.atoms if a.name == "SG"), None)
            if not sg2: continue
            dist = np.linalg.norm(np.array([sg1.xx, sg1.xy, sg1.xz]) - np.array([sg2.xx, sg2.xy, sg2.xz]))
            if dist < 2.5:
                cyx_res.add(res1)
                cyx_res.add(res2)

    # 2. Rename residues and atoms
    for res in struct.residues:
        # Standard amino acid backbone amide hydrogen fix (H1 / HN / 1H -> H)
        if res.name not in ["ACE", "NME", "NMA"]:
            for a in res.atoms:
                if a.name in ["H1", "HN", "1H"]:
                    a.name = "H"

        # C-Terminal Cap (NMA / NME -> NME: atoms N, H, CH3, H1, H2, H3)
        if res.name in ["NMA", "NME"]:
            res.name = "NME"
            for a in res.atoms:
                if a.name in ["CA", "CH3"]: a.name = "C"
                elif a.name in ["1HA", "1H", "H1", "HH31"]: a.name = "H1"
                elif a.name in ["2HA", "2H", "H2", "HH32"]: a.name = "H2"
                elif a.name in ["3HA", "3H", "H3", "HH33"]: a.name = "H3"
        
        # N-Terminal Cap (ACE: atoms C, O, CH3, H1, H2, H3)
        elif res.name == "ACE":
            for a in res.atoms:
                if a.name in ["1H", "H1", "HH31"]: a.name = "H1"
                elif a.name in ["2H", "H2", "HH32"]: a.name = "H2"
                elif a.name in ["3H", "H3", "HH33"]: a.name = "H3"
        
        # Protonated Aspartate (ASP with HD2 -> ASH)
        elif res.name == "ASP":
            if any(a.name in ["HD2", "2HD"] for a in res.atoms):
                res.name = "ASH"

        # Protonated Glutamate (GLU with HE2 -> GLH)
        elif res.name == "GLU":
            if any(a.name in ["HE2", "2HE"] for a in res.atoms):
                res.name = "GLH"

        # Neutral Lysine (LYS without HZ3 -> LYN)
        elif res.name == "LYS":
            if not any(a.name in ["HZ3", "3HZ", "HZ"] for a in res.atoms):
                res.name = "LYN"

        # Histidine Protonation States (HIP, HID, HIE)
        elif res.name in ["HIS", "HSD", "HSE", "HSP"]:
            names = [a.name for a in res.atoms]
            if "HD1" in names and "HE2" in names: res.name = "HIP"
            elif "HD1" in names: res.name = "HID"
            elif "HE2" in names: res.name = "HIE"
            else: res.name = "HIE"
        
        # Cysteine Disulfides
        elif res in cyx_res:
            res.name = "CYX"
            hg_atom = next((a for a in res.atoms if a.name == "HG"), None)
            if hg_atom:
                struct.atoms.remove(hg_atom)
                res.atoms.remove(hg_atom)

    logger.info(f"Saving AMBER-compliant PDB to {out_pdb}...")
    struct.save(out_pdb, overwrite=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    fix_for_amber(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2])

======================================================================
FILE: bin/utils/cmd_runner.py
======================================================================
"""
File: src/utils/cmd_runner.py
Description: Generic, tool-agnostic command execution engine with live log monitoring.
Uses modular parsers from src/core/parsers.py and outputs real-time parser_debug.log.
"""
import subprocess
import time
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from loguru import logger

from core.parsers import get_parser, BaseLogParser

def run_command(cmd: list, cwd: Path, log_name: str, progress=None, task_id=None, 
                parser: BaseLogParser = None, stdin: Path = None, total_override: int = None,
                step_offset: int = 0) -> bool:
    cmd_str = " ".join(str(x) for x in cmd)
    
    log_dir = cwd / "logs"
    log_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    log_file = log_dir / f"{log_name}.log"
    
    logger.info(f"Executing: {cmd[0]} (Log: {log_file.name})")
    logger.debug(f"Full command: {cmd_str}")
    
    # Auto-resolve parser if not explicitly passed
    active_parser = parser or get_parser(cmd)
    
    # Target log detection: external file for Glide (.in), Prime (-jobname), or GROMACS (-deffnm)
    if len(cmd) > 1 and str(cmd[1]).endswith(".in"):
        target_job_log = cwd / f"{Path(cmd[1]).stem}.log"
        target_job_log.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    elif "-jobname" in cmd:
        job_name_arg = cmd[cmd.index("-jobname") + 1]
        target_job_log = cwd / f"{job_name_arg}.log"
    elif "-deffnm" in cmd:
        deffnm_arg = cmd[cmd.index("-deffnm") + 1]
        target_job_log = cwd / f"{deffnm_arg}.log"
    else:
        target_job_log = log_file

    start_time = time.time()

    try:
        with open(log_file, "w") as f_out:
            f_out.write(f"COMMAND: {cmd_str}\n\nOUTPUT:\n")
            f_out.flush()

            f_in = open(stdin, "r") if stdin and stdin.exists() else None
            process = subprocess.Popen(
                cmd, cwd=cwd, stdin=f_in, stdout=f_out, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT
            )

            while process.poll() is None:
                # Dynamically resolve active log file (handles GROMACS .part000X.log suffixes)
                active_log = target_job_log
                if "-deffnm" in cmd:
                    deffnm_arg = cmd[cmd.index("-deffnm") + 1]
                    matching_logs = sorted(cwd.glob(f"{deffnm_arg}*.log"), key=lambda p: p.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True)
                    if matching_logs:
                        active_log = matching_logs[0]

                if progress and (task_id is not None) and active_parser and active_log.exists():
                    try:
                        with open(active_log, "rb") as f_read:
                            f_read.seek(0, 2)
                            f_size = f_read.tell()
                            f_read.seek(max(0, f_size - 8192))
                            raw_data = f_read.read().decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")

                        update = active_parser.parse(raw_data)
                        if update:
                            update_kwargs = {}
                            if update.completed is not None:
                                current_step = max(0, update.completed - step_offset)
                                update_kwargs["completed"] = current_step
                            
                            if total_override is not None:
                                update_kwargs["total"] = total_override
                            elif update.total is not None: 
                                update_kwargs["total"] = update.total
                                
                            if update.description is not None: update_kwargs["description"] = update.description
                            
                            progress.update(task_id, **update_kwargs)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                time.sleep(0.5)

        returncode = process.returncode
        
        # Explicit progress finalization
        if progress and (task_id is not None):
            task = progress._tasks.get(task_id)
            if returncode == 0 and task and task.total:
                progress.update(task_id, completed=task.total)
            elif returncode != 0:
                progress.update(task_id, description=f"[red]FAILED: {Path(cmd[0]).name}[/red]")

        # Move stray logs created by Schrodinger JobControl
        for stray_log in cwd.glob("*.log"):
            if stray_log.is_file() and stray_log.name != log_file.name:
                shutil.move(str(stray_log), str(log_dir / stray_log.name))

    except Exception as e:
        logger.critical(f"Failed to launch command {cmd[0]}: {e}")
        return False

    duration = time.time() - start_time
    
    if returncode != 0:
        logger.error(f"Command '{cmd[0]}' failed with exit code {returncode}.")
        try:
            with open(log_file, "r") as f:
                logger.error(f"Tail of output:\n{''.join(f.readlines()[-10:])}")
        except Exception:
            pass
        return False
        
    logger.success(f"Command '{cmd[0]}' completed in {duration:.1f}s.")
    return True

======================================================================
FILE: bin/utils/count_structures.py
======================================================================
"""
Dynamically counts the number of structures in MAE/MAEGZ/SDF using Schrodinger API.
"""
import os
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from loguru import logger

def get_structure_count(file_path: Path) -> int:
    schrodinger_path = os.environ.get("SCHRODINGER")
    cmd = [
        f"{schrodinger_path}/run", "python3", "-c",
        f"from schrodinger.structure import count_structures; print(count_structures(r'{file_path.resolve()}'))"
    ]
    try:
        res = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True, check=True)
        return int(res.stdout.strip())
    except Exception as e:
        logger.warning(f"Could not automatically count structures in {file_path.name}: {e}")
        return 0

======================================================================
FILE: bin/utils/data_extractors.py
======================================================================
"""
File: src/utils/data_extractors.py
Description: Parsers for extracting scoring metrics from CSV and SDF files.
"""
import csv
from pathlib import Path
import pandas as pd
from loguru import logger

def parse_glide_csv(csv_path: Path) -> dict:
    """Strict parser for Glide CSV tables, prioritizing docking_score with Epik penalties."""
    data = {}
    if not csv_path.exists(): return data
    with open(csv_path, "r", errors="ignore") as f:
        reader = csv.DictReader(f)
        for row in reader:
            title = row.get("title") or row.get("Title") or row.get("s_m_title")
            if not title or title in data: continue
            
            score = None
            # Strict match for docking score (with Epik penalties)
            for k in ["r_i_docking_score", "docking_score"]:
                if k in row and row[k]:
                    try: score = float(row[k]); break
                    except ValueError: pass
            # Fallback to gscore
            if score is None:
                for k in ["r_i_glide_gscore", "gscore"]:
                    if k in row and row[k]:
                        try: score = float(row[k]); break
                        except ValueError: pass
            if score is not None:
                data[title] = {"GlideScore": score}
    return data

def build_funnel_whitelist(dock_dir: Path, out_csv: Path, top_k_frames: int, percentile_cutoff: float, force_include: list = None) -> bool:
    """Aggregates Glide and GNINA Rescore data to triage candidates using consensus (Physics + AI)."""
    csv_files = list((dock_dir / "tables").glob("glide_dock_f*.csv"))
    if not csv_files:
        return False
        
    all_data = []
    for f in csv_files:
        frame_id = f.stem.split("_f")[-1]
        glide_data = parse_glide_csv(f)
        
        # Load GNINA rescore results if available
        resc_sdf = dock_dir / "export" / f"gnina_score_only_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf"
        resc_data = parse_gnina_sdf(resc_sdf, prefix="Resc_") if resc_sdf.exists() else {}

        for title, scores in glide_data.items():
            entry = {
                "Title": title, 
                "Frame": frame_id, 
                "GlideScore": scores["GlideScore"],
                "Resc_CNNaffinity": resc_data.get(title, {}).get("Resc_CNNaffinity", None)
            }
            all_data.append(entry)
            
    df = pd.DataFrame(all_data)
    if df.empty:
        return False
        
    # Calculate preliminary percentile ranks (0.0 to 1.0, higher is better)
    df["PR_Glide"] = df["GlideScore"].rank(ascending=False, pct=True)
    
    if df["Resc_CNNaffinity"].notna().any():
        df["PR_CNN"] = df["Resc_CNNaffinity"].rank(ascending=True, pct=True).fillna(df["PR_Glide"])
        # Preliminary Triage Score: 50% Physics (Glide) + 50% Deep Learning (GNINA)
        df["Triage_Score"] = (df["PR_Glide"] + df["PR_CNN"]) / 2.0
    else:
        df["Triage_Score"] = df["PR_Glide"]
        
    # Global best triage score per ligand across all frames
    best_triage = df.groupby("Title")["Triage_Score"].max()
    
    # Threshold cutoff (e.g. top 50% highest triage scores)
    threshold = best_triage.quantile(1.0 - percentile_cutoff)
    
    forced_set = set(force_include or [])
    passing_score = set(best_triage[best_triage >= threshold].index)
    kept_ligands = passing_score | (forced_set & set(df["Title"]))
    
    df_filtered = df[df["Title"].isin(kept_ligands)]
    
    # Pick Top K frames with the highest Triage Score for each ligand
    whitelist = df_filtered.sort_values("Triage_Score", ascending=False).groupby("Title").head(top_k_frames)
    
    whitelist[["Title", "Frame"]].to_csv(out_csv, index=False)
    logger.info(f"Screening Funnel (Glide + GNINA Triage): Retained {len(kept_ligands)}/{len(best_triage)} ligands. Generated {len(whitelist)} MM-GBSA tasks.")
    return True

def filter_sdf_by_whitelist(in_sdf: Path, out_sdf: Path, frame_id: str, whitelist_csv: Path):
    """Filters a multi-conformer SDF based on a funnel whitelist CSV."""
    if not in_sdf.exists() or not whitelist_csv.exists():
        return
        
    df_wl = pd.read_csv(whitelist_csv)
    # Get allowed titles for THIS specific frame
    allowed_titles = set(df_wl[df_wl["Frame"].astype(str) == str(frame_id)]["Title"])
    
    blocks = []
    with open(in_sdf, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
        content = f.read()
        
    for block in content.split("$$$$"):
        lines = block.strip().splitlines()
        if not lines: continue
        title = lines[0].strip()
        if title in allowed_titles:
            blocks.append(block.strip() + "\n$$$$\n")
            
    with open(out_sdf, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f_out:
        f_out.writelines(blocks)

def parse_prime_csv(csv_path: Path) -> dict:
    """Strict parser for Prime MM-GBSA CSV tables, ignoring (NS) and energy breakdown components."""
    data = {}
    if not csv_path.exists(): return data
    with open(csv_path, "r", errors="ignore") as f:
        reader = csv.DictReader(f)
        for row in reader:
            title = row.get("title") or row.get("Title") or row.get("s_m_title")
            if not title or title in data: continue
            props = {}
            
            # 1. Exact match for total dG Bind (strictly ignore (NS) and Coulomb/vdW parts)
            for k, v in row.items():
                if not k or not v: continue
                k_clean = k.strip()
                if k_clean == "r_psp_MMGBSA_dG_Bind" or k_clean == "MMGBSA dG Bind":
                    try: props["MMGBSA"] = float(v); break
                    except ValueError: pass
                    
            # 2. Match Ligand Strain
            for k, v in row.items():
                if not k or not v: continue
                if k.strip() == "r_psp_Lig_Strain_Energy" or k.strip() == "Lig Strain Energy":
                    try: props["Lig_Strain"] = float(v); break
                    except ValueError: pass
                    
            if props: data[title] = props
    return data

def _extract_sdf_block(filepath: Path, target_title: str) -> str:
    """Extracts a specific molecule's 3D SDF block as raw text."""
    if not filepath.exists(): return ""
    with open(filepath, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
        content = f.read()
    for block in content.split("$$$$"):
        lines = block.strip().splitlines()
        if not lines: continue
        title = lines[0].strip()
        if title == target_title:
            return block.strip() + "\n$$$$\n"
    return ""

def compute_pose_consensus_rmsd(df, cwd: Path):
    """Calculates in-place heavy-atom RMSD between Glide and independent GNINA dock poses."""
    from rdkit import Chem
    from rdkit.Chem import rdMolAlign
    import numpy as np
    
    dock_export = cwd / "04b_screening" / "export"
    rmsd_list = []
    
    for _, row in df.iterrows():
        if row.get("Is_Native", False):
            rmsd_list.append(np.nan)
            continue
            
        frame_id = str(row["Frame"]).replace("F", "")
        title = row["Title"]
        
        glide_sdf = dock_export / f"glide_dock_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf"
        gnina_sdf = dock_export / f"gnina_dock_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf"
        
        block_g = _extract_sdf_block(glide_sdf, title)
        block_n = _extract_sdf_block(gnina_sdf, title)
        
        if block_g and block_n:
            try:
                mg = Chem.RemoveHs(Chem.MolFromMolBlock(block_g))
                mn = Chem.RemoveHs(Chem.MolFromMolBlock(block_n))
                if mg and mn and mg.GetNumAtoms() == mn.GetNumAtoms():
                    val = rdMolAlign.GetBestRMS(mg, mn)
                    rmsd_list.append(round(val, 2))
                else:
                    rmsd_list.append(np.nan)
            except Exception:
                rmsd_list.append(np.nan)
        else:
            rmsd_list.append(np.nan)
            
    df["Pose_RMSD"] = rmsd_list
    return df

def parse_gnina_sdf(sdf_path: Path, prefix: str = "") -> dict:
    import re
    data = {}
    if not sdf_path or not sdf_path.exists(): return data
    with open(sdf_path, "r", errors="ignore") as f:
        content = f.read()
    for entry in content.split("$$$$"):
        lines = entry.strip().splitlines()
        if not lines: continue
        
        raw_title = lines[0].strip()
        prop_title = None
        props = {}
        
        for i, line in enumerate(lines):
            line_str = line.strip()
            if line_str.startswith("> <") and line_str.endswith(">"):
                tag_clean = line_str[3:-1].strip().lower()
                if i + 1 < len(lines):
                    val_str = lines[i + 1].strip()
                    if tag_clean in ["_name", "s_m_title", "title", "compound_id"]:
                        prop_title = val_str
                    else:
                        try:
                            val = float(val_str)
                            if tag_clean == "cnnscore": 
                                props[f"{prefix}CNNscore"] = val
                            elif tag_clean == "cnnaffinity": 
                                props[f"{prefix}CNNaffinity"] = val
                            elif tag_clean in ["minimizedaffinity", "affinity"]: 
                                props[f"{prefix}VinaAff"] = val
                        except ValueError: pass
        
        # Resolve title prioritizing property tags, then raw title
        title = prop_title or raw_title
        if not title: continue
        
        # Clean conformer suffixes like '_1', '_model_1', '_dock_1'
        title_clean = re.sub(r'(_model_\d+|_dock_\d+|_\d+)$', '', title).strip()
        
        # Preserve top-ranked pose data, prevent overwrite by lower-ranked poses
        if props:
            if title not in data:
                data[title] = props
            if title_clean and title_clean not in data:
                data[title_clean] = props
    return data

"""
File: src/utils/excel_export.py
Description: Generates an Excel spreadsheet with embedded 2D chemical structures,
formatted table headers, cell borders, and customized scientific metrics.
"""
import io
from pathlib import Path
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from loguru import logger
from rdkit import Chem
from rdkit.Chem import rdDepictor
from rdkit.Chem.Draw import rdMolDraw2D

try:
    import openpyxl
    from openpyxl.styles import Font, Alignment, PatternFill, Border, Side
    from openpyxl.drawing.image import Image as OpenpyxlImage
    from openpyxl.utils import get_column_letter
    OPENPYXL_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    OPENPYXL_AVAILABLE = False


def _render_mol_png(mol, width: int = 180, height: int = 130) -> io.BytesIO:
    """Renders an RDKit Mol to a high-contrast transparent PNG in memory."""
    if mol is None:
        return None
    try:
        m = Chem.Mol(mol)
        rdDepictor.Compute2DCoords(m)
        drawer = rdMolDraw2D.MolDraw2DCairo(width, height)
        opts = drawer.drawOptions()
        opts.clearBackground = True
        opts.bondLineWidth = 2
        drawer.DrawMolecule(m)
        drawer.FinishDrawing()
        png_bytes = drawer.GetDrawingText()
        return io.BytesIO(png_bytes)
    except Exception:
        # Fallback if Cairo is not available in current RDKit build
        try:
            from rdkit.Chem import Draw
            img = Draw.MolToImage(m, size=(width, height))
            bio = io.BytesIO()
            img.save(bio, format="PNG")
            bio.seek(0)
            return bio
        except Exception:
            return None


def export_hits_to_excel(df: pd.DataFrame, cwd: Path, out_dir: Path):
    """
    Exports consensus hitlist to Excel with embedded 2D structures.
    """
    if not OPENPYXL_AVAILABLE:
        logger.warning("openpyxl is not installed. Skipping Excel report generation. Run: pip install openpyxl pillow")
        return

    if df.empty:
        return

    from utils.html_export import _load_structures_for_hits
    mols_map = _load_structures_for_hits(df, cwd)

    wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
    ws = wb.active
    ws.title = "Top Candidates"
    ws.views.sheetView[0].showGridLines = True

    # 1. Заголовки таблицы
    headers = [
        "Структура",
        "Соединение",
        "Фрейм",
        "GlideScore, ккал/моль",
        "Prime MM-GBSA, ккал/моль",
        "CNNaffinity, pKd",
        "Итоговый балл",
        "RMSD (Glide/GNINA), Å"
    ]

    # Стили
    header_fill = PatternFill(start_color="1E293B", end_color="1E293B", fill_type="solid")  # Slate 800
    header_font = Font(name="Calibri", size=11, bold=True, color="FFFFFF")
    cell_font = Font(name="Calibri", size=11)
    bold_cell_font = Font(name="Calibri", size=11, bold=True)
    
    center_align = Alignment(horizontal="center", vertical="center", wrap_text=True)
    left_align = Alignment(horizontal="left", vertical="center")
    right_align = Alignment(horizontal="right", vertical="center")

    thin_border = Border(
        left=Side(style="thin", color="CBD5E1"),
        right=Side(style="thin", color="CBD5E1"),
        top=Side(style="thin", color="CBD5E1"),
        bottom=Side(style="thin", color="CBD5E1")
    )

    # Запись строки заголовков
    ws.append(headers)
    ws.row_dimensions[1].height = 28
    for col_idx in range(1, len(headers) + 1):
        cell = ws.cell(row=1, column=col_idx)
        cell.fill = header_fill
        cell.font = header_font
        cell.alignment = center_align
        cell.border = thin_border

    # 2. Заполнение данными
    img_width, img_height = 160, 110
    row_height = 90

    for row_idx, (_, row) in enumerate(df.iterrows(), start=2):
        ws.row_dimensions[row_idx].height = row_height

        raw_title = str(row.get("Title", ""))
        is_native = bool(row.get("Is_Native", False))
        display_title = "Native Reference" if is_native else raw_title

        frame = str(row.get("Frame", ""))
        
        # Скоррелированные метрики
        gscore = row.get("GlideScore")
        mmgbsa = row.get("MMGBSA")
        
        # Выбираем лучший доступный скор CNN (Rescore или Dock)
        cnn_aff = row.get("Resc_CNNaffinity")
        if pd.isna(cnn_aff):
            cnn_aff = row.get("Dock_CNNaffinity")

        ultimate = row.get("Ultimate_Score")
        p_rmsd = row.get("Pose_RMSD")

        # Добавляем пустую ячейку под картинку в кол. 1
        ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=1, value="")
        
        c2 = ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=2, value=display_title)
        c2.font = bold_cell_font if is_native else cell_font
        c2.alignment = left_align

        c3 = ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=3, value=frame)
        c3.alignment = center_align

        c4 = ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=4, value=round(float(gscore), 2) if pd.notna(gscore) else "—")
        c4.alignment = right_align

        c5 = ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=5, value=round(float(mmgbsa), 2) if pd.notna(mmgbsa) else "—")
        c5.alignment = right_align

        c6 = ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=6, value=round(float(cnn_aff), 2) if pd.notna(cnn_aff) else "—")
        c6.alignment = right_align

        c7 = ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=7, value=round(float(ultimate), 2) if pd.notna(ultimate) else "—")
        c7.font = bold_cell_font
        c7.alignment = right_align

        c8 = ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=8, value=round(float(p_rmsd), 2) if pd.notna(p_rmsd) else "—")
        c8.alignment = right_align

        # Стили границ
        for col_idx in range(1, len(headers) + 1):
            cell = ws.cell(row=row_idx, column=col_idx)
            cell.border = thin_border
            if is_native:
                cell.fill = PatternFill(start_color="F0FDF4", end_color="F0FDF4", fill_type="solid")  # Light green for native

        # Вставка картинки молекулы
        mol = mols_map.get(raw_title)
        if mol is not None:
            bio = _render_mol_png(mol, width=img_width, height=img_height)
            if bio is not None:
                img = OpenpyxlImage(bio)
                img.width = img_width
                img.height = img_height
                # Привязываем изображение к ячейке A{row_idx}
                cell_coord = f"A{row_idx}"
                ws.add_image(img, cell_coord)

    # 3. Автоширина колонок
    col_widths = {
        "A": 24,  # Структура
        "B": 28,  # Соединение
        "C": 12,  # Фрейм
        "D": 22,  # GlideScore
        "E": 24,  # MM-GBSA
        "F": 18,  # CNNaffinity
        "G": 16,  # Итоговый балл
        "H": 22   # RMSD
    }
    for col_letter, width in col_widths.items():
        ws.column_dimensions[col_letter].width = width

    excel_path = out_dir / "00_summary_table.xlsx"
    wb.save(excel_path)
    logger.success(f"Excel report with 2D structures generated: {excel_path.name}")

"""
File: src/utils/fs.py
Description: File system utilities, including robust numerical sorting for frames.
"""
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Tuple

def get_frames_sorted(directory: Path, pattern: str) -> List[Tuple[Path, str]]:
    """
    Finds files by pattern and sorts them numerically by frame ID '_f(\\d+)'.
    Returns a list of tuples: (Path, frame_id_as_string)
    """
    if not directory.exists():
        return []
    
    files = list(directory.glob(pattern))
    
    def extract_frame(p: Path) -> int:
        match = re.search(r'_f(\d+)', p.stem)
        return int(match.group(1)) if match else 0

    sorted_files = sorted(files, key=extract_frame)
    
    return [(f, str(extract_frame(f))) for f in sorted_files if extract_frame(f) > 0]

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
File: src/utils/gromacs_utils.py
Description: Safe topology and index manipulations for GROMACS.
Builds thermostat groups strictly by dictionary name lookup, NO index offsets.
"""
import os
import sys
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Set
from loguru import logger

def get_gmx_binary(config: dict = None) -> str:
    """
    Strictly validates and returns the system GROMACS binary 'gmx' from PATH.
    Terminates gracefully on failure without unhandled tracebacks.
    """
    if not shutil.which("gmx"):
        logger.critical("System GROMACS binary 'gmx' not found in PATH.")
        logger.error("Please ensure your newly compiled GROMACS is added to PATH (e.g., source GMXRC).")
        sys.exit(1)
    return "gmx"

def parse_ndx(ndx_path: Path) -> Dict[str, List[int]]:
    """Parses an NDX file into a dictionary of group_name -> list of atom IDs."""
    groups: Dict[str, List[int]] = {}
    current_group = None
    with open(ndx_path, "r") as f:
        for line in f:
            line = line.strip()
            if not line:
                continue
            if line.startswith("[") and line.endswith("]"):
                current_group = line[1:-1].strip()
                groups[current_group] = []
            elif current_group is not None:
                groups[current_group].extend([int(x) for x in line.split()])
    return groups

def write_ndx_group(f, name: str, atom_ids: List[int]):
    """Writes a single NDX group strictly formatted in columns of 15."""
    f.write(f"\n[ {name} ]\n")
    for i in range(0, len(atom_ids), 15):
        f.write(" ".join(f"{x:5d}" for x in atom_ids[i:i+15]) + "\n")

def add_thermostat_groups_by_name(ndx_path: Path):
    """
    Builds PROT_LIG and SOL_ION groups strictly by string matching names in index.ndx.
    NO numerical index offsets. Guaranteed partition of the entire System.
    """
    groups = parse_ndx(ndx_path)
    
    if "System" not in groups:
        raise KeyError("Group [ System ] not found in index.ndx")

    water_and_ion_atoms: Set[int] = set()
    ion_names = {"K+", "Cl-", "NA+", "CL-", "NA", "CL", "K", "POT", "CLA", "SOD", "MG", "ZN", "CA", "CAL"}
    
    # 1. Match water strictly by name
    if "SOL" in groups:
        water_and_ion_atoms.update(groups["SOL"])
    elif "Water" in groups:
        water_and_ion_atoms.update(groups["Water"])
    else:
        raise KeyError("Neither [ SOL ] nor [ Water ] found in index.ndx")

    # 2. Match ions strictly by name
    for ion_name in ion_names:
        if ion_name in groups:
            water_and_ion_atoms.update(groups[ion_name])

    # 3. Everything else in System is solute + membrane (Protein + UNK + Lipids)
    system_atoms = set(groups["System"])
    solute_membrane_atoms = system_atoms - water_and_ion_atoms

    # Strict partition validation
    if not solute_membrane_atoms:
        raise ValueError("PROT_LIG group is empty! Check index.ndx names.")
    if not water_and_ion_atoms:
        raise ValueError("SOL_ION group is empty! Check index.ndx names.")
    if len(solute_membrane_atoms) + len(water_and_ion_atoms) != len(system_atoms):
        raise ValueError("Partition mismatch: PROT_LIG + SOL_ION != System!")

    # Append new groups strictly formatted
    with open(ndx_path, "a") as f:
        write_ndx_group(f, "PROT_LIG", sorted(list(solute_membrane_atoms)))
        write_ndx_group(f, "SOL_ION", sorted(list(water_and_ion_atoms)))

def inject_ligand_posre(top_file: Path):
    """Injects position restraints with multiple force constants at the end of UNK moleculetype."""
    if not top_file.exists(): return
    lines = top_file.read_text().splitlines()
    
    out_lines = []
    in_unk = False
    injected = False
    
    posre_block = (
        "\n#ifdef POSRES_LIG\n"
        "#include \"posre_lig.itp\"\n"
        "#endif\n"
        "#ifdef POSRES_LIG_250\n"
        "#include \"posre_lig_250.itp\"\n"
        "#endif\n"
        "#ifdef POSRES_LIG_50\n"
        "#include \"posre_lig_50.itp\"\n"
        "#endif\n"
    )
    
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        
        if in_unk and not injected and (stripped.startswith("[ moleculetype ]") or stripped.startswith("[ system ]")):
            out_lines.append(posre_block)
            injected = True
            in_unk = False
            
        if stripped.startswith("[ moleculetype ]"):
            in_unk = False
            
        if not stripped.startswith(";") and stripped.startswith("UNK") and len(stripped.split()) >= 2:
            in_unk = True
            
        out_lines.append(line)

    if in_unk and not injected:
        out_lines.append(posre_block)
        
    # Inject protein multi-level posres at the end of system1 or Protein
    out_lines_prot = []
    in_prot = False
    injected_prot = False
    
    posre_block_prot = (
        "\n#ifdef POSRES\n"
        "#include \"posre.itp\"\n"
        "#endif\n"
        "#ifdef POSRES_250\n"
        "#include \"posre_250.itp\"\n"
        "#endif\n"
        "#ifdef POSRES_50\n"
        "#include \"posre_50.itp\"\n"
        "#endif\n"
    )
    
    for line in out_lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        
        if in_prot and not injected_prot and (stripped.startswith("[ moleculetype ]") or stripped.startswith("[ system ]")):
            out_lines_prot.append(posre_block_prot)
            injected_prot = True
            in_prot = False
            
        if stripped.startswith("[ moleculetype ]"):
            in_prot = False
            
        if not stripped.startswith(";") and (stripped.startswith("system1") or stripped.startswith("Protein")) and len(stripped.split()) >= 2:
            in_prot = True
            
        out_lines_prot.append(line)

    top_file.write_text("\n".join(out_lines_prot) + "\n")

def fix_posre_indices(itp_file: Path):
    """Converts global atom indices in posre_lig.itp to 1-based local molecule indices (1 to N)."""
    if not itp_file.exists(): return
    lines = itp_file.read_text().splitlines()
    out_lines = []
    atom_idx = 1
    for line in lines:
        stripped = line.strip()
        if not stripped or stripped.startswith(";") or stripped.startswith("["):
            out_lines.append(line)
            continue
        parts = stripped.split()
        if len(parts) >= 5:
            out_lines.append(f"{atom_idx:5d}{int(parts[1]):5d}{float(parts[2]):10.1f}{float(parts[3]):10.1f}{float(parts[4]):10.1f}")
            atom_idx += 1
        else:
            out_lines.append(line)
    itp_file.write_text("\n".join(out_lines) + "\n")

FILE: bin/utils/html_export.py
