"""
File: src/utils/html_export.py
Description: Generates standalone HTML report with 2D structures using RDKit and templates.
"""
import os
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path
from loguru import logger
from rdkit import Chem, RDLogger
from rdkit.Chem import rdDepictor
from rdkit.Chem.Draw import rdMolDraw2D

# Suppress internal C++ parser warnings from RDKit
RDLogger.DisableLog('rdApp.*')

def _mol_to_svg(mol, width=180, height=130) -> str:
    if mol is None: return '<div style="color:#aaa;font-size:11px;text-align:center;">No 2D Structure</div>'
    try:
        m = Chem.Mol(mol)
        rdDepictor.Compute2DCoords(m)
        drawer = rdMolDraw2D.MolDraw2DSVG(width, height)
        opts = drawer.drawOptions()
        opts.clearBackground = True
        opts.bondLineWidth = 2
        drawer.DrawMolecule(m)
        drawer.FinishDrawing()
        svg = drawer.GetDrawingText()
        if "<?xml" in svg: svg = svg[svg.find("<svg"):]
        return svg
    except Exception:
        return '<div style="color:#e74c3c;font-size:11px;text-align:center;">Render Error</div>'

def _load_structures_for_hits(df_hits: pd.DataFrame, cwd: Path) -> dict:
    mols = {}
    needed = df_hits[["Title", "Frame", "Is_Native"]].to_dict("records")
    grouped = {}
    for item in needed:
        key = (item["Is_Native"], item["Frame"].replace("F", ""))
        grouped.setdefault(key, set()).add(item["Title"])
        
    for (is_native, frame_id), titles in grouped.items():
        if is_native:
            candidates = [
                cwd / "01_prep_receptor" / "export" / f"ref_native_f{frame_id}.sdf",
                cwd / "02_prep_ligands" / "export" / "native_prepared.sdf"
            ]
        else:
            dock_export = cwd / "04b_screening" / "export"
            candidates = [
                dock_export / f"glide_dock_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
                dock_export / f"gnina_dock_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
                dock_export / f"gnina_score_only_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
                dock_export / f"gnina_minimize_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
                cwd / "02_prep_ligands" / "export" / "library_prepared.sdf"
            ]
            
        found_titles = set()
        for sdf_path in candidates:
            if not sdf_path.exists(): continue
            suppl = Chem.SDMolSupplier(str(sdf_path), removeHs=True)
            for mol in suppl:
                if mol is None: continue
                name = mol.GetProp("_Name") if mol.HasProp("_Name") else mol.GetProp("s_m_title") if mol.HasProp("s_m_title") else ""
                if name in titles and name not in mols:
                    mols[name] = mol
                    found_titles.add(name)
            if titles.issubset(found_titles): break
    return mols

def generate_html_report(df: pd.DataFrame, cwd: Path, out_dir: Path):
    if df.empty: return
    
    mols_map = _load_structures_for_hits(df, cwd)
    fdir_env = os.environ.get("SBDD_FRAMEWORK_DIR")
    framework_dir = Path(fdir_env) if fdir_env else Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    template_path = framework_dir / "templates" / "report_template.html"
    
    if not template_path.exists():
        logger.error(f"HTML Template missing at {template_path}. Report skipped.")
        return
        
    rows_html = []
    for rank, (_, row) in enumerate(df.iterrows(), start=1):
        raw_title = row["Title"]
        is_native = row.get("Is_Native", False)
        title = "Native Reference" if is_native else raw_title
        svg_code = _mol_to_svg(mols_map.get(raw_title))
        
        is_bench = row.get("Is_Benchmark", False)
        row_class = "native-row" if is_native else "control-row" if is_bench else ""
        badge = '<span class="badge-native">REF</span>' if is_native else '<span class="badge-control">CTRL</span>' if is_bench else f'<span class="badge-rank">#{rank}</span>'
        
        def fmt(val, dec=2): return f"{val:.{dec}f}" if pd.notna(val) else '<span class="na">—</span>'
        
        # Format Delta to Native
        delta_val = row.get("Δ_to_Native", np.nan)
        delta_class = "delta-pos" if pd.notna(delta_val) and delta_val > 0 else "delta-neutral"
        delta_str = f"+{delta_val:.2f}" if pd.notna(delta_val) and delta_val > 0 else fmt(delta_val)
        
        # Format Pose Consensus RMSD (Glide vs GNINA dock)
        p_rmsd = row.get("Pose_RMSD", np.nan)
        if pd.isna(p_rmsd):
            rmsd_badge = '<span class="na">—</span>'
        elif p_rmsd <= 2.0:
            rmsd_badge = f'<span class="col-num" style="color:#16a34a; font-weight:600;">{p_rmsd:.2f} Å</span>'
        else:
            rmsd_badge = f'<span class="col-num">{p_rmsd:.2f} Å</span>'

        # Color coding for internal strain (torsional deformation)
        strain_val = row.get("Lig_Strain", np.nan)
        if pd.isna(strain_val):
            strain_style = "color:#64748b;"
        elif strain_val <= 4.0:
            strain_style = "color:#16a34a; font-weight:600;"  # Low/Good strain (Green)
        elif strain_val <= 8.0:
            strain_style = "color:#d97706; font-weight:600;"  # Moderate strain (Amber)
        else:
            strain_style = "color:#dc2626; font-weight:700;"  # High strain (Red)
            
        # Extract best available CNN pose quality score (0.0 to 1.0)
        cnn_score = row.get("Resc_CNNscore") if pd.notna(row.get("Resc_CNNscore")) else row.get("Dock_CNNscore")

        rows_html.append(f"""
        <tr class="{row_class}">
            <td class="col-rank">{badge}</td>
            <td class="col-mol">{svg_code}</td>
            <td class="col-title"><strong>{title}</strong></td>
            <td class="col-frame"><span class="frame-tag">{row['Frame']}</span></td>
            <td class="col-score"><strong>{fmt(row.get('Ultimate_Score'))}</strong></td>
            <td class="col-delta {delta_class}">{delta_str}</td>
            <td>{rmsd_badge}</td>
            <td>{fmt(row.get('PR_Glide'))}</td>
            <td>{fmt(row.get('PR_MMGBSA'))}</td>
            <td>{fmt(row.get('PR_CNN_Resc'))}</td>
            <td>{fmt(row.get('PR_GNINA_Dock'))}</td>
            <td class="col-num">{fmt(row.get('GlideScore'))}</td>
            <td class="col-num">{fmt(row.get('MMGBSA'))}</td>
            <td class="col-num" style="{strain_style}">{fmt(strain_val)}</td>
            <td class="col-num">{fmt(row.get('Resc_CNNaffinity'))}</td>
            <td class="col-num">{fmt(row.get('Dock_CNNaffinity'))}</td>
            <td class="col-num">{fmt(cnn_score)}</td>
        </tr>
        """)

    with open(template_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        html_template = f.read()
        
    html_content = html_template.replace("{table_body}", "\n".join(rows_html))
    
    out_file = out_dir / "00_summary_report.html"
    with open(out_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(html_content)
        
    logger.success(f"Visual HTML report saved to: {out_file.name}")

======================================================================
FILE: bin/utils/lid_export.py
======================================================================
"""
File: src/utils/lid_export.py
Description: Orchestrates 2D Ligand Interaction Diagram (LID) generation for
the Top-N screened hits, native reference ligand, and benchmark controls.
"""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
import pandas as pd
from loguru import logger

from utils.cmd_runner import run_command


def _resolve_receptor_file(cwd: Path, frame_id: str) -> Optional[Path]:
    """Finds the prepared receptor structure for a given frame."""
    candidates = [
        cwd / "01_prep_receptor" / "structures" / f"receptor_prepared_f{frame_id}.mae",
        cwd / "01_prep_receptor" / "raw" / f"receptor_raw_f{frame_id}.mae"
    ]
    for c in candidates:
        if c.exists():
            return c
    return None


def _resolve_ligand_file(cwd: Path, frame_id: str, is_native: bool) -> Optional[Path]:
    """Locates the 3D docked pose or crystal reference file for a ligand."""
    if is_native:
        candidates = [
            cwd / "04a_redocking" / "poses" / f"glide_dock_f{frame_id}_pv.maegz",
            cwd / "01_prep_receptor" / "structures" / f"ref_native_f{frame_id}.mae",
            cwd / "04a_redocking" / "export" / f"glide_dock_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
            cwd / "04a_redocking" / "poses" / f"gnina_dock_f{frame_id}.sdf"
        ]
    else:
        candidates = [
            cwd / "04b_screening" / "poses" / f"glide_dock_f{frame_id}_pv.maegz",
            cwd / "04b_screening" / "export" / f"glide_dock_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
            cwd / "04b_screening" / "export" / f"gnina_minimize_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
            cwd / "04b_screening" / "export" / f"gnina_score_only_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
            cwd / "04b_screening" / "export" / f"gnina_dock_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
            cwd / "04b_screening" / "poses" / f"gnina_dock_f{frame_id}.sdf"
        ]

    for c in candidates:
        if c.exists():
            return c
    return None


def generate_lid_diagrams(df_resolved: pd.DataFrame, cwd: Path, out_dir: Path, top_n: int = 20, force: bool = False):
    """
    Builds task manifest and executes Schrodinger worker to generate 2D LIDs.
    Selects Top-N library hits, all benchmark controls, and native reference.
    """
    schrodinger_path = os.environ.get("SCHRODINGER")
    if not schrodinger_path:
        logger.warning("SCHRODINGER environment variable is not set. Skipping LID generation.")
        return

    fdir_env = os.environ.get("SBDD_FRAMEWORK_DIR")
    if fdir_env:
        worker_script = Path(fdir_env) / "bin" / "generate_lids.py"
        if not worker_script.exists():
            worker_script = Path(fdir_env) / "src" / "schrodinger_workers" / "generate_lids.py"
    else:
        worker_script = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "generate_lids.py"

    if not worker_script.exists():
        logger.error(f"LID worker script not found at {worker_script}")
        return

    lids_dir = out_dir / "lids"
    lids_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    manifest_path = lids_dir / "lid_manifest.json"

    # Select candidates: Native + Controls + Top-N Library Hits
    native_df = df_resolved[df_resolved["Is_Native"] == True]
    bench_df = df_resolved[df_resolved.get("Is_Benchmark", False) == True]
    library_top = df_resolved[(df_resolved["Is_Native"] == False) & (~df_resolved["Title"].isin(bench_df["Title"]))].head(top_n)

    target_df = pd.concat([native_df, bench_df, library_top], ignore_index=True).drop_duplicates(subset="Title")

    if target_df.empty:
        logger.warning("No targets available for 2D LID generation.")
        return

    manifest_tasks: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    # Map rank among library hits for standardized naming
    lib_titles_ranked = df_resolved[(df_resolved["Is_Native"] == False) & (~df_resolved["Title"].isin(bench_df["Title"]))]["Title"].tolist()

    for _, row in target_df.iterrows():
        title = str(row["Title"])
        frame_id = str(row["Frame"]).replace("F", "")
        is_native = bool(row.get("Is_Native", False))
        is_bench = bool(row.get("Is_Benchmark", False))

        rec_file = _resolve_receptor_file(cwd, frame_id)
        lig_file = _resolve_ligand_file(cwd, frame_id, is_native)

        if not rec_file or not lig_file:
            logger.warning(f"Could not resolve 3D structure files for '{title}' (Frame {frame_id}). Skipping LID.")
            continue

        safe_title = "".join(c if c.isalnum() or c in "-_" else "_" for c in title)

        if is_native:
            png_name = f"native_ref_f{frame_id}_2d_lid.png"
        elif is_bench:
            png_name = f"control_{safe_title}_f{frame_id}_2d_lid.png"
        else:
            rank_num = lib_titles_ranked.index(title) + 1 if title in lib_titles_ranked else 99
            png_name = f"Rank_{rank_num:02d}_{safe_title}_f{frame_id}_2d_lid.png"

        out_png = lids_dir / png_name

        manifest_tasks.append({
            "title": title,
            "frame": frame_id,
            "is_native": is_native,
            "receptor_file": str(rec_file.resolve()),
            "ligand_file": str(lig_file.resolve()),
            "out_png": str(out_png.resolve())
        })

    if not manifest_tasks:
        logger.error("No valid LID tasks generated.")
        return

    # Checkpoint validation
    all_pngs_exist = all(Path(t["out_png"]).exists() for t in manifest_tasks)
    if all_pngs_exist and not force:
        logger.info(f"All {len(manifest_tasks)} 2D LID diagrams already exist in {lids_dir.name}/. Skipping. Use --force to override.")
        return

    with open(manifest_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(manifest_tasks, f, indent=2)

    logger.info(f"Generating 2D Ligand Interaction Diagrams for {len(manifest_tasks)} compound(s)...")

    cmd = [
        f"{schrodinger_path}/run", "python3", str(worker_script),
        "--manifest", str(manifest_path.resolve()),
        "--out_dir", str(lids_dir.resolve())
    ]

    success = run_command(cmd, out_dir, "05_generate_lids")

    if success:
        generated_count = len(list(lids_dir.glob("*.png")))
        logger.success(f"2D Interaction Diagrams complete! Saved {generated_count} image(s) to: {lids_dir.name}/")
    else:
        logger.error("LID worker execution encountered errors. Inspect logs/05_generate_lids.log.")

======================================================================
FILE: bin/utils/md_metrics.py
======================================================================
"""
File: src/utils/md_metrics.py
Description: Clean mathematical extractors for MD metrics and statistical inefficiency.
"""
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import MDAnalysis as mda
from MDAnalysis.analysis import align, rms
from MDAnalysis.lib.distances import capped_distance
from loguru import logger


def compute_rmsd_rmsf(pdb_path: str, xtc_path: str) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
    """Calculates Protein Backbone RMSD, Ligand Heavy RMSD (drift), and Protein C-alpha RMSF."""
    u = mda.Universe(pdb_path, xtc_path)
    ref = mda.Universe(pdb_path)
    
    prot_bb = u.select_atoms("protein and backbone")
    ref_bb = ref.select_atoms("protein and backbone")
    lig_heavy = u.select_atoms("resname UNK and not name H*")
    ref_lig = ref.select_atoms("resname UNK and not name H*")
    
    rmsd_results = []
    for ts in u.trajectory:
        # 1. Align the entire system based on the protein backbone
        align.alignto(u.atoms, ref.atoms, select="protein and backbone")
        time_ns = ts.time / 1000.0
        
        # 2. Calculate RMSD. Ligand RMSD tracks physical unbinding (drift)
        prot_rmsd = rms.rmsd(prot_bb.positions, ref_bb.positions)
        lig_rmsd = rms.rmsd(lig_heavy.positions, ref_lig.positions) if lig_heavy.n_atoms > 0 else np.nan
        
        rmsd_results.append({"Time_ns": time_ns, "Prot_BB_RMSD": prot_rmsd, "Lig_Heavy_RMSD": lig_rmsd})
        
    df_rmsd = pd.DataFrame(rmsd_results)
    
    calphas = u.select_atoms("protein and name CA")
    R = rms.RMSF(calphas).run()
    
    df_rmsf = pd.DataFrame({
        "ResID": calphas.resids,
        "ResName": calphas.resnames,
        "RMSF": R.results.rmsf
    })
    
    return df_rmsd, df_rmsf

def compute_contacts(pdb_path: str, xtc_path: str, cutoffs: dict) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Computes dynamic contacts occupancy (Total, Hydrophobic, Polar)."""
    u = mda.Universe(pdb_path, xtc_path)
    lig = u.select_atoms("resname UNK")
    prot = u.select_atoms("protein")
    
    if lig.n_atoms == 0 or prot.n_atoms == 0:
        return pd.DataFrame()
        
    l_heavy, p_heavy = lig.select_atoms("not name H*"), prot.select_atoms("not name H*")
    l_c, p_c = lig.select_atoms("name C*"), prot.select_atoms("name C*")
    l_pol = lig.select_atoms("name N* or name O* or name S* or name P*")
    p_pol = prot.select_atoms("name N* or name O* or name S* or name P*")
    
    unique_resids = np.unique(p_heavy.resids)
    counts = {r: {"Total": 0, "Hydrophobic": 0, "Polar": 0} for r in unique_resids}
    n_frames = len(u.trajectory)
    
    c_heavy, c_hyd, c_pol = cutoffs.get("heavy", 3.5), cutoffs.get("hydrophobic", 4.0), cutoffs.get("polar", 3.4)
    
    for _ in u.trajectory:
        # Total heavy contacts
        pairs_tot = capped_distance(l_heavy.positions, p_heavy.positions, max_cutoff=c_heavy, return_distances=False)
        if len(pairs_tot) > 0:
            for r in np.unique(p_heavy.resids[pairs_tot[:, 1]]): counts[r]["Total"] += 1
                
        # Hydrophobic (C-C)
        if l_c.n_atoms > 0 and p_c.n_atoms > 0:
            pairs_hyd = capped_distance(l_c.positions, p_c.positions, max_cutoff=c_hyd, return_distances=False)
            if len(pairs_hyd) > 0:
                for r in np.unique(p_c.resids[pairs_hyd[:, 1]]): counts[r]["Hydrophobic"] += 1
                    
        # Polar (N/O/S/P)
        if l_pol.n_atoms > 0 and p_pol.n_atoms > 0:
            pairs_pol = capped_distance(l_pol.positions, p_pol.positions, max_cutoff=c_pol, return_distances=False)
            if len(pairs_pol) > 0:
                for r in np.unique(p_pol.resids[pairs_pol[:, 1]]): counts[r]["Polar"] += 1

    results = []
    for r, c in counts.items():
        occ_tot = (c["Total"]/n_frames)*100.0
        if occ_tot > 1.0:
            results.append({
                "ResID": r, "ResName": prot.select_atoms(f"resid {r}").resnames[0],
                "Total_%": occ_tot, "Hydrophobic_%": (c["Hydrophobic"]/n_frames)*100.0,
                "Polar_%": (c["Polar"]/n_frames)*100.0
            })
            
    return pd.DataFrame(results).sort_values("Total_%", ascending=False)

======================================================================
FILE: bin/utils/merge_complex_parmed.py
======================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
File: src/utils/merge_complex_parmed.py
Description: Safely merges Protein PDB and Ligand MOL2 using ParmEd.
Converts ResidueTemplate to Structure if necessary.
"""
import sys
import parmed as pmd
from loguru import logger

def merge_complex(prot_pdb: str, lig_mol2: str, out_pdb: str):
    logger.info(f"Merging {prot_pdb} and {lig_mol2}...")
    prot = pmd.load_file(prot_pdb)
    lig = pmd.load_file(lig_mol2)

    # Convert ResidueTemplate to Structure to expose .residues
    if hasattr(lig, "to_structure"):
        lig = lig.to_structure()

    # Find a free chain ID for the ligand
    used_chains = {res.chain for res in prot.residues}
    lig_chain = next((c for c in "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ" if c not in used_chains), "Z")

    for res in lig.residues:
        res.chain = lig_chain
        res.name = "UNK"

    complex_struct = prot + lig
    complex_struct.save(out_pdb, overwrite=True)
    logger.info(f"Complex saved to {out_pdb} (Ligand assigned to chain {lig_chain})")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    merge_complex(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3])

FILE: bin/utils/pymol_export.py

# FILE: src/utils/pymol_export.py
"""
File: src/utils/pymol_export.py
Description: Generates PyMOL sessions with 3.5 A pocket residues and Test #4 H-bonds.
"""
import re
import shutil
import pandas as pd
from pathlib import Path
from loguru import logger
from rdkit import Chem


def _find_3d_mol(cwd: Path, title: str, frame_id: str, is_native: bool):
    """Loads exact 3D RDKit Mol object keeping coordinates and hydrogens."""
    if is_native:
        candidates = [
            cwd / "01_prep_receptor" / "export" / f"ref_native_f{frame_id}.sdf",
            cwd / "04a_redocking" / "export" / f"glide_dock_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
            cwd / "04a_redocking" / "export" / f"gnina_dock_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
            cwd / "02_prep_ligands" / "export" / "native_prepared.sdf"
        ]
    else:
        dock_export = cwd / "04b_screening" / "export"
        candidates = [
            dock_export / f"glide_dock_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
            dock_export / f"gnina_dock_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
            dock_export / f"gnina_minimize_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
            dock_export / f"gnina_score_only_f{frame_id}_poses.sdf",
            cwd / "02_prep_ligands" / "export" / "library_prepared.sdf"
        ]

    clean_target = re.sub(r'(_model_\d+|_dock_\d+|_\d+)$', '', title).strip()

    for cand in candidates:
        if not cand.exists():
            continue
        try:
            suppl = Chem.SDMolSupplier(str(cand), removeHs=False, sanitize=False)
            for mol in suppl:
                if mol is None:
                    continue
                name = mol.GetProp("_Name") if mol.HasProp("_Name") else (
                    mol.GetProp("s_m_title") if mol.HasProp("s_m_title") else ""
                )
                clean_name = re.sub(r'(_model_\d+|_dock_\d+|_\d+)$', '', name).strip()
                if name == title or clean_name == clean_target or name == clean_target:
                    return mol
            if is_native and "ref_native" in cand.name:
                suppl = Chem.SDMolSupplier(str(cand), removeHs=False, sanitize=False)
                for mol in suppl:
                    if mol is not None:
                        return mol
        except Exception:
            continue
    return None


def generate_pymol_session(df: pd.DataFrame, cwd: Path, out_dir: Path):
    if df.empty:
        return

    pymol_dir = out_dir / "pymol_session"
    pymol_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    pml_file = pymol_dir / "view_hits.pml"

    hit_items = []
    first_focus = None

    with open(pml_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as pml:
        # 1. Global Viewport & Rendering Settings
        pml.write("# PyMOL SBDD Hit Session\n")
        pml.write("set bg_rgb, white\n")
        pml.write("set ray_opaque_background, on\n")
        pml.write("set defer_builds_mode, 3\n")
        
        # Fancy Helices
        pml.write("set cartoon_fancy_helices, 1\n")
        pml.write("set cartoon_highlight_color, grey90\n")
        pml.write("set cartoon_flat_sheets, 1\n")
        pml.write("set cartoon_smooth_loops, 1\n")
        pml.write("set cartoon_side_chain_helper, on\n")
        pml.write("set stick_radius, 0.22\n")
        pml.write("set valence, 1\n")
        pml.write("set auto_show_spheres, off\n")

        # Labels (Strictly in front)
        pml.write("set float_labels, on\n")
        pml.write("set label_position, [0.0, 0.0, 5.0]\n")
        pml.write("set label_color, black\n")
        pml.write("set label_outline_color, none\n")
        pml.write("set label_size, 18\n")
        pml.write("set label_font_id, 7\n")

        # H-Bond Dashes Styling
        pml.write("set dash_color, marine\n")
        pml.write("set dash_gap, 0.20\n")
        pml.write("set dash_width, 3.0\n")
        pml.write("set dash_radius, 0.06\n\n")

        # 2. Iterate hits
        for _, row in df.iterrows():
            raw_title = str(row["Title"])
            frame_id = str(row["Frame"]).replace("F", "")
            is_native = bool(row.get("Is_Native", False))
            is_bench = bool(row.get("Is_Benchmark", False))

            safe_code = re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9_]', '_', raw_title)

            if is_native:
                code_name = f"REF_Native_F{frame_id}"
            elif is_bench:
                code_name = f"CTRL_{safe_code}_F{frame_id}"
            else:
                code_name = f"{safe_code}_F{frame_id}"

            grp_name = code_name
            rec_obj = f"rec_{code_name}"
            lig_obj = f"lig_{code_name}"
            ref_obj = f"native_{code_name}"
            hb_obj = f"hb_{code_name}"

            if first_focus is None and not is_native:
                first_focus = lig_obj
            elif first_focus is None and is_native:
                first_focus = ref_obj

            # Copy receptor PDB
            src_rec = cwd / "01_prep_receptor" / "export" / f"receptor_prepared_f{frame_id}.pdb"
            dst_rec = pymol_dir / f"{rec_obj}.pdb"
            if src_rec.exists() and not dst_rec.exists():
                shutil.copy(src_rec, dst_rec)

            # Copy native reference SDF
            src_ref = cwd / "01_prep_receptor" / "export" / f"ref_native_f{frame_id}.sdf"
            dst_ref = pymol_dir / f"{ref_obj}.sdf"
            if src_ref.exists() and not dst_ref.exists():
                shutil.copy(src_ref, dst_ref)

            # Write docked ligand pose SDF
            dst_lig = pymol_dir / f"{lig_obj}.sdf"
            mol = _find_3d_mol(cwd, raw_title, frame_id, is_native)
            if mol is not None:
                writer = Chem.SDWriter(str(dst_lig))
                writer.write(mol)
                writer.close()

            # Load objects into PyMOL
            pml.write(f"# --- Load {code_name} ---\n")
            loaded_objs = []

            if dst_rec.exists():
                pml.write(f"load {dst_rec.name}, {rec_obj}\n")
                loaded_objs.append(rec_obj)

            if dst_ref.exists():
                pml.write(f"load {dst_ref.name}, {ref_obj}\n")
                loaded_objs.append(ref_obj)

            if dst_lig.exists():
                pml.write(f"load {dst_lig.name}, {lig_obj}\n")
                loaded_objs.append(lig_obj)

            active_lig = lig_obj if dst_lig.exists() else ref_obj

            if loaded_objs:
                hit_items.append((grp_name, rec_obj, active_lig, ref_obj, hb_obj, loaded_objs))

        # 3. Base Clean Styling (Global)
        pml.write("\n# --- Base Styling ---\n")
        pml.write("hide everything\n")
        pml.write("show cartoon, rec_*\n")
        pml.write("color gray85, rec_*\n")
        pml.write("color gray85, rec_* and elem c\n\n")

        pml.write("show sticks, lig_*\n")
        pml.write("color cyan, lig_* and elem c\n\n")

        pml.write("show sticks, native_*\n")
        pml.write("color forest, native_* and elem c\n\n")

        # 4. Pocket 3.5 A Residues + Method 4 H-Bonds
        pml.write("# --- Pocket Contacts (3.5 A) & Method 4 H-Bonds ---\n")
        for grp_name, rec_obj, active_lig, ref_obj, hb_obj, loaded_objs in hit_items:
            sel_pocket = f"p_{grp_name}"
            
            # Simple direct 3.5 A shell selection
            pml.write(f"select {sel_pocket}, (byres ({rec_obj} within 3.5 of {active_lig}))\n")
            pml.write(f"show sticks, ({sel_pocket} and not name c+n+o)\n")
            pml.write(f"set stick_radius, 0.16, {sel_pocket}\n")
            pml.write(f"color gray70, {sel_pocket} and elem c\n")
            pml.write(f'label ({sel_pocket} and (name CB or (name CA and resn GLY))), "%s%s" % (resn, resi)\n')

            # Method 4 H-Bonds
            pml.write(f"dist {hb_obj}, ({active_lig} and elem n+o+s+f+cl+br), ({rec_obj} and elem n+o+s), 3.5\n")
            pml.write(f"show dashes, {hb_obj}\n")
            pml.write(f"hide labels, {hb_obj}\n")

            # Group everything into compound name
            group_members = [o for o in loaded_objs] + [hb_obj]
            pml.write(f"group {grp_name}, {' '.join(group_members)}\n\n")

        pml.write("delete p_*\n")
        pml.write("deselect\n\n")

        # 5. Viewport Focus
        pml.write("# --- Initial Camera View ---\n")
        pml.write("disable all\n")
        if hit_items:
            pml.write(f"enable {hit_items[0][0]}\n")
            target = first_focus if first_focus else hit_items[0][0]
            pml.write(f"zoom {target}, 5\n")

    logger.success(f"PyMOL Session cleanly exported to: {pml_file.absolute()}")

"""
File: src/utils/schrodinger.py
Description: Wrappers for Schrodinger Python API workers to avoid code duplication.
"""
import os
import csv
from pathlib import Path
from loguru import logger
from utils.cmd_runner import run_command

def run_rmsd_worker(ref_ligand: Path, poses_file: Path, out_csv: Path, work_dir: Path) -> tuple:
    """
    Invokes the run_rmsd.py worker and parses the best pose result.
    Returns (best_rmsd, best_score) or (None, None).
    """
    schrodinger_path = os.environ.get("SCHRODINGER")
    framework_dir = Path(os.environ.get("SBDD_FRAMEWORK_DIR"))
    worker_script = framework_dir / "src" / "schrodinger_workers" / "run_rmsd.py"
    
    cmd_rmsd = [
        f"{schrodinger_path}/run", "python3", str(worker_script),
        "--reference", str(ref_ligand.resolve()),
        "--poses", str(poses_file.resolve()),
        "--output", str(out_csv.resolve())
    ]
    
    run_command(cmd_rmsd, work_dir, f"rmsd_{poses_file.stem}")
    
    if out_csv.exists():
        with open(out_csv, "r") as f_rmsd:
            rows = list(csv.DictReader(f_rmsd))
        if rows:
            best_pose = min(rows, key=lambda r: float(r["RMSD_Heavy"]))
            score_val = float(best_pose.get("Score", 0.0) or best_pose.get("GlideScore", 0.0) or 0.0)
            rmsd_val = float(best_pose["RMSD_Heavy"])
            return rmsd_val, score_val
            
    return None, None

======================================================================
FILE: bin/utils/topology_prep.py
======================================================================
"""
File: src/utils/topology_prep.py
Description: Cleaned and refactored logic for PDB merging and tLEaP bond detection.
Replaces the legacy 'combine_to_complex.py' and 'detect_bonds.py'.
"""
import math
from pathlib import Path

def merge_protein_ligand(prot_pdb: Path, lig_mol2: Path, out_pdb: Path) -> int:
    """
    Merges AMBER-prepped protein PDB with ligand Mol2.
    Returns the number of ligand atoms inserted.
    """
    lines = []
    last_serial = 0
    used_chains = set()

    with open(prot_pdb, "r") as f:
        for line in f:
            rec = line[0:6]
            if rec.startswith(("CONECT", "MASTER", "END")):
                continue
            if rec.startswith(("ATOM", "HETATM")):
                try:
                    s = int(line[6:11])
                    if s > last_serial: last_serial = s
                except ValueError: pass
                if len(line) >= 22 and line[21].strip():
                    used_chains.add(line[21])
            lines.append(line)

    ligand_chain = next((c for c in "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ" if c not in used_chains), "Z")
    lig_atoms = []

    with open(lig_mol2, "r") as f:
        in_atom = False
        for line in f:
            line = line.strip()
            if line.startswith("@<TRIPOS>ATOM"):
                in_atom = True
                continue
            if line.startswith("@<TRIPOS>") and not line.startswith("@<TRIPOS>ATOM"):
                in_atom = False
                continue
            if in_atom and line:
                parts = line.split()
                if len(parts) >= 6:
                    elem = parts[5].split(".")[0][:2].capitalize() if "." in parts[5] else parts[5][0].upper()
                    lig_atoms.append({
                        "name": parts[1][:4], "x": float(parts[2]), "y": float(parts[3]), 
                        "z": float(parts[4]), "elem": elem
                    })

    serial = last_serial
    ligand_lines = []
    for a in lig_atoms:
        serial += 1
        line = (
            f"HETATM{serial:>5} {a['name']:>4} UNK {ligand_chain}   1       "
            f"{a['x']:8.3f}{a['y']:8.3f}{a['z']:8.3f}  1.00  0.00          {a['elem']:>2}  \n"
        )
        ligand_lines.append(line)

    with open(out_pdb, "w") as out:
        out.writelines(lines)
        out.write("TER\n")
        out.writelines(ligand_lines)
        out.write("END\n")

    return len(lig_atoms)

def generate_tleap_bonds(pdb_path: Path, unit_name: str = "complex") -> str:
    """
    Parses PDB for Disulfide bridges (SG-SG distance) and generates tLEaP bond commands.
    """
    cys_atoms = []
    with open(pdb_path, "r") as f:
        for line in f:
            if line.startswith(("ATOM", "HETATM")):
                if line[12:16].strip() == "SG" and line[17:20].strip() in ["CYS", "CYX", "CYM"]:
                    cys_atoms.append({
                        "chain": line[21], "resSeq": int(line[22:26]),
                        "name": "SG", "x": float(line[30:38]),
                        "y": float(line[38:46]), "z": float(line[46:54])
                    })

    bonds = []
    n = len(cys_atoms)
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            a, b = cys_atoms[i], cys_atoms[j]
            if a["resSeq"] == b["resSeq"] and a["chain"] == b["chain"]: continue
            dist = math.sqrt((a["x"]-b["x"])**2 + (a["y"]-b["y"])**2 + (a["z"]-b["z"])**2)
            if dist <= 2.3:
                bonds.append(f"bond {unit_name}.{a['resSeq']}.SG {unit_name}.{b['resSeq']}.SG")

    return "\n".join(bonds)

"""
File: src/utils/ui.py
Description: Centralized UI drawing for rich tables.
"""
from rich.table import Table
from core.logger import console
from typing import List, Dict

def print_summary_table(title: str, columns: List[str], data: List[Dict[str, str]]):
    """
    Draws a stylized Rich table from a list of dictionaries.
    """
    if not data:
        return
        
    table = Table(title=title, header_style="bold cyan")
    
    for i, col in enumerate(columns):
        justify = "center" if i == 0 else "right"
        table.add_column(col, justify=justify, style="bold" if i == 0 else "")
        
    for row in data:
        table.add_row(*[str(row.get(col, "")) for col in columns])
        
    console.print("")
    console.print(table)
    console.print("")

"""
File: src/utils/visualizations.py
Description: Generation of consensus scatter plots and MPO heatmaps.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import seaborn as sns

def generate_plots(df: pd.DataFrame, out_dir: Path, top_n: int):
    if df.empty: return
    sns.set_theme(style="whitegrid")
    
    # 1. Consensus Scatter Plot
    if all(c in df.columns for c in ["GlideScore", "MMGBSA", "Ultimate_Score"]):
        plt.figure(figsize=(10, 8))
        cnn_col = "Resc_CNNaffinity" if "Resc_CNNaffinity" in df.columns else "Dock_CNNaffinity" if "Dock_CNNaffinity" in df.columns else None
        sizes = df[cnn_col] * 10 if cnn_col else 50
        scatter = plt.scatter(
            x=df["GlideScore"], y=df["MMGBSA"], s=sizes, 
            c=df["Ultimate_Score"], cmap="viridis", alpha=0.8, edgecolor="k"
        )
        native_df = df[df["Is_Native"] == True]
        if not native_df.empty:
            plt.axvline(x=native_df["GlideScore"].iloc[0], color='r', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)
            plt.axhline(y=native_df["MMGBSA"].iloc[0], color='r', linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)
            plt.scatter(
                native_df["GlideScore"], native_df["MMGBSA"], 
                s=150, facecolors='none', edgecolors='r', linewidth=2, label="Native Baseline"
            )
            plt.legend()
            
        plt.colorbar(scatter, label="Ultimate Score")
        plt.xlabel("GlideScore (kcal/mol)")
        plt.ylabel("Prime MM-GBSA (kcal/mol)")
        plt.title("Thermodynamic Landscape & Consensus Score")
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.savefig(out_dir / "04_consensus_scatter.png", dpi=300)
        plt.close()

    # 2. MPO Heatmap
    pr_cols = [c for c in ["PR_Glide", "PR_MMGBSA", "PR_CNN_Resc", "PR_GNINA_Dock"] if c in df.columns]
    if pr_cols:
        top_df = df.head(top_n).set_index("Title")[pr_cols]
        plt.figure(figsize=(8, max(4, int(len(top_df) * 0.3))))
        sns.heatmap(top_df, cmap="RdYlGn", vmin=0, vmax=1, annot=True, fmt=".2f", cbar_kws={'label': 'Percentile Rank'})
        plt.title(f"Multiparameter Optimization (Top {len(top_df)})")
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.savefig(out_dir / "03_mpo_heatmap.png", dpi=300)
        plt.close()

def plot_md_rmsd(df: pd.DataFrame, out_file: Path):
    if df.empty: return
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 5))
    plt.plot(df["Time_ns"], df["Prot_BB_RMSD"], label="Protein Backbone", color="gray", alpha=0.8)
    plt.plot(df["Time_ns"], df["Lig_Heavy_RMSD"], label="Ligand Heavy", color="red", linewidth=2)
    plt.xlabel("Time (ns)")
    plt.ylabel("RMSD (Å)")
    plt.title("Molecular Dynamics RMSD")
    plt.legend()
    plt.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(out_file, dpi=300)
    plt.close()

def plot_md_rmsf(df: pd.DataFrame, df_contacts: pd.DataFrame, out_file: Path):
    if df.empty: return
    plt.figure(figsize=(12, 4))
    plt.plot(df["ResID"], df["RMSF"], color="blue", linewidth=1.2)
    
    # Highlight high-frequency contact residues
    if not df_contacts.empty:
        occ_col = "Total_%" if "Total_%" in df_contacts.columns else "Occupancy_pct"
        if occ_col in df_contacts.columns:
            high_contacts = df_contacts[df_contacts[occ_col] >= 20.0]["ResID"]
            for resid in high_contacts:
                val = df[df["ResID"] == resid]["RMSF"].values
                if len(val) > 0:
                    plt.axvline(x=resid, color='orange', alpha=0.4, linestyle='--')
                
    plt.xlabel("Residue Index")
    plt.ylabel("RMSF (Å)")
    plt.title("Protein C-alpha RMSF (Interacting residues highlighted)")
    plt.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(out_file, dpi=300)
    plt.close()

def plot_mmpbsa_decomp(csv_path: Path, out_file: Path):
    """Plots top contributing residues from gmx_MMPBSA decomp CSV."""
    if not csv_path.exists(): return
    try:
        res_data = []
        with open(csv_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
            for line in f:
                line = line.strip()
                if not line or line.startswith("#"): continue
                parts = [p.strip() for p in line.split(",")]
                # Decomp row format: R:Chain:ResName:ResID, ..., TOTAL
                if len(parts) >= 7 and parts[0].startswith(("R:", "L:")):
                    res_tag = parts[0]
                    if "UNK" in res_tag or "LIG" in res_tag: continue
                    val_str = parts[-1].split("+/-")[0].split("±")[0].strip()
                    try:
                        val = float(val_str)
                        if abs(val) >= 0.5:
                            p_parts = res_tag.split(":")
                            label = f"{p_parts[1]}:{p_parts[2]}{p_parts[3]}" if len(p_parts) >= 4 else res_tag
                            res_data.append({"Residue": label, "Energy": val})
                    except ValueError:
                        continue

        if not res_data: return
        df_plot = pd.DataFrame(res_data).sort_values("Energy")

        plt.figure(figsize=(10, max(4, len(df_plot) * 0.25)))
        colors = ["#16a34a" if x < 0 else "#dc2626" for x in df_plot["Energy"]]
        plt.barh(df_plot["Residue"], df_plot["Energy"], color=colors, edgecolor="black")
        plt.axvline(x=0, color='black', linewidth=1)
        plt.xlabel("ΔG Contribution (kcal/mol)")
        plt.title("Per-Residue MM-PBSA Energy Decomposition (Hot-Spots)")
        plt.grid(axis='x', alpha=0.3)
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.savefig(out_file, dpi=300)
        plt.close()
    except Exception as e:
        import logging
        logging.warning(f"Failed to plot MM-PBSA decomp: {e}")

======================================================================
FILE: cli/core/config_loader.py

"""
File: cli/core/config_loader.py
Description: Scans, loads, and merges atomic configuration YAML files from configs/.
"""
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Any
import yaml

def load_project_configs(project_dir: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Merges all YAML files in project_dir/configs or project_dir into a single dictionary."""
    config_dir = project_dir / "configs"
    search_dirs = [config_dir] if config_dir.exists() else [project_dir]
    
    merged: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    yaml_files = []
    for sdir in search_dirs:
        yaml_files.extend(list(sdir.glob("*.yaml")) + list(sdir.glob("*.yml")))

    for yf in sorted(yaml_files):
        try:
            with open(yf, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                data = yaml.safe_load(f)
                if isinstance(data, dict):
                    merged.update(data)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Warning: Could not parse {yf.name}: {e}")

    # Auto-detection fallbacks for inputs in project root
    if "receptor" not in merged or not merged["receptor"].get("input_file"):
        cif_files = list(project_dir.glob("*.cif"))
        pdb_files = list(project_dir.glob("*.pdb"))
        if cif_files:
            merged.setdefault("receptor", {})["input_file"] = cif_files[0].name
        elif pdb_files:
            merged.setdefault("receptor", {})["input_file"] = pdb_files[0].name

    if "screening" not in merged or not merged["screening"].get("library_file"):
        sdf_files = [f for f in project_dir.glob("*.sdf") if "dock" not in f.name.lower()]
        if sdf_files:
            merged.setdefault("screening", {})["library_file"] = sdf_files[0].name

    return merged

def build_nextflow_args(config: Dict[str, Any], project_dir: Path) -> list:
    """Translates dictionary of parameters into Nextflow CLI arguments."""
    args = []

    # Project
    proj_cfg = config.get("project", {})
    proj_name = proj_cfg.get("name", project_dir.name)
    args.extend(["--project_name", proj_name])

    # Receptor
    rec_cfg = config.get("receptor", {})
    if rec_cfg.get("input_file"):
        rec_path = project_dir / rec_cfg["input_file"]
        if not rec_path.exists():
            # Check receptor/ folder
            rec_path = project_dir / "receptor" / rec_cfg["input_file"]
        args.extend(["--receptor", str(rec_path.resolve())])

    if rec_cfg.get("native_ligand_resname"):
        args.extend(["--native_ligand", str(rec_cfg["native_ligand_resname"])])

    if rec_cfg.get("model_index") is not None:
        args.extend(["--model_index", str(rec_cfg["model_index"])])

    if rec_cfg.get("total_models"):
        args.extend(["--total_models", str(rec_cfg["total_models"])])

    if rec_cfg.get("keep_cofactors"):
        cofactors = rec_cfg["keep_cofactors"]
        if isinstance(cofactors, list):
            args.extend(["--keep_cofactors", " ".join(cofactors)])

    # Membrane
    mem_cfg = config.get("membrane", {})
    if "enabled" in mem_cfg:
        args.extend(["--membrane", "true" if mem_cfg["enabled"] else "false"])
    if mem_cfg.get("type"):
        args.extend(["--membrane_type", str(mem_cfg["type"])])
    if mem_cfg.get("topology"):
        args.extend(["--membrane_topo", str(mem_cfg["topology"])])
    if mem_cfg.get("chains"):
        chains = mem_cfg["chains"]
        args.extend(["--membrane_chains", ",".join(chains) if isinstance(chains, list) else str(chains)])

    # Library
    screen_cfg = config.get("screening", {})
    if screen_cfg.get("library_file"):
        lib_path = project_dir / screen_cfg["library_file"]
        if not lib_path.exists():
            lib_path = project_dir / "ligands" / screen_cfg["library_file"]
        if lib_path.exists():
            args.extend(["--library", str(lib_path.resolve())])

    # Docking Engine
    gnina_cfg = config.get("gnina", {})
    glide_cfg = config.get("glide", {})
    if glide_cfg.get("enabled", False) and gnina_cfg.get("enabled", False):
        args.extend(["--dock_engine", "both"])
    elif glide_cfg.get("enabled", False):
        args.extend(["--dock_engine", "glide"])
    else:
        args.extend(["--dock_engine", "gnina"])

    if gnina_cfg.get("exhaustiveness"):
        args.extend(["--exhaustiveness", str(gnina_cfg["exhaustiveness"])])
    if gnina_cfg.get("cnn_scoring"):
        args.extend(["--cnn_scoring", str(gnina_cfg["cnn_scoring"])])
    if gnina_cfg.get("batch_size"):
        args.extend(["--batch_size", str(gnina_cfg["batch_size"])])

    # Analysis & Funnel Triage
    funnel_cfg = config.get("analysis", {}).get("funnel", {}) or config.get("screening", {}).get("funnel", {})
    if funnel_cfg.get("top_frames_per_ligand"):
        args.extend(["--top_k_frames", str(funnel_cfg["top_frames_per_ligand"])])
    if funnel_cfg.get("library_percentile_cutoff"):
        args.extend(["--percentile_cutoff", str(funnel_cfg["library_percentile_cutoff"])])

    # MD Settings
    md_cfg = config.get("md_simulation", {})
    if "enabled" in md_cfg:
        args.extend(["--run_md", "true" if md_cfg["enabled"] else "false"])
    prod_cfg = md_cfg.get("production", {})
    if prod_cfg.get("total_time_ns"):
        args.extend(["--md_total_ns", str(prod_cfg["total_time_ns"])])
    if prod_cfg.get("chunk_size_ns"):
        args.extend(["--md_chunk_ns", str(prod_cfg["chunk_size_ns"])])
    if prod_cfg.get("replicates"):
        args.extend(["--md_replicates", str(prod_cfg["replicates"])])
    if prod_cfg.get("temperature"):
        args.extend(["--md_temperature", str(prod_cfg["temperature"])])

    # Output directory
    args.extend(["--outdir", str(project_dir.resolve())])

    return args


======================================================================
FILE: cli/core/runner.py
======================================================================
"""
File: cli/core/runner.py
Description: Nextflow orchestrator execution wrapper with Slurm monitoring and /shared scratch lifecycle.
"""
import subprocess
import shutil
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from rich.console import Console

console = Console()
FRAMEWORK_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent

def clean_shared_scratch(project_name: str, verbose: bool = True):
    """Safely cleans the project's scratch workspace on /shared."""
    scratch_dir = Path(f"/shared/work/{project_name}")
    if scratch_dir.exists():
        if verbose:
            console.print(f"[yellow]Cleaning temporary files in {scratch_dir}...[/yellow]")
        try:
            shutil.rmtree(scratch_dir)
            if verbose:
                console.print(f"[green]Successfully cleaned {scratch_dir}[/green]")
        except Exception as e:
            console.print(f"[red]Error cleaning {scratch_dir}: {e}[/red]")
    else:
        if verbose:
            console.print(f"[dim]No scratch directory found at {scratch_dir}[/dim]")

def execute_nextflow(entrypoint: str, extra_args: list, project_dir: Path,
                     profile: str = "slurm", resume: bool = True,
                     auto_clean: bool = True) -> int:
    """Invokes Nextflow orchestrator for a given subworkflow."""
    main_nf = FRAMEWORK_DIR / "main.nf"
    
    project_name = project_dir.name
    # Extract project_name from extra_args if present
    if "--project_name" in extra_args:
        idx = extra_args.index("--project_name")
        if idx + 1 < len(extra_args):
            project_name = extra_args[idx + 1]

    log_file = project_dir / "logs" / f"nextflow_{entrypoint}.log"
    (project_dir / "logs").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    cmd = [
        "nextflow",
        "-log", str(log_file.resolve()),
        "run", str(main_nf.resolve()),
        "-profile", profile,
        "--entry", entrypoint
    ]

    if resume:
        cmd.append("-resume")

    cmd.extend(extra_args)

    console.rule(f"[bold cyan]Nextflow Orchestrator: {entrypoint.upper()} ({profile.upper()})[/bold cyan]")
    console.print(f"[dim]Project Scratch: /shared/work/{project_name}[/dim]")
    console.print(f"[dim]Deliverables:    {project_dir.resolve()}[/dim]")
    console.print(f"[bold blue]Command:[/bold blue] {' '.join(cmd)}\n")

    try:
        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, cwd=project_dir)
        ret = proc.wait()

        if ret == 0:
            console.print(f"\n[bold green]✓ Subworkflow '{entrypoint}' finished successfully![/bold green]")
            if auto_clean:
                clean_shared_scratch(project_name, verbose=True)
            return 0
        else:
            console.print(f"\n[bold red]✗ Nextflow failed with exit code {ret}[/bold red]")
            console.print(f"[yellow]Scratch directory /shared/work/{project_name} preserved for '-resume'.[/yellow]")
            return ret

    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        console.print("\n[bold yellow]Execution canceled by user. Scratch preserved for resume.[/bold yellow]")
        return 130
    except Exception as e:
        console.print(f"\n[bold red]Failed to launch Nextflow: {e}[/bold red]")
        return 1


======================================================================
FILE: cli/main.py
======================================================================
"""
File: cli/main.py
Description: Main CLI entrypoint for `sbdd` orchestrator tool.
"""
import argparse
import sys
import shutil
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from rich.console import Console
from rich.table import Table

from cli.core.config_loader import load_project_configs, build_nextflow_args
from cli.core.runner import execute_nextflow, clean_shared_scratch, FRAMEWORK_DIR

console = Console()

def cmd_init(args):
    """Initializes a new project workspace with atomic YAML configs."""
    target_dir = Path(args.directory).resolve()
    target_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    config_dir = target_dir / "configs"
    config_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    src_configs = FRAMEWORK_DIR / "assets" / "configs"
    if src_configs.exists():
        for f in src_configs.glob("*.yaml"):
            dest = config_dir / f.name
            if not dest.exists() or args.force:
                shutil.copy(f, dest)

    console.print(f"[bold green]✓ Project initialized at: {target_dir}[/bold green]")
    console.print(f"[cyan]Configurations created in:[/cyan] {config_dir}")
    console.print("\n[bold]Next steps:[/bold]")
    console.print("  1. Copy your receptor (.cif or .pdb) into the project directory.")
    console.print("  2. Copy your ligand library (.sdf) into the project directory.")
    console.print("  3. Review configs in configs/ (e.g. receptor.yaml, gnina.yaml).")
    console.print("  4. Run [bold cyan]sbdd prep[/bold cyan] or [bold cyan]sbdd all[/bold cyan]!\n")

def cmd_status(args):
    """Inspects active Slurm jobs and Nextflow status."""
    console.rule("[bold cyan]Cluster & Slurm Status[/bold cyan]")
    try:
        res = subprocess.run(["squeue", "-o", "%.10i %.10P %.20j %.10u %.2t %.10M %.6D %R"], capture_output=True, text=True)
        console.print(res.stdout)
    except Exception as e:
        console.print(f"[red]Could not query Slurm squeue: {e}[/red]")

    # Check Nextflow log in cwd
    log_f = Path.cwd() / ".nextflow.log"
    if log_f.exists():
        console.rule("[bold cyan]Recent Nextflow Activity[/bold cyan]")
        tail = subprocess.run(["tail", "-n", "15", str(log_f)], capture_output=True, text=True)
        console.print(tail.stdout)

def cmd_clean(args):
    """Cleans /shared/work/<project_name> scratch directory."""
    cwd = Path.cwd()
    configs = load_project_configs(cwd)
    proj_name = configs.get("project", {}).get("name", cwd.name)
    clean_shared_scratch(proj_name, verbose=True)

def run_workflow(entrypoint: str, args):
    """Generic dispatcher from CLI flags and configs to Nextflow execution."""
    cwd = Path.cwd()
    configs = load_project_configs(cwd)
    nf_args = build_nextflow_args(configs, cwd)

    # CLI flag overrides
    if getattr(args, "receptor", None):
        nf_args.extend(["--receptor", str(Path(args.receptor).resolve())])
    if getattr(args, "library", None):
        nf_args.extend(["--library", str(Path(args.library).resolve())])
    if getattr(args, "engine", None):
        nf_args.extend(["--dock_engine", args.engine])
    if getattr(args, "hits", None):
        nf_args.extend(["--md_hits", args.hits])
    if getattr(args, "urgent", False):
        nf_args.extend(["--queue", "urgent"])

    profile = "local" if getattr(args, "local", False) else "slurm"
    resume = not getattr(args, "no_resume", False)
    auto_clean = not getattr(args, "keep_scratch", False)

    return execute_nextflow(entrypoint, nf_args, cwd, profile=profile, resume=resume, auto_clean=auto_clean)

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="sbdd",
        description="SBDD Pipeline Nextflow HPC Orchestrator CLI"
    )
    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="subcommand", help="Available subcommands")

    # 1. init
    p_init = subparsers.add_parser("init", help="Initialize new project workspace")
    p_init.add_argument("directory", nargs="?", default=".", help="Target project directory (default: current)")
    p_init.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="Overwrite existing templates")

    # Common options for workflows
    def add_common_wf_args(p):
        p.add_argument("--local", action="store_true", help="Run locally without Slurm")
        p.add_argument("--no-resume", action="store_true", help="Start from scratch without -resume")
        p.add_argument("--keep-scratch", action="store_true", help="Do not delete /shared/work upon completion")
        p.add_argument("--urgent", action="store_true", help="Route tasks to urgent Slurm partition")

    # 2. prep / prepare
    p_prep = subparsers.add_parser("prep", aliases=["prepare"], help="Run receptor & ligand preparation")
    p_prep.add_argument("--receptor", help="Path to input structure (.cif/.pdb)")
    p_prep.add_argument("--library", help="Path to ligand library (.sdf)")
    add_common_wf_args(p_prep)

    # 3. dock / screen
    p_dock = subparsers.add_parser("dock", aliases=["screen"], help="Run distributed docking across GPU nodes")
    p_dock.add_argument("--engine", choices=["gnina", "glide", "both"], default="gnina", help="Docking engine choice")
    p_dock.add_argument("--library", help="Path to ligand library (.sdf)")
    add_common_wf_args(p_dock)

    # 4. rescore
    p_rescore = subparsers.add_parser("rescore", help="Run MM-GBSA and CNN rescoring")
    add_common_wf_args(p_rescore)

    # 5. md / dynamics
    p_dyn = subparsers.add_parser("dynamics", aliases=["md", "md-run", "md-setup"], help="Execute chunked MD simulations on Slurm GPUs")
    p_dyn.add_argument("--hits", help="Comma-separated hits to simulate")
    p_dyn.add_argument("--replicates", type=int, help="Number of MD replicates")
    add_common_wf_args(p_dyn)

    # 6. md-analyze
    p_mda = subparsers.add_parser("md-analyze", help="Run trajectory PBC fix, metrics, and MM-PBSA")
    p_mda.add_argument("--traj", help="Direct path to custom .xtc file")
    p_mda.add_argument("--topol", help="Direct path to custom topol.top or PDB")
    add_common_wf_args(p_mda)

    # 7. all / run
    p_all = subparsers.add_parser("all", aliases=["run"], help="Execute end-to-end pipeline")
    p_all.add_argument("--engine", choices=["gnina", "glide", "both"], help="Docking engine choice")
    p_all.add_argument("--hits", help="Comma-separated hit titles for MD")
    p_all.add_argument("--top-n", dest="top_n", type=int, help="Number of top hits for MD")
    add_common_wf_args(p_all)

    # 8. clean
    p_clean = subparsers.add_parser("clean", help="Clean /shared/work scratch directory for current project")

    # 9. status
    p_stat = subparsers.add_parser("status", help="Inspect Slurm queue and Nextflow job status")

    args = parser.parse_args()

    if not args.subcommand:
        parser.print_help()
        sys.exit(0)

    subcmd = args.subcommand
    if subcmd == "init":
        cmd_init(args)
    elif subcmd == "status":
        cmd_status(args)
    elif subcmd == "clean":
        cmd_clean(args)
    elif subcmd in ["prep", "prepare"]:
        sys.exit(run_workflow("prepare", args))
    elif subcmd in ["dock", "screen"]:
        sys.exit(run_workflow("screen", args))
    elif subcmd in ["dynamics", "md", "md-run", "md-setup", "md-analyze"]:
        sys.exit(run_workflow("dynamics", args))
    elif subcmd in ["all", "run"]:
        sys.exit(run_workflow("all", args))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

/*
 * conf/base.config
 * Default process directives and computational resource labels
 */


process {
    // Default error handling
    errorStrategy = { task.exitStatus in [143, 137, 104, 134, 139] ? 'retry' : 'terminate' }
    maxRetries    = 2

    // Default resources
    cpus   = { 1 * task.attempt }
    memory = { 2.GB * task.attempt }
    time   = { 1.h * task.attempt }

    // Master node local execution (for preprocessing, staging, and packaging)
    withLabel: process_master {
        executor = 'local'
        scratch  = false
        cpus     = 4
    }

    // Standard resource profiles
    withLabel: process_single {
        cpus   = 1
        memory = 2.GB
        time   = 30.m
    }

    withLabel: process_low {
        cpus   = { 2 * task.attempt }
        memory = { 4.GB * task.attempt }
        time   = 2.h
    }

    withLabel: process_medium {
        cpus   = { 8 * task.attempt }
        memory = { 16.GB * task.attempt }
        time   = 12.h
    }

    withLabel: process_high {
        cpus   = 16
        memory = 32.GB
        time   = 24.h
    }

    withLabel: process_gpu {
        cpus     = 4
        memory   = 16.GB
        time     = 48.h
        maxForks = 3 // 1 task per GPU node on cluster (3 GPU nodes total)
    }

    withLabel: process_urgent {
        queue = 'urgent'
        time  = 2.h
    }
}

/*
 * conf/modules.config
 * Defines output publishing directories for all pipeline stages
 */

process {
    // 1. Receptor & Ligand Preparation
    withName: '.*:PREPARE_RECEPTOR:.*' {
        publishDir = [
            path: { "${params.outdir}/receptor" },
            mode: 'copy',
            saveAs: { filename -> filename.equals('versions.yml') ? null : filename }
        ]
    }

    withName: '.*:PREPARE_LIGANDS:.*' {
        publishDir = [
            path: { "${params.outdir}/ligands" },
            mode: 'copy',
            saveAs: { filename -> filename.equals('versions.yml') ? null : filename }
        ]
    }

    // 2. Docking
    withName: '.*:DOCK_LIBRARY:.*' {
        publishDir = [
            path: { "${params.outdir}/docking" },
            mode: 'copy',
            saveAs: { filename -> filename.equals('versions.yml') ? null : filename }
        ]
    }

    // 3. MD Setup
    withName: '.*:SETUP_MD_SYSTEM:.*' {
        publishDir = [
            path: { "${params.outdir}/md/${meta.id}" },
            mode: 'copy',
            saveAs: { filename -> filename.equals('versions.yml') ? null : filename }
        ]
    }

    // 5. MD Production & Equilibration
    withName: '.*:RUN_CHUNKED_MD:.*' {
        publishDir = [
            path: { "${params.outdir}/md/${meta.id}/rep_${meta.rep}" },
            mode: 'copy',
            saveAs: { filename -> filename.equals('versions.yml') ? null : filename }
        ]
    }

    // 6. MD Analysis & MMPBSA
    withName: '.*:ANALYZE_MD_TRAJ:.*' {
        publishDir = [
            path: { "${params.outdir}/md/${meta.id}/rep_${meta.rep}/analysis" },
            mode: 'copy',
            saveAs: { filename -> filename.equals('versions.yml') ? null : filename }
        ]
    }

    // 7. Master Reporting
    withName: '.*:SUMMARIZE_.*' {
        publishDir = [
            path: { "${params.outdir}/reports" },
            mode: 'copy'
        ]
    }
}

profiles {
    slurm {
        executor {
            name         = 'slurm'
            queueSize    = 50
            pollInterval = '1 sec'
        }

        process {
            executor       = 'slurm'
            queue          = 'main'
            clusterOptions = '--requeue'
            stageInMode    = 'copy'

            // Before script to activate cluster conda environment
            beforeScript = 'source /home/user/miniforge3/bin/activate sbdd_cluster_env 2>/dev/null || source /home/andrey/miniforge3/bin/activate sbdd_cluster_env 2>/dev/null || source ~/miniforge3/bin/activate sbdd_cluster_env 2>/dev/null || true'

            withLabel: process_master {
                executor = 'local'
                scratch  = false
            }

            withLabel: process_gpu {
                queue          = 'main'
                clusterOptions = '--requeue'
            }

            withLabel: process_urgent {
                queue = 'urgent'
            }
        }
    }

    local {
        process {
            executor = 'local'
            beforeScript = 'source /home/user/miniforge3/bin/activate sbdd_cluster_env 2>/dev/null || source ~/miniforge3/bin/activate sbdd_cluster_env 2>/dev/null || true'
            
            withLabel: process_gpu {
                maxForks = 1
            }
        }
    }
}

======================================================================
FILE: main.nf

#!/usr/bin/env nextflow
/*
 * main.nf
 * SBDD Pipeline Nextflow HPC Orchestrator Entrypoint
 */

nextflow.enable.dsl = 2

// Include top-level workflows
include { PREPARE_WF  } from './workflows/prepare'
include { SCREEN_WF   } from './workflows/screen'
include { DYNAMICS_WF } from './workflows/dynamics'
include { SBDD_WF     } from './workflows/sbdd'

def helpMessage() {
    log.info """
    ========================================================================
     SBDD Pipeline Nextflow HPC Orchestrator
    ========================================================================
    Usage:
      nextflow run ${projectDir}/main.nf -profile slurm [options]

    Primary Parameters:
      --entry            Target subworkflow: 'prepare', 'screen', 'dynamics', 'all' (default: 'all')
      --project_name     Project name for isolated /shared/work scratch
      --receptor         Path to input receptor structure (.cif or .pdb)
      --library          Path to ligand library (.sdf)
      --native_ligand    3-letter residue name of native reference ligand (default: PKA)
      --dock_engine      Docking engine: 'gnina' or 'glide' (default: gnina)
      --membrane         Enable PPM 3.0 membrane immersion & lipid bilayer (default: true)
      --run_md           Enable downstream MD simulations (default: false)
      --md_hits          Hits to simulate in MD: 'native' or comma-separated list
      --outdir           Target directory for final outputs (default: current directory)
    ========================================================================
    """.stripIndent()
}

workflow {
    if (params.help) {
        helpMessage()
        exit 0
    }

    def entrypoint = params.entry ? params.entry.toLowerCase() : "all"

    if (entrypoint == "prepare") {
        PREPARE_WF()
    } else if (entrypoint == "screen") {
        SCREEN_WF()
    } else if (entrypoint == "dynamics") {
        DYNAMICS_WF()
    } else {
        SBDD_WF()
    }
}

/*
 * modules/amber/antechamber.nf
 * Antechamber AM1-BCC Charge Calculation and GAFF2 Parametrization
 */

process ANTECHAMBER {
    tag "${meta.id}"
    label 'process_high'

    input:
    tuple val(meta), path(ligand_mol2), path(charge_file)

    output:
    tuple val(meta), path("UNK_gaff2.mol2"), path("UNK.frcmod"), emit: gaff_params

    script:
    """
    charge=\$(cat ${charge_file} | tr -d '[:space:]')
    [ -z "\$charge" ] && charge=0

    echo "Running Antechamber with net charge \$charge"
    antechamber \
        -i ${ligand_mol2} \
        -fi mol2 \
        -o UNK_gaff2.mol2 \
        -fo mol2 \
        -at gaff2 \
        -c bcc \
        -nc "\$charge" \
        -rn UNK

    parmchk2 \
        -i UNK_gaff2.mol2 \
        -f mol2 \
        -o UNK.frcmod \
        -s 2
    """

    stub:
    """
    touch UNK_gaff2.mol2
    touch UNK.frcmod
    """
}

/*
 * modules/amber/tleap.nf
 * Amber tLEaP System Topology and Coordinate Builder
 */

process TLEAP {
    tag "${meta.id}"
    label 'process_medium'

    input:
    tuple val(meta), path(complex_pdb), path(unk_mol2), path(unk_frcmod)
    val(water_model)
    val(is_membrane)
    val(box_padding)

    output:
    tuple val(meta), path("complex.prmtop"), path("complex.inpcrd"), emit: amber_system
    tuple val(meta), path("complex_tleap.pdb"), emit: tleap_pdb

    script:
    def solvate_cmd = !is_membrane ? "solvateBox complex OPCBOX ${box_padding}" : ""
    """
    # Clean artificial TER around terminal caps
    python3 - << 'EOF'
    with open("${complex_pdb}", "r") as f:
        lines = f.readlines()
    cleaned = []
    last_chain = "A"
    for i, l in enumerate(lines):
        if l.startswith(("ATOM", "HETATM")) and len(l) >= 22:
            ch = l[21].strip()
            if ch: last_chain = ch
        if l.startswith("TER"):
            if i > 0 and "ACE" in lines[i-1]: continue
            if i + 1 < len(lines) and "NME" in lines[i+1]: continue
        if ("ACE" in l or "NME" in l) and l.startswith(("ATOM", "HETATM")):
            l = l[:21] + last_chain + l[22:]
        if "ASP" in l and ("HD2" in l or "2HD" in l): l = l[:17] + "ASH" + l[20:]
        elif "GLU" in l and ("HE2" in l or "2HE" in l): l = l[:17] + "GLH" + l[20:]
        cleaned.append(l)
    with open("complex_clean.pdb", "w") as f:
        f.writelines(cleaned)
    EOF

    cat << EOF > tleap.in
source leaprc.protein.ff19SB
source leaprc.gaff2
source leaprc.lipid21
source leaprc.water.opc
loadAmberParams frcmod.ionslm_126_opc
UNK = loadmol2 ${unk_mol2}
loadAmberParams ${unk_frcmod}
complex = loadPdb complex_clean.pdb
set default PBRadii mbondi3
addPdbResMap { {"Zn2+" "ZN"} {"Ca2+" "CA"} {"Mg2+" "MG"} {"Na+" "NA"} {"K+" "K"} {"Cl-" "CL"} }
${solvate_cmd}
addIonsRand complex Cl- 0
addIonsRand complex K+ 0
charge complex
saveAmberParm complex complex.prmtop complex.inpcrd
savepdb complex complex_tleap.pdb
quit
EOF

    tleap -f tleap.in
    """

    stub:
    """
    touch complex.prmtop
    touch complex.inpcrd
    touch complex_tleap.pdb
    """
}

/*
 * modules/common/stage_inputs.nf
 * Stages initial input files from Master local filesystem into /shared scratch storage
 */

process STAGE_INPUTS {
    executor 'local'
    scratch false
    label 'process_single'

    input:
    val rec_file
    val lib_file
    val ref_file

    output:
    path("receptor.*"), emit: staged_receptor
    path("library.sdf"), emit: staged_library, optional: true
    path("ref_native.*"), emit: staged_ref, optional: true

    script:
    """
    ext="\$(basename "${rec_file}" | awk -F. '{if (NF>1) print \$NF; else print "pdb"}')"
    cp -L "${rec_file}" "receptor.\${ext}"

    if [ -n "${lib_file}" ] && [ "${lib_file}" != "NO_FILE" ] && [ -f "${lib_file}" ]; then
        cp -L "${lib_file}" library.sdf
    fi

    if [ -n "${ref_file}" ] && [ "${ref_file}" != "NO_FILE" ] && [ "${ref_file}" != "${rec_file}" ] && [ -f "${ref_file}" ]; then
        ref_ext="\$(basename "${ref_file}" | awk -F. '{if (NF>1) print \$NF; else print "sdf"}')"
        cp -L "${ref_file}" "ref_native.\${ref_ext}"
    fi
    """

    stub:
    """
    touch receptor.pdb
    touch library.sdf
    touch ref_native.sdf
    """
}

/*
 * modules/gnina/dock.nf
 * Distributed GNINA Docking Process for Slurm GPU nodes
 */

process GNINA_DOCK {
    tag "${meta.id}_chunk_${chunk_idx}"
    label 'process_gpu'

    input:
    tuple val(meta), path(receptor_pdb), path(ref_lig_sdf), path(chunk_sdf), val(chunk_idx)
    val(exhaustiveness)
    val(cnn_scoring)
    val(num_modes)
    val(autobox_add)

    output:
    tuple val(meta), path("docked_chunk_${chunk_idx}.sdf"), emit: docked_sdf
    tuple val(meta), path("gnina_chunk_${chunk_idx}.log"), emit: log

    script:
    """
    echo "=== Worker: \$(hostname) (GPU Node) ==="
    nvidia-smi --query-gpu=name,memory.total --format=csv,noheader || true

    gnina \
        -r ${receptor_pdb} \
        -l ${chunk_sdf} \
        --autobox_ligand ${ref_lig_sdf} \
        --autobox_add ${autobox_add} \
        --exhaustiveness ${exhaustiveness} \
        --cnn_scoring ${cnn_scoring} \
        --num_modes ${num_modes} \
        -o docked_chunk_${chunk_idx}.sdf > gnina_chunk_${chunk_idx}.log
    """

    stub:
    """
    touch docked_chunk_${chunk_idx}.sdf
    touch gnina_chunk_${chunk_idx}.log
    """
}
