/*
 * subworkflows/prepare_ligands.nf
 * Splits SDF library into parallel chunks and/or runs Schrodinger LigPrep
 */

include { LIGPREP } from '../modules/schrodinger/ligprep'

process SPLIT_SDF {
    tag "${meta.id}"
    executor 'local'
    scratch false
    label 'process_single'

    input:
    tuple val(meta), path(library_sdf)
    val(batch_size)

    output:
    tuple val(meta), path("chunk_*.sdf"), emit: chunks

    script:
    """
    python3 ${projectDir}/bin/split_sdf.py \\
        --input ${library_sdf} \\
        --batch_size ${batch_size} \\
        --outdir .
    """

    stub:
    """
    touch chunk_000.sdf
    touch chunk_001.sdf
    """
}

process MERGE_LIGPREP {
    tag "${project_name}"
    executor 'local'
    scratch false
    label 'process_single'

    input:
    val(project_name)
    path(chunk_maes)

    output:
    tuple val([id: project_name, project: project_name]), path("${project_name}_prepared.maegz"), emit: prepared_maegz

    script:
    """
    schrodinger structcat ${chunk_maes} -omae ${project_name}_prepared.maegz
    """

    stub:
    """
    touch ${project_name}_prepared.maegz
    """
}

workflow PREPARE_LIGANDS {
    take:
    ch_library    // channel: [ val(meta), path(library_file) ]
    dock_engine   // val: "both", "gnina", "glide"
    batch_size    // val: 15
    target_ph     // val: 7.4
    ph_tol        // val: 2.0
    ff_version    // val: 16
    max_states    // val: 32

    main:
    ch_prepared_mae = Channel.empty()
    ch_chunks       = Channel.empty()

    // 1. Split SDF library into chunks for distributed computing across Slurm
    SPLIT_SDF(ch_library, batch_size)
    ch_chunks = SPLIT_SDF.out.chunks.flatMap { meta, files ->
        def fileList = files instanceof List ? files : [files]
        fileList.withIndex().collect { f, idx ->
            def chunk_meta = [
                id: "${meta.id}_c${idx}",
                chunk_idx: idx,
                project: meta.project ?: meta.id
            ]
            [ chunk_meta, f, idx ]
        }
    }

    // 2. Parallel LIGPREP across Slurm worker nodes
    if (dock_engine in ["glide", "both"]) {
        ch_ligprep_in = ch_chunks.map { chunk_meta, f, idx -> [ chunk_meta, f ] }
        LIGPREP(ch_ligprep_in, target_ph, ph_tol, ff_version, max_states)

        ch_all_maegz = LIGPREP.out.prepared_maegz
            .map { chunk_meta, maegz -> maegz }
            .collect()

        MERGE_LIGPREP(params.project_name, ch_all_maegz)
        ch_prepared_mae = LIGPREP.out.prepared_maegz
    }

    emit:
    prepared_ligands = ch_prepared_mae
    chunks           = ch_chunks
}

======================================================================
FILE: subworkflows/prepare_receptor.nf
======================================================================
/*
 * subworkflows/prepare_receptor.nf
 * Decoupled Receptor preparation pipeline:
 * 1. EXTRACT_COMPLEX (Schrödinger): complex (.cif/.pdb) -> raw_protein.pdb + raw_native.mae + raw_native.sdf
 * 2. If membrane:
 *      IMMERS (Pure PPM 3.0 CLI): raw_protein.pdb -> oriented_protein.pdb + membrane_info
 *      ALIGN_LIGAND (Schrödinger): raw_protein.pdb + oriented_protein.pdb + raw_native.mae -> ref_native.mae + ref_native.sdf
 * 3. PREPWIZARD (Schrödinger): oriented (or raw) protein -> prepared_mae + prepared_pdb
 * 4. If membrane:
 *      APPLY_MEMBRANE (Schrödinger Prime): prepared_mae + membrane_info -> membrane_mae
 */

include { EXTRACT_COMPLEX } from "../modules/schrodinger/extract_complex"
include { IMMERS          } from "../modules/membrane/immers"
include { ALIGN_LIGAND    } from "../modules/schrodinger/align_ligand"
include { PREPWIZARD      } from "../modules/schrodinger/prepwizard"
include { APPLY_MEMBRANE  } from "../modules/membrane/apply_membrane"

workflow PREPARE_RECEPTOR {
    take:
    ch_input          // channel: [ val(meta), path(complex_file), val(frame_idx) ]
    native_ligand     // val: "PKA"
    cofactors         // val: "HEM,MG"
    membrane_enabled  // val: true/false
    membrane_type     // val: "MOM"
    membrane_topo     // val: "in"
    membrane_chains   // val: "A"
    target_ph         // val: 7.4
    ph_tol            // val: 1.0
    min_rmsd          // val: 0.3
    cap_termini       // val: true

    main:
    // 1. Structure extraction: Separate raw protein from native ligand/cofactors
    EXTRACT_COMPLEX(
        ch_input,
        native_ligand,
        cofactors
    )

    if (membrane_enabled) {
        // 2. Pure PPM 3.0 membrane orientation calculation (standalone, no Schrödinger)
        IMMERS(
            EXTRACT_COMPLEX.out.raw_protein_pdb,
            membrane_type,
            membrane_topo,
            membrane_chains
        )

        // 3. Align native reference ligand to the oriented receptor frame
        ch_align_in = EXTRACT_COMPLEX.out.raw_protein_pdb
            .join(IMMERS.out.oriented_pdb)
            .join(EXTRACT_COMPLEX.out.raw_native_mae)
        ALIGN_LIGAND(ch_align_in)

        ch_prep_in          = IMMERS.out.oriented_pdb
        ch_final_ref_native = ALIGN_LIGAND.out.ref_native_mae
        ch_final_ref_sdf    = ALIGN_LIGAND.out.ref_native_sdf
        ch_mem_json         = IMMERS.out.membrane_json
        ch_mem_txt          = IMMERS.out.membrane_txt
    } else {
        ch_prep_in          = EXTRACT_COMPLEX.out.raw_protein_pdb
        ch_final_ref_native = EXTRACT_COMPLEX.out.raw_native_mae
        ch_final_ref_sdf    = EXTRACT_COMPLEX.out.raw_native_sdf
        ch_mem_json         = Channel.empty()
        ch_mem_txt          = Channel.empty()
    }

    // 4. Schrodinger Protein Preparation Wizard
    PREPWIZARD(
        ch_prep_in,
        target_ph,
        ph_tol,
        min_rmsd,
        cap_termini
    )

    ch_final_mae = PREPWIZARD.out.prepared_mae
    ch_final_pdb = PREPWIZARD.out.prepared_pdb

    // 5. Optional implicit membrane property injection for Prime
    if (membrane_enabled) {
        ch_apply_in = PREPWIZARD.out.prepared_mae
            .join(ch_mem_txt)
            
        APPLY_MEMBRANE(ch_apply_in)
        ch_final_mae = APPLY_MEMBRANE.out.membrane_mae
    }

    emit:
    prepared_pdb     = ch_final_pdb
    prepared_mae     = ch_final_mae
    ref_native_mae   = ch_final_ref_native
    ref_native_sdf   = ch_final_ref_sdf
    membrane_json    = ch_mem_json
}

======================================================================
FILE: subworkflows/run_chunked_md.nf
======================================================================
/*
 * subworkflows/run_chunked_md.nf
 * Replicated production MD where each 10 ns chunk is an independent Slurm job,
 * allowing faster cluster nodes to dynamically pick up subsequent chunks.
 */

include { GMX_EQUILIBRATION } from '../modules/gromacs/equilibration'
include { RUN_MD_INIT       } from '../modules/gromacs/run_chunk'
include { RUN_MD_CHUNK as CHUNK_2 } from '../modules/gromacs/run_chunk'
include { RUN_MD_CHUNK as CHUNK_3 } from '../modules/gromacs/run_chunk'
include { RUN_MD_CHUNK as CHUNK_4 } from '../modules/gromacs/run_chunk'
include { RUN_MD_CHUNK as CHUNK_5 } from '../modules/gromacs/run_chunk'
include { RUN_MD_CHUNK as CHUNK_6 } from '../modules/gromacs/run_chunk'
include { TRJCAT            } from '../modules/gromacs/trjcat'
include { TRJCONV           } from '../modules/gromacs/trjconv'

workflow RUN_CHUNKED_MD {
    take:
    ch_system        // channel: [ val(meta), path(gro), path(topol), path(ndx), path(posre_files) ]
    is_membrane      // val: true/false
    temperature      // val: 310.15
    total_ns         // val: 60
    chunk_ns         // val: 10
    replicates       // val: 3

    main:
    // 1. Equilibration on Slurm GPU
    GMX_EQUILIBRATION(
        ch_system,
        is_membrane,
        temperature
    )

    // System topology/index lookup keyed by target_id
    ch_sys_info = ch_system.map { meta, gro, topol, ndx, posre -> [ meta.id, topol, ndx ] }

    // 2. Expand into independent replicates for production MD
    def num_reps = (replicates as int)
    ch_chunk1_in = GMX_EQUILIBRATION.out.equilibrated_state
        .map { meta, eq_gro, eq_cpt -> [ meta.id, meta, eq_gro, eq_cpt ] }
        .combine(ch_sys_info, by: 0)
        .flatMap { target_id, meta, eq_gro, eq_cpt, topol, ndx ->
            (1..num_reps).collect { rep_id ->
                def rep_meta = meta + [rep: rep_id]
                [ rep_meta, eq_gro, eq_cpt, topol, ndx ]
            }
        }

    // 3. Chunk 1 (0 -> 10 ns) - Initializes velocities & generates md_1.tpr
    RUN_MD_INIT(ch_chunk1_in, is_membrane, chunk_ns, temperature)
    ch_all_xtcs = RUN_MD_INIT.out.xtc
    ch_last_tpr = RUN_MD_INIT.out.next_chunk_input.map { meta, gro, cpt, tpr -> [ meta, tpr ] }

    // Chunk 2 (10 -> 20 ns)
    ch_chunk2_in = RUN_MD_INIT.out.next_chunk_input
        .map { meta, gro, cpt, tpr -> [ meta, cpt, tpr, 2 ] }
    CHUNK_2(ch_chunk2_in, chunk_ns)
    ch_all_xtcs = ch_all_xtcs.mix(CHUNK_2.out.xtc)
    ch_last_tpr = CHUNK_2.out.next_chunk_input.map { meta, gro, cpt, tpr -> [ meta, tpr ] }

    // Chunk 3 (20 -> 30 ns)
    ch_chunk3_in = CHUNK_2.out.next_chunk_input
        .map { meta, gro, cpt, tpr -> [ meta, cpt, tpr, 3 ] }
    CHUNK_3(ch_chunk3_in, chunk_ns)
    ch_all_xtcs = ch_all_xtcs.mix(CHUNK_3.out.xtc)
    ch_last_tpr = CHUNK_3.out.next_chunk_input.map { meta, gro, cpt, tpr -> [ meta, tpr ] }

    // Chunk 4 (30 -> 40 ns)
    ch_chunk4_in = CHUNK_3.out.next_chunk_input
        .map { meta, gro, cpt, tpr -> [ meta, cpt, tpr, 4 ] }
    CHUNK_4(ch_chunk4_in, chunk_ns)
    ch_all_xtcs = ch_all_xtcs.mix(CHUNK_4.out.xtc)
    ch_last_tpr = CHUNK_4.out.next_chunk_input.map { meta, gro, cpt, tpr -> [ meta, tpr ] }

    // Chunk 5 (40 -> 50 ns)
    ch_chunk5_in = CHUNK_4.out.next_chunk_input
        .map { meta, gro, cpt, tpr -> [ meta, cpt, tpr, 5 ] }
    CHUNK_5(ch_chunk5_in, chunk_ns)
    ch_all_xtcs = ch_all_xtcs.mix(CHUNK_5.out.xtc)
    ch_last_tpr = CHUNK_5.out.next_chunk_input.map { meta, gro, cpt, tpr -> [ meta, tpr ] }

    // Chunk 6 (50 -> 60 ns)
    ch_chunk6_in = CHUNK_5.out.next_chunk_input
        .map { meta, gro, cpt, tpr -> [ meta, cpt, tpr, 6 ] }
    CHUNK_6(ch_chunk6_in, chunk_ns)
    ch_all_xtcs = ch_all_xtcs.mix(CHUNK_6.out.xtc)
    ch_last_tpr = CHUNK_6.out.next_chunk_input.map { meta, gro, cpt, tpr -> [ meta, tpr ] }

    // 4. Concatenate all chunk xtcs for each replicate
    ch_to_concat = ch_all_xtcs.groupTuple(by: 0)
    TRJCAT(ch_to_concat)

    // 5. Clean trajectory (PBC fix + centering)
    ch_trjconv_in = TRJCAT.out.concat_xtc
        .join(ch_last_tpr, by: 0)
        .map { meta, xtc, tpr -> [ meta.id, meta, xtc, tpr ] }
        .combine(ch_system.map { meta, gro, topol, ndx, posre -> [ meta.id, ndx ] }, by: 0)
        .map { target_id, meta, xtc, tpr, ndx -> [ meta, xtc, tpr, ndx ] }

    TRJCONV(ch_trjconv_in)

    // Join with topol and ndx for downstream analysis
    ch_ready_analysis = TRJCONV.out.clean_traj
        .map { meta, clean_xtc, ref_pdb -> [ meta.id, meta, clean_xtc, ref_pdb ] }
        .combine(ch_system.map { meta, gro, topol, ndx, posre -> [ meta.id, topol, ndx ] }, by: 0)
        .map { target_id, meta, clean_xtc, ref_pdb, topol, ndx ->
            [ meta, clean_xtc, ref_pdb, topol, ndx ]
        }

    emit:
    clean_trajectory = ch_ready_analysis
}

======================================================================
FILE: subworkflows/setup_md_system.nf
======================================================================
/*
 * subworkflows/setup_md_system.nf
 * Full MD System Build: Extraction -> Amberization -> Membrane/Solvation -> GROMACS conversion -> Restraints
 */

include { ANTECHAMBER    } from '../modules/amber/antechamber'
include { PACKMOL_MEMGEN } from '../modules/membrane/packmol'
include { TLEAP          } from '../modules/amber/tleap'
include { PARMED         } from '../modules/amber/parmed'
include { DECLASH        } from '../modules/membrane/declash'
include { NDX_POSRE      } from '../modules/gromacs/ndx_posre'

process EXTRACT_MD_SYSTEM {
    tag "${meta.id}"
    label 'process_single'

    input:
    tuple val(meta), path(lig_file), val(lig_title), path(rec_file)

    output:
    tuple val(meta), path("receptor.pdb"), path("ligand.mol2"), path("lig_charge.txt"), emit: extracted

    script:
    """
    cat << 'EOF' > extract_md.py
import sys, shutil
from pathlib import Path
from rdkit import Chem
from rdkit.Chem import AllChem
import subprocess

rec_path = Path("${rec_file}")
shutil.copy(rec_path, "receptor.pdb")

suppl = Chem.SDMolSupplier("${lig_file}", removeHs=False)
target_mol = None
for m in suppl:
    if m:
        target_mol = m
        break
if target_mol is None:
    sys.exit(1)

charge = Chem.GetFormalCharge(target_mol)
target_mol = Chem.AddHs(target_mol, addCoords=True)
if not target_mol.GetNumConformers() or all(target_mol.GetConformer().GetAtomPosition(i).z == 0 for i in range(target_mol.GetNumAtoms())):
    AllChem.EmbedMolecule(target_mol)

w = Chem.SDWriter("ligand.sdf")
w.write(target_mol)
w.close()

subprocess.run(["antechamber", "-i", "ligand.sdf", "-fi", "sdf", "-o", "ligand.mol2", "-fo", "mol2"], check=True)
with open("lig_charge.txt", "w") as f:
    f.write(str(charge))
EOF

    python3 extract_md.py
    """

    stub:
    """
    touch receptor.pdb
    touch ligand.mol2
    echo "0" > lig_charge.txt
    """
}

process MERGE_AMBER_COMPLEX {
    tag "${meta.id}"
    label 'process_single'

    input:
    tuple val(meta), path(rec_pdb), path(unk_mol2)

    output:
    tuple val(meta), path("complex.pdb"), emit: complex_pdb

    script:
    """
    python3 -c "
import parmed as pmd
prot = pmd.load_file('${rec_pdb}')
lig = pmd.load_file('${unk_mol2}')
if hasattr(lig, 'to_structure'):
    lig = lig.to_structure()
used_chains = {res.chain for res in prot.residues}
lig_chain = next((c for c in 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ' if c not in used_chains), 'Z')
for res in lig.residues:
    res.chain = lig_chain
    res.name = 'UNK'
complex_struct = prot + lig
complex_struct.save('complex.pdb', overwrite=True)
"
    """

    stub:
    """
    touch complex.pdb
    """
}

workflow SETUP_MD_SYSTEM {
    take:
    ch_targets        // channel: [ val(meta), path(docked_pose), val(hit_title), path(receptor_file) ]
    is_membrane       // val: true/false
    upper_lipids      // val: "POPC:POPE:CHL1"
    upper_ratio       // val: "5:4:1"
    lower_lipids      // val: "POPC:POPE:POPS:CHL1"
    lower_ratio       // val: "4:4:1:1"
    lipid_pad         // val: 10.0
    wat_pad           // val: 15.0
    salt_conc         // val: 0.15
    water_model       // val: "opc"

    main:
    // 1. Extract files
    EXTRACT_MD_SYSTEM(ch_targets)
    
    ch_ante_in = EXTRACT_MD_SYSTEM.out.extracted.map { meta, rec_pdb, lig_mol2, charge_txt ->
        [ meta, lig_mol2, charge_txt ]
    }

    // 2. Antechamber GAFF2 / AM1-BCC
    ANTECHAMBER(ch_ante_in)

    // 3. Merge Amber Complex: join receptor.pdb with UNK_gaff2.mol2
    ch_merge_in = EXTRACT_MD_SYSTEM.out.extracted
        .map { meta, rec_pdb, lig_mol2, charge_txt -> [ meta, rec_pdb ] }
        .join(ANTECHAMBER.out.gaff_params)
        .map { meta, rec_pdb, unk_mol2, unk_frcmod -> [ meta, rec_pdb, unk_mol2 ] }

    MERGE_AMBER_COMPLEX(ch_merge_in)

    // 4. Membrane Assembly or Solvation
    if (is_membrane) {
        PACKMOL_MEMGEN(
            MERGE_AMBER_COMPLEX.out.complex_pdb,
            upper_lipids,
            upper_ratio,
            lower_lipids,
            lower_ratio,
            lipid_pad,
            wat_pad,
            salt_conc
        )
        ch_tleap_pdb = PACKMOL_MEMGEN.out.mem_complex_pdb
    } else {
        ch_tleap_pdb = MERGE_AMBER_COMPLEX.out.complex_pdb
    }

    // 5. tLEaP topology building
    ch_tleap_in = ch_tleap_pdb.join(ANTECHAMBER.out.gaff_params)
    TLEAP(ch_tleap_in, water_model, is_membrane, wat_pad)

    // 6. ParmEd conversion to GROMACS
    PARMED(TLEAP.out.amber_system)

    // 7. Declashing if membrane system
    if (is_membrane) {
        DECLASH(PARMED.out.gmx_system)
        ch_final_gro = DECLASH.out.declashed_gro
    } else {
        ch_final_gro = PARMED.out.gmx_system.map { meta, topol, gro -> [ meta, gro ] }
    }

    // 8. Index & Position Restraints
    ch_ndx_in = ch_final_gro.join(PARMED.out.gmx_system.map { meta, topol, gro -> [ meta, topol ] })
    NDX_POSRE(ch_ndx_in)

    ch_system_ready = ch_final_gro.join(NDX_POSRE.out.restraints)
        .map { meta, gro, ndx, topol, posre_files ->
            [ meta, gro, topol, ndx, posre_files ]
        }

    emit:
    system_ready = ch_system_ready
}

======================================================================
FILE: tests/cluster_smoke/run_smoke_test.sh
======================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
#
# tests/cluster_smoke/run_smoke_test.sh
# End-to-End Real Slurm Cluster Integration Smoke Test
# Dispatches 1 fast docking job across the Slurm GPU cluster,
# verifying Slurm submission, GPU worker allocation, NFS /shared staging,
# and deliverable publishing in < 30 seconds.
#
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
ROOT_DIR="$(cd "${SCRIPT_DIR}/../.." && pwd)"

echo "=========================================================="
echo " Starting End-to-End Slurm Cluster Integration Smoke Test"
echo " Framework Root: ${ROOT_DIR}"
echo "=========================================================="

SMOKE_DIR="/tmp/cluster_smoke_$(date +%s)"
mkdir -p "${SMOKE_DIR}"
trap "rm -rf ${SMOKE_DIR} /shared/work/cluster_smoke_test 2>/dev/null || true" EXIT

# Copy smoke data and configs
cp -r "${SCRIPT_DIR}/configs" "${SMOKE_DIR}/"
cp "${SCRIPT_DIR}/data/smoke_receptor.pdb" "${SMOKE_DIR}/"
cp "${SCRIPT_DIR}/data/smoke_ligand.sdf" "${SMOKE_DIR}/"

START_TIME=$(date +%s)

echo -e "\n[1/3] Launching 'sbdd dock --engine gnina' on Slurm HPC cluster..."
(
    cd "${SMOKE_DIR}"
    # sbdd uses default slurm profile and main partition
    sbdd dock --engine gnina
)

END_TIME=$(date +%s)
ELAPSED=$((END_TIME - START_TIME))

echo -e "\n[2/3] Verifying deliverables in ${SMOKE_DIR}..."
if [ -f "${SMOKE_DIR}/docking/cluster_smoke_test_docked_all.sdf" ]; then
    echo "  ✓ Found merged docking poses: docking/cluster_smoke_test_docked_all.sdf ($(wc -c < "${SMOKE_DIR}/docking/cluster_smoke_test_docked_all.sdf") bytes)"
else
    echo "  ✗ ERROR: docking/cluster_smoke_test_docked_all.sdf was not generated!" >&2
    exit 1
fi

if [ -f "${SMOKE_DIR}/reports/ranking.csv" ]; then
    echo "  ✓ Found master ranking report: reports/ranking.csv"
    cat "${SMOKE_DIR}/reports/ranking.csv"
else
    echo "  ✗ ERROR: reports/ranking.csv was not generated!" >&2
    exit 1
fi

echo -e "\n[3/3] Checking cluster scratch cleanliness..."
if [ ! -d "/shared/work/cluster_smoke_test" ]; then
    echo "  ✓ /shared/work/cluster_smoke_test was automatically cleaned upon success."
else
    echo "  (Scratch directory present - cleaning now)"
    rm -rf /shared/work/cluster_smoke_test
fi

echo -e "\n=========================================================="
echo " ✓ CLUSTER INTEGRATION SMOKE TEST PASSED IN ${ELAPSED}s!"
echo "=========================================================="

======================================================================
FILE: tests/test_workflows_stub.sh
======================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
#
# tests/test_workflows_stub.sh
# Automated Nextflow DSL2 DAG & Process Verification on Slurm Cluster in Stub Mode
# Fast verification (~1 minute) by validating multi-frame/multi-replicate DAGs with 2 ensemble models.
#
set -euo pipefail

SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
ROOT_DIR="$(cd "${SCRIPT_DIR}/.." && pwd)"
MAIN_NF="${ROOT_DIR}/main.nf"

echo "=========================================================="
echo " Starting Nextflow DSL2 Workflow Stub Tests on Slurm"
echo " Framework Root: ${ROOT_DIR}"
echo "=========================================================="

TMP_OUT="/shared/work/sbdd_stub_tests_$(date +%s)"
mkdir -p "${TMP_OUT}"
trap "rm -rf ${TMP_OUT}" EXIT

# 1. Test PREPARE workflow
echo -e "\n[1/5] Testing PREPARE workflow on Slurm cluster (-stub)..."
nextflow -log "${TMP_OUT}/nextflow_prep.log" run "${MAIN_NF}" \
    -stub \
    -work-dir "${TMP_OUT}/work_prep" \
    --entry prepare \
    --total_models 2 \
    --project_name test_stub_prep \
    --receptor "${SCRIPT_DIR}/../tests/fixtures/dummy_rec.pdb" \
    --library "${SCRIPT_DIR}/../tests/fixtures/dummy_lib.sdf" \
    --outdir "${TMP_OUT}/prep" \
    -profile slurm

# 2. Test SCREEN workflow (GNINA)
echo -e "\n[2/5] Testing SCREEN workflow (GNINA) on Slurm cluster (-stub)..."
nextflow -log "${TMP_OUT}/nextflow_screen_gnina.log" run "${MAIN_NF}" \
    -stub \
    -work-dir "${TMP_OUT}/work_screen_gnina" \
    --entry screen \
    --total_models 2 \
    --dock_engine gnina \
    --project_name test_stub_screen_gnina \
    --receptor "${SCRIPT_DIR}/../tests/fixtures/dummy_rec.pdb" \
    --library "${SCRIPT_DIR}/../tests/fixtures/dummy_lib.sdf" \
    --outdir "${TMP_OUT}/screen_gnina" \
    -profile slurm

# 3. Test SCREEN workflow (Both: Glide + GNINA Rescore + MM-GBSA 2D)
echo -e "\n[3/5] Testing SCREEN workflow (Both: Glide + GNINA + 2D MM-GBSA) on Slurm (-stub)..."
nextflow -log "${TMP_OUT}/nextflow_screen_both.log" run "${MAIN_NF}" \
    -stub \
    -work-dir "${TMP_OUT}/work_screen_both" \
    --entry screen \
    --total_models 2 \
    --dock_engine both \
    --project_name test_stub_screen_both \
    --receptor "${SCRIPT_DIR}/../tests/fixtures/dummy_rec.pdb" \
    --library "${SCRIPT_DIR}/../tests/fixtures/dummy_lib.sdf" \
    --outdir "${TMP_OUT}/screen_both" \
    -profile slurm

# 4. Test DYNAMICS workflow
echo -e "\n[4/5] Testing DYNAMICS workflow on Slurm cluster (-stub)..."
nextflow -log "${TMP_OUT}/nextflow_dyn.log" run "${MAIN_NF}" \
    -stub \
    -work-dir "${TMP_OUT}/work_dyn" \
    --entry dynamics \
    --md_replicates 1 \
    --project_name test_stub_dyn \
    --receptor "${SCRIPT_DIR}/../tests/fixtures/dummy_rec.pdb" \
    --docked_poses "${SCRIPT_DIR}/../tests/fixtures/dummy_lib.sdf" \
    --outdir "${TMP_OUT}/dynamics" \
    -profile slurm

# 5. Test SBDD master end-to-end workflow
echo -e "\n[5/5] Testing Master SBDD workflow on Slurm cluster (-stub)..."
nextflow -log "${TMP_OUT}/nextflow_all.log" run "${MAIN_NF}" \
    -stub \
    -work-dir "${TMP_OUT}/work_all" \
    --entry all \
    --total_models 2 \
    --md_replicates 1 \
    --project_name test_stub_all \
    --receptor "${SCRIPT_DIR}/../tests/fixtures/dummy_rec.pdb" \
    --library "${SCRIPT_DIR}/../tests/fixtures/dummy_lib.sdf" \
    --outdir "${TMP_OUT}/all" \
    -profile slurm

echo -e "\n=========================================================="
echo " ✓ ALL 5 NEXTFLOW WORKFLOW DAGs PASSED ON SLURM IN STUB MODE!"
echo "=========================================================="

======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/test_cli.py
======================================================================
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from cli.core.config_loader import load_project_configs, build_nextflow_args

def test_config_loader(tmp_path):
    # Test loading and building Nextflow args from project dir
    configs_dir = tmp_path / "configs"
    configs_dir.mkdir()

    (configs_dir / "project.yaml").write_text("project:\n  name: TestProj\n")
    (configs_dir / "gnina.yaml").write_text("gnina:\n  exhaustiveness: 8\n  cnn_scoring: rescore\n  batch_size: 10\n")
    (configs_dir / "membrane.yaml").write_text("membrane:\n  enabled: true\n  type: MOM\n")

    cfg = load_project_configs(tmp_path)
    assert cfg["project"]["name"] == "TestProj"
    assert cfg["gnina"]["exhaustiveness"] == 8
    assert cfg["membrane"]["enabled"] is True

    nf_args = build_nextflow_args(cfg, tmp_path)
    assert "--project_name" in nf_args
    assert "TestProj" in nf_args
    assert "--exhaustiveness" in nf_args
    assert "8" in nf_args
    assert "--membrane" in nf_args
    assert "true" in nf_args

def test_sbdd_init(tmp_path):
    import shutil
    target = tmp_path / "new_project"
    sbdd_bin = shutil.which("sbdd") or str(Path(sys.prefix) / "bin" / "sbdd")
    cmd = [sbdd_bin, "init", str(target)]
    res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert res.returncode == 0, f"sbdd init failed: {res.stderr}"

    assert (target / "configs").exists()
    assert (target / "configs" / "project.yaml").exists()
    assert (target / "configs" / "gnina.yaml").exists()
    assert (target / "configs" / "receptor.yaml").exists()
    assert (target / "configs" / "membrane.yaml").exists()


======================================================================
FILE: tests/unit/test_complex_assembly.py
======================================================================
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

SAMPLE_PDB_RAW = """ATOM      1  N   ALA A   1       0.000   0.000   0.000  1.00  0.00           N
ATOM      2  CA  ALA A   1       1.458   0.000   0.000  1.00  0.00           C
ATOM      3  C   ALA A   1       2.009   1.362   0.000  1.00  0.00           C
ATOM      4  O   ALA A   1       1.272   2.348   0.000  1.00  0.00           O
ATOM      5  H1  ALA A   1      -0.500   0.000   0.800  1.00  0.00           H
ATOM      6  N   NMA A   2       3.200   1.362   0.000  1.00  0.00           N
ATOM      7  H   NMA A   2       3.700   0.500   0.000  1.00  0.00           H
ATOM      8  CH3 NMA A   2       3.900   2.500   0.000  1.00  0.00           C
TER
END
"""

SAMPLE_MOL2 = """@<TRIPOS>MOLECULE
UNK
 2 1 0 0 0
SMALL
NO_CHARGES

@<TRIPOS>ATOM
      1 C1          5.0000    5.0000    5.0000 c3        1 UNK       0.0000
      2 C2          6.2000    5.0000    5.0000 c3        1 UNK       0.0000
@<TRIPOS>BOND
     1     1     2 1
"""

def test_amber_fix_and_merge(tmp_path):
    raw_pdb = tmp_path / "raw.pdb"
    raw_pdb.write_text(SAMPLE_PDB_RAW)

    fixed_pdb = tmp_path / "fixed.pdb"
    lig_mol2 = tmp_path / "lig.mol2"
    lig_mol2.write_text(SAMPLE_MOL2)
    complex_pdb = tmp_path / "complex.pdb"

    amber_fix_script = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "bin" / "amber_fix_pdb.py"
    merge_script = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "bin" / "merge_complex.py"

    # 1. amber_fix_pdb
    cmd_fix = [sys.executable, str(amber_fix_script), str(raw_pdb), str(fixed_pdb)]
    res_fix = subprocess.run(cmd_fix, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert res_fix.returncode == 0, f"amber_fix failed: {res_fix.stderr}"
    assert fixed_pdb.exists()

    fixed_text = fixed_pdb.read_text()
    # Check H1 renamed to H, NMA renamed to NME
    assert "NME" in fixed_text

    # 2. merge_complex
    cmd_merge = [sys.executable, str(merge_script), str(fixed_pdb), str(lig_mol2), str(complex_pdb)]
    res_merge = subprocess.run(cmd_merge, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert res_merge.returncode == 0, f"merge_complex failed: {res_merge.stderr}"
    assert complex_pdb.exists()

    complex_text = complex_pdb.read_text()
    assert "UNK" in complex_text
    assert "ALA" in complex_text

FILE: tests/unit/test_declash.py
======================================================================
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

SAMPLE_GRO = """Declash Test System
    4
    1MOL1     C1    1   1.000   1.000   1.000
    1MOL1     C2    2   1.100   1.000   1.000
    2MOL2     C1    3   1.020   1.000   1.000
    2MOL2     C2    4   1.120   1.000   1.000
   5.00000   5.00000   5.00000
"""

def make_gro_line(resid, resname, atomname, atomnr, x, y, z):
    return f"{resid:5d}{resname[:5]:<5}{atomname[:5]:>5}{atomnr:5d}{x:8.3f}{y:8.3f}{z:8.3f}\n"

SAMPLE_TOP = """[ moleculetype ]
; Name   nrexcl
MOL1     3

[ atoms ]
; nr  type  resnr resid atom cgnr charge mass
1     c3    1     MOL1  C1   1    0      12.011
2     c3    1     MOL1  C2   1    0      12.011

[ moleculetype ]
; Name   nrexcl
MOL2     3

[ atoms ]
; nr  type  resnr resid atom cgnr charge mass
1     c3    2     MOL2  C1   1    0      12.011
2     c3    2     MOL2  C2   1    0      12.011

[ system ]
Test

[ molecules ]
MOL1  1
MOL2  1
"""

def test_declash_gmx(tmp_path):
    gro_content = "Declash Test System\n    4\n"
    gro_content += make_gro_line(1, "MOL1", "C1", 1, 1.000, 1.000, 1.000)
    gro_content += make_gro_line(1, "MOL1", "C2", 2, 1.100, 1.000, 1.000)
    gro_content += make_gro_line(2, "MOL2", "C1", 3, 1.020, 1.000, 1.000)
    gro_content += make_gro_line(2, "MOL2", "C2", 4, 1.120, 1.000, 1.000)
    gro_content += "   5.00000   5.00000   5.00000\n"

    gro_file = tmp_path / "clashed.gro"
    gro_file.write_text(gro_content)

    top_file = tmp_path / "topol.top"
    top_file.write_text(SAMPLE_TOP)

    out_gro = tmp_path / "declashed.gro"
    report_txt = tmp_path / "report.txt"

    declash_script = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "bin" / "declash_gmx.py"

    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        str(declash_script),
        "-f", str(gro_file),
        "-p", str(top_file),
        "-o", str(out_gro),
        "--cutoff", "0.15",
        "--niter", "50",
        "--report", str(report_txt)
    ]
    res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert res.returncode == 0, f"declash failed: {res.stderr}\n{res.stdout}"

    assert out_gro.exists()
    assert report_txt.exists()

    # Read declashed gro and verify coordinates moved apart
    lines = out_gro.read_text().splitlines()
    assert len(lines) == 7
    # Original distance was 0.02 nm between atom 1 and 3 (1.000 vs 1.020).
    # After declash with cutoff 0.15 nm, distance should increase.
    x1 = float(lines[2][20:28])
    x3 = float(lines[4][20:28])
    assert abs(x3 - x1) > 0.03

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

SAMPLE_PDB = """ATOM      1  N   ALA A   1       0.000   0.000   0.000  1.00  0.00           N
ATOM      2  CA  ALA A   1       1.458   0.000   0.000  1.00  0.00           C
ATOM      3  C   ALA A   1       2.009   1.362   0.000  1.00  0.00           C
ATOM      4  O   ALA A   1       1.272   2.348   0.000  1.00  0.00           O
ATOM      5  CB  ALA A   1       1.966  -0.764  -1.217  1.00  0.00           C
TER
END
"""

def test_extract_md_files_rdkit(sample_sdf, tmp_path):
    rec_pdb = tmp_path / "receptor.pdb"
    rec_pdb.write_text(SAMPLE_PDB)

    outdir = tmp_path / "extracted"
    extract_script = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "bin" / "extract_md_files.py"

    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        str(extract_script),
        "--lig_file", str(sample_sdf),
        "--lig_title", "MOL1",
        "--rec_file", str(rec_pdb),
        "--out_dir", str(outdir)
    ]
    res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert res.returncode == 0, f"extract_md_files failed: {res.stderr}\n{res.stdout}"

    assert (outdir / "receptor.pdb").exists()
    assert (outdir / "lig_charge.txt").exists()
    assert (outdir / "ligand.mol2").exists() or (outdir / "ligand.sdf").exists()

import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

SAMPLE_PDB_COMPLEX = """ATOM      1  N   ALA A   1       0.000   0.000   0.000  1.00  0.00           N
ATOM      2  CA  ALA A   1       1.458   0.000   0.000  1.00  0.00           C
ATOM      3  C   ALA A   1       2.009   1.362   0.000  1.00  0.00           C
ATOM      4  O   ALA A   1       1.272   2.348   0.000  1.00  0.00           O
ATOM      5  CB  ALA A   1       1.966  -0.764  -1.217  1.00  0.00           C
TER
HETATM    6  C1  UNK Z   1       3.000   2.000   0.000  1.00  0.00           C
HETATM    7  C2  UNK Z   1       4.200   2.000   0.000  1.00  0.00           C
TER
END
"""

def test_md_metrics_calculation(tmp_path):
    pdb_file = tmp_path / "complex.pdb"
    pdb_file.write_text(SAMPLE_PDB_COMPLEX)

    outdir = tmp_path / "metrics"
    metrics_script = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "bin" / "md_metrics.py"

    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        str(metrics_script),
        "--pdb", str(pdb_file),
        "--xtc", str(pdb_file),
        "--outdir", str(outdir)
    ]
    res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert res.returncode == 0, f"md_metrics failed: {res.stderr}\n{res.stdout}"

    assert (outdir / "rmsd.csv").exists()
    assert (outdir / "rmsf.csv").exists()
    assert (outdir / "contacts.csv").exists()
    assert (outdir / "md_metrics_summary.json").exists()

    with open(outdir / "md_metrics_summary.json") as f:
        summary = json.load(f)

    assert "Prot_BB_RMSD_Mean_A" in summary
    assert "Lig_Heavy_RMSD_Mean_A" in summary

import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def test_parse_immers(sample_datapar1, tmp_path):
    out_json = tmp_path / "membrane.json"
    out_txt = tmp_path / "membrane.txt"
    parse_script = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "bin" / "parse_immers.py"

    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        str(parse_script),
        "--datapar", str(sample_datapar1),
        "--out_json", str(out_json),
        "--out_txt", str(out_txt)
    ]
    res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert res.returncode == 0, f"parse_immers failed: {res.stderr}"

    assert out_json.exists()
    assert out_txt.exists()

    with open(out_json) as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    assert data["thickness"] == 20.7
    assert data["tilt"] == 28.0
    assert data["dg"] == -20.2
    assert out_txt.read_text().strip() == "20.7"

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def test_select_hits(tmp_path):
    csv_file = tmp_path / "ranking.csv"
    csv_file.write_text("Rank,Title,GlideScore,CNNaffinity\n1,PPZ_1H_Ph_BnMe,-8.5,6.2\n2,PPZ_1H_Ph_Pr2,-7.8,5.9\n")
    out_file = tmp_path / "hits.txt"

    script = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "bin" / "select_hits.py"
    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        str(script),
        "--ranking_csv", str(csv_file),
        "--top_n", "1",
        "--include_native", "true",
        "--out", str(out_file)
    ]
    res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert res.returncode == 0
    lines = [l.strip() for l in out_file.read_text().splitlines() if l.strip()]
    assert lines == ["PPZ_1H_Ph_BnMe", "native"]

import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

def test_split_sdf_basic(sample_sdf, tmp_path):
    outdir = tmp_path / "chunks"
    split_script = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "bin" / "split_sdf.py"

    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        str(split_script),
        "--input", str(sample_sdf),
        "--chunk_size", "2",
        "--outdir", str(outdir)
    ]
    res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert res.returncode == 0, f"split_sdf failed: {res.stderr}"

    chunks = sorted(list(outdir.glob("chunk_*.sdf")))
    assert len(chunks) == 2, f"Expected 2 chunks for 3 molecules with batch_size=2, got {len(chunks)}"

    # Check chunk 0 has 2 molecules
    content_0 = chunks[0].read_text()
    assert content_0.count("$$$$") == 2
    assert "MOL1" in content_0
    assert "MOL2" in content_0

    # Check chunk 1 has 1 molecule
    content_1 = chunks[1].read_text()
    assert content_1.count("$$$$") == 1
    assert "MOL3" in content_1

import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
import pandas as pd

def test_summarize_results_gnina(sample_sdf, tmp_path):
    docking_dir = tmp_path / "docking"
    docking_dir.mkdir()
    # Put sample_sdf into docking_dir
    (docking_dir / "docked.sdf").write_text(sample_sdf.read_text())

    reports_dir = tmp_path / "reports"
    summarize_script = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent / "bin" / "summarize_results.py"

    cmd = [
        sys.executable,
        str(summarize_script),
        "--docking_dir", str(docking_dir),
        "--outdir", str(reports_dir)
    ]
    res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert res.returncode == 0, f"summarize_results failed: {res.stderr}"

    ranking_csv = reports_dir / "ranking.csv"
    summary_json = reports_dir / "summary.json"

    assert ranking_csv.exists()
    assert summary_json.exists()

    df = pd.read_csv(ranking_csv)
    assert len(df) == 3
    # Sorted by CNNaffinity descending: MOL3 (8.4) > MOL1 (7.2) > MOL2 (6.1)
    assert df.iloc[0]["Title"] == "MOL3"
    assert df.iloc[0]["CNNaffinity"] == 8.4
    assert df.iloc[1]["Title"] == "MOL1"
    assert df.iloc[2]["Title"] == "MOL2"

/*
 * workflows/dynamics.nf
 * Standalone MD system setup, chunked simulation, and trajectory analysis
 */

include { STAGE_INPUTS    } from '../modules/common/stage_inputs'
include { SETUP_MD_SYSTEM } from '../subworkflows/setup_md_system'
include { RUN_CHUNKED_MD  } from '../subworkflows/run_chunked_md'
include { ANALYZE_MD_TRAJ } from '../subworkflows/analyze_md_traj'

workflow DYNAMICS_WF {
    // Automatic resolution of inputs
    def prepared_pdb = file("${params.outdir}/receptor/${params.project_name}_prepared.pdb")
    def docked_sdf   = file("${params.outdir}/docking/${params.project_name}_docked_all.sdf")
    def ref_sdf      = file("${params.outdir}/receptor/${params.project_name}_ref_native.sdf")

    def rec_file     = prepared_pdb.exists() ? prepared_pdb : (params.receptor ? file(params.receptor) : file('NO_FILE'))
    def dock_file    = docked_sdf.exists() ? docked_sdf : (params.docked_poses ? file(params.docked_poses) : ref_sdf)

    STAGE_INPUTS(rec_file, dock_file, ref_sdf)

    // Parse target hit list (e.g. "native,PPZ_1H_Ph_BnMe" or single hit)
    def hits = params.md_hits ? params.md_hits.split(",").collect { it.trim() } : ["native"]

    ch_targets = Channel.fromList(hits)
        .combine(STAGE_INPUTS.out.staged_library)
        .combine(STAGE_INPUTS.out.staged_receptor)
        .map { hit_name, staged_dock, staged_rec ->
            def target_id = hit_name.replaceAll("[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]", "_")
            [ [id: target_id], staged_dock, hit_name, staged_rec ]
        }

    // 1. Build systems
    SETUP_MD_SYSTEM(
        ch_targets,
        params.membrane ?: false,
        params.upper_lipids ?: "POPC:POPE:CHL1",
        params.upper_ratio ?: "5:4:1",
        params.lower_lipids ?: "POPC:POPE:POPS:CHL1",
        params.lower_ratio ?: "4:4:1:1",
        10.0,
        15.0,
        params.md_salt_conc ?: 0.15,
        "opc"
    )

    // 2. Equilibration & chunked production MD on Slurm GPU
    RUN_CHUNKED_MD(
        SETUP_MD_SYSTEM.out.system_ready,
        params.membrane ?: false,
        params.md_temperature ?: 310.15,
        params.md_total_ns ?: 60,
        params.md_chunk_ns ?: 10,
        params.md_replicates ? (params.md_replicates as int) : 3
    )

    // 3. Trajectory analysis & MM-PBSA
    ANALYZE_MD_TRAJ(
        RUN_CHUNKED_MD.out.clean_trajectory,
        params.mmpbsa_cores ?: 16,
        params.mmpbsa_start_ns ?: 20.0,
        params.mmpbsa_step_frames ?: 40,
        params.md_temperature ?: 310.15,
        true
    )
}

/*
 * workflows/prepare.nf
 * Standalone receptor and ligand preparation workflow with ensemble support
 */

include { STAGE_INPUTS     } from '../modules/common/stage_inputs'
include { PREPARE_RECEPTOR } from '../subworkflows/prepare_receptor'
include { PREPARE_LIGANDS  } from '../subworkflows/prepare_ligands'

workflow PREPARE_WF {
    def rec_file = params.receptor ? file(params.receptor) : file('NO_FILE')
    def lib_file = params.library ? file(params.library) : file('NO_FILE')

    // 1. Stage input files from local home to /shared scratch
    STAGE_INPUTS(rec_file, lib_file, file('NO_FILE'))

    // 2. Determine models channel:
    // If params.model_index > 0: run single specified model.
    // If params.model_index == 0 or null: run all ensemble models.
    def requested_model = params.model_index ? (params.model_index as int) : 0
    def num_models = requested_model > 0 ? requested_model : (params.total_models ? (params.total_models as int) : 20)
    def model_list = requested_model > 0 ? [ requested_model ] : (1..num_models).toList()

    ch_rec = STAGE_INPUTS.out.staged_receptor
        .combine(Channel.fromList(model_list))
        .map { staged_rec, idx ->
            def meta = [
                id: "${params.project_name}_f${idx}",
                frame: idx,
                project: params.project_name
            ]
            [ meta, staged_rec, idx ]
        }

    PREPARE_RECEPTOR(
        ch_rec,
        params.native_ligand,
        params.keep_cofactors ?: "",
        params.membrane ?: false,
        params.membrane_type ?: "MOM",
        params.membrane_topo ?: "in",
        params.membrane_chains ?: "A",
        7.4,
        1.0,
        0.3,
        true
    )

    // 3. Prepare Ligands (if provided)
    if (params.library) {
        ch_lib = STAGE_INPUTS.out.staged_library
            .map { staged_lib -> [ [id: params.project_name, project: params.project_name], staged_lib ] }

        PREPARE_LIGANDS(
            ch_lib,
            params.dock_engine ?: "gnina",
            params.batch_size ?: 15,
            7.4,
            2.0,
            16,
            32
        )
    }
}

======================================================================
FILE: workflows/sbdd.nf
======================================================================
/*
 * workflows/sbdd.nf
 * Complete end-to-end SBDD pipeline with multi-engine docking, rescoring,
 * Screening Funnel Triage (Physics + AI), Prime MM-GBSA, automated hit selection, and chunked GROMACS MD.
 */

include { STAGE_INPUTS      } from '../modules/common/stage_inputs'
include { PREPARE_RECEPTOR  } from '../subworkflows/prepare_receptor'
include { PREPARE_LIGANDS   } from '../subworkflows/prepare_ligands'
include { DOCK_LIBRARY      } from '../subworkflows/dock_library'
include { SETUP_MD_SYSTEM   } from '../subworkflows/setup_md_system'
include { RUN_CHUNKED_MD    } from '../subworkflows/run_chunked_md'
include { ANALYZE_MD_TRAJ   } from '../subworkflows/analyze_md_traj'
include { SUMMARIZE_DOCKING } from '../modules/reporting/summarize'
include { SELECT_HITS       } from '../modules/reporting/select_hits'

workflow SBDD_WF {
    def rec_file = params.receptor ? file(params.receptor) : file('NO_FILE')
    def lib_file = params.library ? file(params.library) : file('NO_FILE')

    STAGE_INPUTS(rec_file, lib_file, file('NO_FILE'))

    // 1. Prepare Receptor across ensemble models
    def requested_model = params.model_index ? (params.model_index as int) : 0
    def num_models = requested_model > 0 ? requested_model : (params.total_models ? (params.total_models as int) : 20)
    def model_list = requested_model > 0 ? [ requested_model ] : (1..num_models).toList()

    ch_rec = STAGE_INPUTS.out.staged_receptor
        .combine(Channel.fromList(model_list))
        .map { staged_rec, idx ->
            def meta = [
                id: "${params.project_name}_f${idx}",
                frame: idx,
                project: params.project_name
            ]
            [ meta, staged_rec, idx ]
        }

    PREPARE_RECEPTOR(
        ch_rec,
        params.native_ligand,
        params.keep_cofactors ?: "",
        params.membrane ?: false,
        params.membrane_type ?: "MOM",
        params.membrane_topo ?: "in",
        params.membrane_chains ?: "A",
        7.4,
        1.0,
        0.3,
        true
    )

    // 2. Prepare Ligands & Docking
    if (params.library) {
        ch_lib = STAGE_INPUTS.out.staged_library
            .map { staged_lib -> [ [id: params.project_name, project: params.project_name], staged_lib ] }

        PREPARE_LIGANDS(
            ch_lib,
            params.dock_engine ?: "gnina",
            params.batch_size ?: 15,
            7.4,
            2.0,
            16,
            32
        )

        // 3. Multi-Engine Docking, Triage, and MM-GBSA
        DOCK_LIBRARY(
            PREPARE_RECEPTOR.out.prepared_pdb,
            PREPARE_RECEPTOR.out.ref_native_sdf,
            PREPARE_LIGANDS.out.chunks,
            PREPARE_RECEPTOR.out.prepared_mae,
            PREPARE_RECEPTOR.out.ref_native_mae,
            PREPARE_LIGANDS.out.prepared_ligands,
            params.dock_engine ?: "gnina",
            params.exhaustiveness ?: 16,
            params.cnn_scoring ?: "rescore",
            params.num_modes ?: 3,
            params.autobox_add ?: 4.0,
            params.top_k_frames ?: 3,
            params.percentile_cutoff ?: 0.50,
            params.membrane ?: false
        )

        // 4. Summarize results into master ranking table and cross-frame matrix
        SUMMARIZE_DOCKING(
            params.project_name,
            DOCK_LIBRARY.out.summary_files
        )

        // 5. Automated Hit Selection for Molecular Dynamics
        ch_ranking_tuple = SUMMARIZE_DOCKING.out.ranking_csv
            .map { csv -> [ [id: params.project_name, project: params.project_name], csv ] }

        SELECT_HITS(
            ch_ranking_tuple,
            params.top_n_hits ?: 1,
            true
        )

        // 6. Downstream MD simulation
        if (params.run_md) {
            ch_hits = SELECT_HITS.out.hits_file
                .flatMap { meta, file -> file.readLines() }
                .map { line -> line.trim() }
                .filter { it.length() > 0 }
                .map { line ->
                    def parts = line.split(',')
                    def title = parts[0].trim()
                    def fid = parts.size() > 1 ? (parts[1].trim() as int) : 1
                    [ fid, title ]
                }

            ch_docked_by_frame = DOCK_LIBRARY.out.docked_poses
                .map { meta, pose -> [ (meta.frame as int), pose ] }

            ch_rec_by_frame = PREPARE_RECEPTOR.out.prepared_pdb
                .map { meta, pdb -> [ (meta.frame as int), pdb ] }

            ch_frame_data = ch_docked_by_frame.join(ch_rec_by_frame, by: 0)

            ch_md_targets = ch_hits.join(ch_frame_data, by: 0)
                .map { fid, hit_name, pose_file, rec_pdb ->
                    def target_id = hit_name.replaceAll("[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]", "_")
                    [ [id: target_id, frame: fid], pose_file, hit_name, rec_pdb ]
                }

            SETUP_MD_SYSTEM(
                ch_md_targets,
                params.membrane ?: false,
                params.upper_lipids ?: "POPC:POPE:CHL1",
                params.upper_ratio ?: "5:4:1",
                params.lower_lipids ?: "POPC:POPE:POPS:CHL1",
                params.lower_ratio ?: "4:4:1:1",
                10.0,
                15.0,
                params.md_salt_conc ?: 0.15,
                "opc"
            )

            RUN_CHUNKED_MD(
                SETUP_MD_SYSTEM.out.system_ready,
                params.membrane ?: false,
                params.md_temperature ?: 310.15,
                params.md_total_ns ?: 60,
                params.md_chunk_ns ?: 10,
                params.md_replicates ? (params.md_replicates as int) : 3
            )

            ANALYZE_MD_TRAJ(
                RUN_CHUNKED_MD.out.clean_trajectory,
                params.mmpbsa_cores ?: 16,
                params.mmpbsa_start_ns ?: 20.0,
                params.mmpbsa_step_frames ?: 40,
                params.md_temperature ?: 310.15,
                true
            )
        }
    }
}

======================================================================

/*
 * workflows/screen.nf
 * Standalone distributed docking and screening workflow with ensemble support
 */

include { STAGE_INPUTS      } from "../modules/common/stage_inputs"
include { PREPARE_RECEPTOR  } from "../subworkflows/prepare_receptor"
include { PREPARE_LIGANDS   } from "../subworkflows/prepare_ligands"
include { DOCK_LIBRARY      } from "../subworkflows/dock_library"
include { SUMMARIZE_DOCKING } from "../modules/reporting/summarize"

workflow SCREEN_WF {
    def rec_file = params.receptor ? file(params.receptor) : file('NO_FILE')
    def lib_file = params.library ? file(params.library) : file('NO_FILE')

    // Discover or generate all ensemble frames
    def requested_model = params.model_index ? (params.model_index as int) : 0
    def num_models = requested_model > 0 ? requested_model : (params.total_models ? (params.total_models as int) : 20)
    def model_list = requested_model > 0 ? [ requested_model ] : (1..num_models).toList()

    def prep_check = file("${params.outdir}/receptor/${params.project_name}_f1_prepared.pdb")

    if (prep_check.exists()) {
        ch_rec_pdb = Channel.fromList(model_list).map { idx ->
            def meta = [id: "${params.project_name}_f${idx}", frame: idx, project: params.project_name]
            [ meta, file("${params.outdir}/receptor/${params.project_name}_f${idx}_prepared.pdb") ]
        }
        ch_ref_sdf = Channel.fromList(model_list).map { idx ->
            def meta = [id: "${params.project_name}_f${idx}", frame: idx, project: params.project_name]
            [ meta, file("${params.outdir}/receptor/${params.project_name}_f${idx}_ref_native.sdf") ]
        }
        ch_rec_mae = Channel.fromList(model_list).map { idx ->
            def meta = [id: "${params.project_name}_f${idx}", frame: idx, project: params.project_name]
            [ meta, file("${params.outdir}/receptor/${params.project_name}_f${idx}_prepared.mae") ]
        }
        ch_ref_mae = Channel.fromList(model_list).map { idx ->
            def meta = [id: "${params.project_name}_f${idx}", frame: idx, project: params.project_name]
            [ meta, file("${params.outdir}/receptor/${params.project_name}_f${idx}_ref_native.mae") ]
        }

        STAGE_INPUTS(rec_file, lib_file, file('NO_FILE'))
    } else {
        STAGE_INPUTS(rec_file, lib_file, file('NO_FILE'))

        ch_rec = STAGE_INPUTS.out.staged_receptor
            .combine(Channel.fromList(model_list))
            .map { staged_rec, idx ->
                def meta = [
                    id: "${params.project_name}_f${idx}",
                    frame: idx,
                    project: params.project_name
                ]
                [ meta, staged_rec, idx ]
            }

        PREPARE_RECEPTOR(
            ch_rec,
            params.native_ligand,
            params.keep_cofactors ?: "",
            params.membrane ?: false,
            params.membrane_type ?: "MOM",
            params.membrane_topo ?: "in",
            params.membrane_chains ?: "A",
            7.4,
            1.0,
            0.3,
            true
        )

        ch_rec_pdb = PREPARE_RECEPTOR.out.prepared_pdb
        ch_ref_sdf = PREPARE_RECEPTOR.out.ref_native_sdf
        ch_rec_mae = PREPARE_RECEPTOR.out.prepared_mae
        ch_ref_mae = PREPARE_RECEPTOR.out.ref_native_mae
    }

    // Prepare ligands (LigPrep and/or SDF chunks)
    ch_lib = STAGE_INPUTS.out.staged_library
        .map { staged_lib -> [ [id: params.project_name, project: params.project_name], staged_lib ] }

    PREPARE_LIGANDS(
        ch_lib,
        params.dock_engine ?: "gnina",
        params.batch_size ?: 15,
        7.4,
        2.0,
        16,
        32
    )

    // Parallel docking (Glide, GNINA, or Both)
    DOCK_LIBRARY(
        ch_rec_pdb,
        ch_ref_sdf,
        PREPARE_LIGANDS.out.chunks,
        ch_rec_mae,
        ch_ref_mae,
        PREPARE_LIGANDS.out.prepared_ligands,
        params.dock_engine ?: "gnina",
        params.exhaustiveness ?: 16,
        params.cnn_scoring ?: "rescore",
        params.num_modes ?: 3,
        params.autobox_add ?: 4.0,
        params.top_k_frames ?: 3,
        params.percentile_cutoff ?: 0.50,
        params.membrane ?: false
    )

    // Summarize raw poses into consensus ranking CSV tables
    SUMMARIZE_DOCKING(
        params.project_name,
        DOCK_LIBRARY.out.summary_files
    )
}
